## [M] src/app/app.routes.ts

```ts
import { Routes } from '@angular/router';
import { authGuard } from '@guards/auth.guard';
import { roleGuard } from '@guards/role.guard';
import { unsavedChangesGuard } from '@guards/unsaved-changes.guard';
import { EmailStore } from '@store/email/email.store';
import { WellDocsStore } from '@store/well-docs/well-docs.store';
import { PresentationStore } from './features/presentation/store/presentation.store';
import { ActiveWwellStore } from './features/active-wwell/store/active-wwell.store';
import { MorningReportStore } from './features/morning-report/store/morning-report.store';
import { WwellPlanningStore } from './features/wwell-planning/store/wwell-planning.store';
import { WwellIrStore } from './features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/store/wwell-ir.store';

export const routes: Routes = [
  {
    path: '',
    redirectTo: 'login',
    pathMatch: 'full',
  },
  {
    path: 'login',
    loadComponent: () =>
      import('./features/auth/login.component').then(
        (m) => m.LoginComponent,
      ),
  },
  {
    path: 'main',
    loadComponent: () =>
      import('./layout/layout.component').then(
        (m) => m.LayoutComponent,
      ),
    canActivate: [authGuard],
    canActivateChild: [authGuard],
    children: [
      {
        path: '',
        redirectTo: 'home',
        pathMatch: 'full',
      },
      {
        path: 'home',
        loadComponent: () =>
          import('./features/home/home.component').then(
            (m) => m.HomeComponent,
          ),
      },
      // PLANNING — open to all roles for now, mirrors left-sidenav.component.ts (no RBAC gating yet)
      {
        path: 'requests',
        loadComponent: () =>
          import('./features/wwell-planning/wwell-requests/wwell-requests.component').then(
            (m) => m.WwellRequestsComponent,
          ),
        providers: [WwellPlanningStore],
      },
      {
        path: 'wlda-approvals',
        loadComponent: () =>
          import('./features/wwell-planning/wwell-wlda-approvals/wwell-wlda-approvals.component').then(
            (m) => m.WwellWldaApprovalsComponent,
          ),
        providers: [WwellPlanningStore],
      },
      {
        path: 'land-affairs-approvals',
        loadComponent: () =>
          import('./features/wwell-planning/wwell-land-affairs-approvals/wwell-land-affairs-approvals.component').then(
            (m) => m.WwellLandAffairsApprovalsComponent,
          ),
        providers: [WwellPlanningStore],
      },
      {
        // New/edit no longer route anywhere — WwellIrComponent creates a draft in place and
        // authors it inline (WwellIrEditorComponent) in the same master-detail view. The
        // read-only "View" stepper is the same story: a display mode toggled in place on the
        // detail panel (see WwellIrComponent's `detailMode`), not a separate route — the master
        // list stays visible and clickable the whole time.
        path: 'integrated-requirements',
        providers: [WwellIrStore],
        canDeactivate: [unsavedChangesGuard],
        loadComponent: () =>
          import('./features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir.component').then(
            (m) => m.WwellIrComponent,
          ),
      },
      {
        path: 'well-profile',
        loadComponent: () => import('./features/well-profile/well-profile.component').then(m => m.WellProfileComponent),
      },
      {
        path: 'presentations',
        data: { routeId: 'presentations' },
        canActivate: [roleGuard],
        loadComponent: () =>
          import('./features/presentation/presentation.component').then(
            (m) => m.PresentationComponent,
          ),
        providers: [PresentationStore, WellDocsStore],
      },
      {
        path: 'active-wwell',
        data: { routeId: 'active-wwell' },
        canActivate: [roleGuard],
        loadComponent: () =>
          import('./features/active-wwell/active-wwell-view/active-wwell-view.component').then(
            (m) => m.ActiveWwellViewComponent,
          ),
        providers: [ActiveWwellStore],
      },
      {
        path: 'morning-report',
        data: { routeId: 'morning-report' },
        canActivate: [roleGuard],
        loadComponent: () =>
          import('./features/morning-report/morning-report.component').then(
            (m) => m.MorningReportComponent,
          ),
        providers: [EmailStore, MorningReportStore],
      },
      {
        path: 'water-wells-overview',
        data: { routeId: 'presentations' },
        canActivate: [roleGuard],
        loadComponent: () =>
          import('./features/water-wells-overview/water-wells-overview.component').then(
            (m) => m.WaterWellsOverviewComponent,
          ),
        providers: [MorningReportStore],
      },
    ],
  },
  { path: '**', redirectTo: 'login' },
];
```

## [M] src/app/core/models/well-design/wellbore-diagram.model.ts

```ts
import { ICasingIR } from 'src/app/shared/models/wwell/casing-ir.model';
import { IDrlgCsg } from 'src/app/shared/models/wwell/drlg-csg.model';
import { ITopsIR } from 'src/app/shared/models/wwell/tops-ir.model';
import { IHydrogeologyIR } from 'src/app/shared/models/wwell/hydrogeology-ir.model';
import { IPreWellData } from 'src/app/shared/models/wwell/pre-well-data.model';
import { IRigActivity } from 'src/app/shared/models/wwell/rig-activity.model';
import { IWellDesign } from 'src/app/shared/models/wwell/well-design.model';
export interface MudCircPoint {
  readonly depth: number;  // parsed from wPrsntDpth
  readonly pct: number;    // parsed from wMudCircPc
}

export interface WellboreDiagramData {
  readonly wellName: string;
  readonly totalDepth: number;
  readonly displayDepth: number;
  readonly casings: ICasingIR[];
  readonly drlgCasings: IDrlgCsg[];
  readonly geologicTops: ITopsIR[];
  readonly hydrogeology: IHydrogeologyIR | null;
  readonly prewap: IPreWellData | null;
  readonly rigActivity: IRigActivity | null;
  readonly currentDepth: number;
  readonly targetDepth: number;
  readonly mudCirculation: MudCircPoint[];
  readonly wellDesign: IWellDesign | null;
}

export interface DiagramLayout {
  readonly svgHeight: number;
  readonly marginTop: number;
  readonly drawingHeight: number;
  readonly depthScaleWidth: number;
  readonly depthAxisX: number;
  readonly wellboreViewWidth: number;
  readonly geoLineX: number;
  readonly depthArrowX: number; // Controls the depth arrow's horizontal position
  readonly casingCenterX: number;
  readonly baseHalfWidth: number;
  readonly halfWidthIncrement: number;
  readonly shoeCurveOffset: number;
  // ── Completion element half-widths (x extents from center) ───────────────
  readonly openHoleHwMargin: number;   // open hole = innerHW + this
  readonly linerScreenInset: number;   // liner screen = innerHW - this
  // ── Gravel pack fill control ────────────────────────────────────────────
  readonly gpScreenGap: number;        // px gap between screen outer wall and GP inner fill edge
  readonly gpFillRatio: number;        // 0.0–1.0: fraction of available annulus (after gpScreenGap) that GP fills
  /**
   * Opt-in: carry Open Hole past a 7" liner's shoe down to the well's target depth, instead of ending
   * it with the rest of the completion elements at that shoe.
   *
   * A rendering policy rather than a geometry constant, but it lives here because `diagramLayout` is
   * the per-call-site seam the shared diagram already exposes — see the wwell-ir layouts, which are
   * tuned entirely through it without touching shared rendering code. Optional and falsy by default, so
   * Presentation's own layouts are unaffected and the shared renderer behaves exactly as it always has
   * for every caller that does not ask for this.
   */
  readonly openHoleRunsToTargetDepth?: boolean;
}

export const DIAGRAM_LAYOUT: DiagramLayout = {
  svgHeight: 840,
  marginTop: 55,
  drawingHeight: 740,

  // -- RESPONSIVE MIDPOINT MATH --
  depthScaleWidth: 116,
  depthAxisX: 82,

  wellboreViewWidth: 1040,

  // ── Horizontal zone layout (left → right inside wellbore SVG) ──────────
  // [0 - 34]    drill arrow body
  // [56 - 120]  geo depth labels
  // [120]       geo axis line
  // [120 - 210] geo formation codes
  // [210+]      gap before bore structure
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  depthArrowX: 24,
  geoLineX: 120,
  casingCenterX: 610,
  baseHalfWidth: 72,
  halfWidthIncrement: 26,
  shoeCurveOffset: 12,
  openHoleHwMargin: 25,
  linerScreenInset: 20,
  gpScreenGap: 3,       // 4px gap so screen is visually inside GP
  gpFillRatio: 1.0,     // fill full annulus by default; reduce to 0.5 etc. to narrow the GP band
};

// Fullscreen layout: landscape viewBox (1400 × 940) so aspect ratio matches a
// typical ~1.5:1 screen. With preserveAspectRatio:none, scale X ≈ scale Y ≈ 0.92
// so nothing distorts. drawingHeight 860 > normal 740 gives 16 % more vertical
// room per depth unit without requiring scrolling.
export const FULLSCREEN_DIAGRAM_LAYOUT: DiagramLayout = {
  svgHeight: 940,
  marginTop: 80,
  drawingHeight: 860,

  depthScaleWidth: 150,
  depthAxisX: 106,

  wellboreViewWidth: 1400,

  depthArrowX: 32,
  geoLineX: 162,
  casingCenterX: 820,
  baseHalfWidth: 96,
  halfWidthIncrement: 34,
  shoeCurveOffset: 16,
  openHoleHwMargin: 34,
  linerScreenInset: 26,
  gpScreenGap: 4,
  gpFillRatio: 1.0,
};

// ─── Animation mode ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 'realistic' : full cinematic sequence — casings drill down one by one,
//               then open-hole / overlays appear after all casings finish.
// 'fast'      : same sequence but 3× faster (good for development).
// 'instant'   : no animation, everything visible immediately.
export type AnimMode = 'realistic' | 'fast' | 'instant';

export const ANIM_MODE: AnimMode = 'fast';   // ← change this to switch modes

// Base timing profile (realistic).
// 'fast' divides every duration & delay by FAST_FACTOR.
// 'instant' sets everything to 0.
const FAST_FACTOR = 3;

function animValue(base: number): number {
  if (ANIM_MODE === 'instant') return 0;
  if (ANIM_MODE === 'fast') return Math.round(base / FAST_FACTOR);
  return base;
}

// ─── Core durations ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const _BASE = {
  SCALE_DURATION: 1000,   // depth scale draws down
  TICK_BASE_DELAY: 120,    // stagger for each depth tick
  GEO_DELAY: 200,    // geologic tops start appearing
  GEO_STAGGER: 55,     // stagger between each geo top
  CASING_DURATION: 800,    // each casing clip reveal duration
  CASING_STAGGER: 420,    // gap between each casing starting
  OH_DURATION: 800,    // open hole + liner screen clip reveal
  GRAVEL_DURATION: 600,    // gravel pack fill reveal
  HANGER_DURATION: 300,    // hanger bracket fade-in
  OVERLAY_FADE: 400,    // generic label / overlay fade-in
  SEQ_GAP: 200,    // breathing gap between sequential phases
};

// Number of structural casings determines when the last casing finishes.
// Components that must wait use computeOverlayDelay(casingCount).
export function computeOverlayDelay(casingCount: number): number {
  // Last casing (index 0 in draw order = innermost = last to animate) starts at:
  //   (casingCount - 1) * CASING_STAGGER, finishes after + CASING_DURATION
  const lastCasingDone = (casingCount - 1) * _BASE.CASING_STAGGER + _BASE.CASING_DURATION;
  return animValue(lastCasingDone + 100); // 100ms breathing room
}

// ─── Wellbore animation config (user-configurable) ────────────────────────

export type AnimSpeed    = 'slow' | 'normal' | 'fast';
export type AnimLineStyle = 'draw' | 'fade';          // draw = dashoffset, fade = opacity
export type AnimPopStyle  = 'elastic' | 'bounce' | 'fade';

export interface WellboreAnimConfig {
  speed:     AnimSpeed;
  lineStyle: AnimLineStyle;
  popStyle:  AnimPopStyle;
}

export const DEFAULT_ANIM_CONFIG: WellboreAnimConfig = {
  speed:     'normal',
  lineStyle: 'draw',
  popStyle:  'elastic',
};

export const ANIM = {
  SCALE_DURATION: animValue(_BASE.SCALE_DURATION),
  TICK_BASE_DELAY: animValue(_BASE.TICK_BASE_DELAY),
  GEO_DELAY: animValue(_BASE.GEO_DELAY),
  GEO_STAGGER: animValue(_BASE.GEO_STAGGER),
  CASING_DURATION: animValue(_BASE.CASING_DURATION),
  CASING_STAGGER: animValue(_BASE.CASING_STAGGER),
  OH_DURATION: animValue(_BASE.OH_DURATION),
  GRAVEL_DURATION: animValue(_BASE.GRAVEL_DURATION),
  HANGER_DURATION: animValue(_BASE.HANGER_DURATION),
  OVERLAY_FADE: animValue(_BASE.OVERLAY_FADE),
  SEQ_GAP: animValue(_BASE.SEQ_GAP),
  // Legacy alias so depth-scale component keeps working unchanged
  OVERLAY_DELAY: animValue((_BASE.CASING_STAGGER * 3) + _BASE.CASING_DURATION + 100),
} as const;
```

## [M] src/app/features/presentation/well-bore-view/renderers/completion.renderer.ts

```ts
import { easeCubicInOut } from 'd3-ease';
import 'd3-transition';

import { ANIM, DiagramLayout, WellboreDiagramData } from '@models/well-design/wellbore-diagram.model';
import {
  buildArrowHeadLeft,
  buildArrowHeadRight,
  buildGravelPackUPath,
  buildOpenHolePath,
  buildScreenHanger,
  computeCasingHalfWidth,
} from '@shared/utils/wellbore-math.util';
import { animateLabel } from './animation.helpers';
import {
  resolveCompletionBottomDepth,
  resolveCompletionBottomPx,
  resolveCompletionRule,
  resolveCompletionShoeCurvePx,
  resolveCompletionTopDepth,
  resolveCompletionTopPx,
  resolveOpenHoleBottomDepth,
  resolveOhHalfWidth,
  resolvePerforatedIntervalInfo,
  shouldRenderGravelPack,
  shouldRenderLinerScreen,
  shouldRenderOpenHole,
} from './wellbore-depth.helpers';
import { DefsSel, DepthScale, GSel, ResolvedAnimation } from './wellbore-renderer.types';

export interface RenderCompletionElementsOptions {
  readonly rootG: GSel;
  readonly defsEl: DefsSel;
  readonly layout: DiagramLayout;
  readonly data: WellboreDiagramData;
  readonly centerX: number;
  readonly scale: DepthScale;
  readonly ohStart: number;
  readonly ohLabelStart: number;
  readonly hangerStart: number;
  readonly animation: ResolvedAnimation;
}

export function renderCompletionElements(options: RenderCompletionElementsOptions): void {
  renderOpenHoleAndScreen(options);
  renderGravelPackDesign(options);
  renderPrePerforatedLiner(options);
}

function renderOpenHoleAndScreen({
  rootG,
  defsEl,
  layout,
  data,
  centerX,
  scale,
  ohStart,
  ohLabelStart,
  animation,
}: RenderCompletionElementsOptions): void {
  // Only skip when there's no casing data of any kind (planned or as-drilled) to anchor
  // to — a DRLG_CSG-only well (no EXAD_GWD_IR_CASING rows) must still render OH/GP when
  // its flags are 'Y', same as an IR-backed well.
  if (!data.casings.length && !data.drlgCasings.length) return;

  const { wellDesign } = data;
  const shouldDrawOH = shouldRenderOpenHole(wellDesign);
  // When a liner qualifies as the 7" completion target (Rule 2 / Rule 3 standard
  // evaluation), liner.renderer owns all liner rendering (shapes, hangers, and the 7"
  // screen via isScreen). The legacy completion-path screen is only drawn when no liner
  // qualifies (no liner at all, or a fallback well with an ordinary liner / no 7" match)
  // and lsFlg opts in.
  const rule = resolveCompletionRule(data.casings, data.drlgCasings);
  const shouldDrawLS = rule.targetLiner === null && shouldRenderLinerScreen(wellDesign);
  if (!shouldDrawOH && !shouldDrawLS) return;

  const { baseHalfWidth, halfWidthIncrement, linerScreenInset, gpScreenGap } = layout;
  const innerHW = computeCasingHalfWidth(0, baseHalfWidth, halfWidthIncrement);
  const ohHW = resolveOhHalfWidth(data, layout);
  const screenHW = Math.min(innerHW, ohHW) - linerScreenInset;
  const liner = data.casings.find(c => c.csgType === 'Liner');
  const gpCasing = data.casings.find(c => c.csgType === 'Gravel Pack');
  const shoePx = resolveCompletionTopPx(data.casings, data.drlgCasings, scale);
  const completionBottomDepth = resolveCompletionBottomDepth(data);
  // Extend past the resolved depth so the OH border reaches the liner's own rendered
  // shoe curve instead of stopping flat short of it (see resolveCompletionShoeCurvePx).
  const completionBottomPx = scale(completionBottomDepth)
    + resolveCompletionShoeCurvePx(data.casings, data.drlgCasings, layout);
  // Open Hole alone runs past the rest of the completion wherever a liner hangs inside it —
  // the hole is what the liner was run into, so it continues to target depth (see
  // resolveOpenHoleBottomDepth). Where it does, it ends in open rock rather than on the liner's
  // shoe, so the shoe-curve offset above is not added to it.
  const ohBottomDepth = resolveOpenHoleBottomDepth(data, layout);
  const ohBottomPx = ohBottomDepth > completionBottomDepth ? scale(ohBottomDepth) : completionBottomPx;
  const hasGP = shouldRenderGravelPack(wellDesign) || !!gpCasing;
  const gpBottomPx = gpCasing ? Math.min(scale(gpCasing.csgDepth), completionBottomPx) : completionBottomPx;
  const screenBottomPx = wellDesign
    ? (shouldDrawLS && hasGP ? gpBottomPx - gpScreenGap : completionBottomPx)
    : (liner ? scale(liner.csgDepth) : completionBottomPx - 15);
  // The clip drives the reveal for both shapes, so it has to reach whichever is drawn deepest —
  // sized to the completion bottom alone it would crop the open hole at the liner shoe, which is
  // the very thing being fixed here.
  const clipBottomPx = shouldDrawOH ? Math.max(completionBottomPx, ohBottomPx) : completionBottomPx;
  const clipHW = shouldDrawOH ? ohHW : screenHW;
  const clipId = 'dyn-oh-clip';
  const clipRect = defsEl.append('clipPath').attr('class', 'dyn-clip').attr('id', clipId).append('rect')
    .attr('x', centerX - clipHW - 15).attr('y', shoePx - 5).attr('width', clipHW * 2 + 30).attr('height', 0);

  if (shouldDrawOH) {
    rootG.append('path').attr('class', 'open-hole')
      .attr('d', buildOpenHolePath(centerX, ohHW, shoePx, ohBottomPx))
      .attr('clip-path', `url(#${clipId})`);
  }

  if (shouldDrawLS) {
    rootG.append('path').attr('class', 'liner-screen')
      .attr('d', buildOpenHolePath(centerX, screenHW, shoePx, screenBottomPx))
      .attr('clip-path', `url(#${clipId})`);
  }

  clipRect.transition().delay(ohStart).duration(ANIM.OH_DURATION).ease(easeCubicInOut).attr('height', clipBottomPx - shoePx + 5);

  if (shouldDrawOH) {
    const ohLabel = wellDesign?.ohRemarks ?? '8 1/2" Open Hole';
    const ohLY = shoePx + (ohBottomPx - shoePx) * 0.2;
    const ohLG = rootG.append('g').attr('class', 'open-hole-label');
    const ohLineX1 = centerX - ohHW;
    const ohLineX2 = centerX - ohHW - 33;

    ohLG.append('line')
      .attr('class', 'oh-label-line')
      .attr('x1', ohLineX1).attr('x2', ohLineX2).attr('y1', ohLY).attr('y2', ohLY);
    ohLG.append('path')
      .attr('class', 'oh-label-arrow')
      .attr('d', buildArrowHeadLeft(ohLineX2, ohLY, 6));
    ohLG.append('text')
      .attr('class', 'open-hole-text')
      .attr('x', ohLineX2 - 6).attr('y', ohLY + 4).attr('text-anchor', 'end').text(ohLabel);

    animateLabel(ohLG, ohLabelStart, animation);
  }

  if (shouldDrawLS) {
    const shoeDepth = resolveCompletionTopDepth(data.casings, data.drlgCasings);
    const fallbackFt = liner ? liner.csgDepth - shoeDepth : completionBottomDepth - shoeDepth;
    const screenLabel = wellDesign?.lsRemarks ?? (liner?.csgRemarks ?? `+/- ${fallbackFt.toLocaleString()} ft of Screen`);
    const scrDelay = ohLabelStart + ANIM.OVERLAY_FADE + ANIM.SEQ_GAP;
    const scrLG = rootG.append('g').attr('class', 'screen-label');
    const scrX1 = centerX + screenHW;
    const scrX2 = centerX + screenHW + 33;

    scrLG.append('line')
      .attr('class', 'screen-label-line')
      .attr('x1', scrX1).attr('x2', scrX2).attr('y1', screenBottomPx).attr('y2', screenBottomPx);
    scrLG.append('path')
      .attr('d', buildArrowHeadRight(scrX2, screenBottomPx, 6));
    scrLG.append('text')
      .attr('class', 'screen-label-text')
      .attr('x', scrX2 + 6).attr('y', screenBottomPx + 4).text(screenLabel);

    animateLabel(scrLG, scrDelay, animation);
  }
}

function renderGravelPackDesign({
  rootG,
  defsEl,
  layout,
  data,
  centerX,
  scale,
  ohStart,
  ohLabelStart,
  animation,
}: RenderCompletionElementsOptions): void {
  const wellDesign = data.wellDesign;
  if (!wellDesign || !shouldRenderGravelPack(wellDesign)) return;
  if (!data.casings.length && !data.drlgCasings.length) return;

  const { baseHalfWidth, halfWidthIncrement, linerScreenInset, gpScreenGap, gpFillRatio } = layout;
  const innerHW = computeCasingHalfWidth(0, baseHalfWidth, halfWidthIncrement);
  const ohHW = resolveOhHalfWidth(data, layout);
  const lsHW = Math.min(innerHW, ohHW) - linerScreenInset;
  const gpMaxOuter = ohHW - gpScreenGap;
  const annulusWidth = Math.max(0, (gpMaxOuter - lsHW) * gpFillRatio);
  const gpOuterEdge = lsHW + annulusWidth;
  const annulusCenter = lsHW + annulusWidth / 2;
  const startDepthPx = resolveCompletionTopPx(data.casings, data.drlgCasings, scale);
  // Extend past the resolved depth so the GP border reaches the liner's own rendered
  // shoe curve instead of stopping flat short of it (see resolveCompletionShoeCurvePx).
  const completionBottomPx = resolveCompletionBottomPx(data, scale)
    + resolveCompletionShoeCurvePx(data.casings, data.drlgCasings, layout);

  const clipId = 'dyn-gp-design-clip';
  const clipRect = defsEl.append('clipPath').attr('class', 'dyn-clip').attr('id', clipId).append('rect')
    .attr('x', centerX - gpOuterEdge).attr('y', startDepthPx)
    .attr('width', gpOuterEdge * 2).attr('height', 0);

  rootG.append('path')
    .attr('class', 'gravelHole')
    .attr('d', buildGravelPackUPath(centerX, annulusCenter, startDepthPx, completionBottomPx))
    .attr('stroke', 'url(#gravelpattern)')
    .attr('stroke-width', String(annulusWidth))
    .style('fill', 'none')
    .attr('clip-path', `url(#${clipId})`);

  clipRect.transition()
    .delay(ohStart)
    .duration(ANIM.GRAVEL_DURATION)
    .ease(easeCubicInOut)
    .attr('height', completionBottomPx - startDepthPx);

  const labelY = startDepthPx + (completionBottomPx - startDepthPx) * 0.5;
  const rEdge = centerX + gpOuterEdge;
  const lEnd = rEdge + 26;
  const lg = rootG.append('g').attr('class', 'gp-design-label');

  lg.append('line')
    .attr('class', 'screen-label-line')
    .attr('x1', rEdge).attr('x2', lEnd).attr('y1', labelY).attr('y2', labelY);
  lg.append('path').attr('d', buildArrowHeadRight(lEnd, labelY, 6));
  lg.append('text')
    .attr('class', 'screen-label-text')
    .attr('x', lEnd + 6).attr('y', labelY + 4).text(wellDesign.gpRemarks ?? 'Gravel Pack');

  animateLabel(lg, ohLabelStart, animation);
}

function renderPrePerforatedLiner({
  rootG,
  defsEl,
  layout,
  data,
  centerX,
  scale,
  ohStart,
  ohLabelStart,
  animation,
}: RenderCompletionElementsOptions): void {
  const wellDesign = data.wellDesign;
  const perfInfo = resolvePerforatedIntervalInfo(wellDesign, data.casings, data.drlgCasings);
  if (!wellDesign || !perfInfo) return;

  const { openHoleHwMargin } = layout;
  const ohHW = resolveOhHalfWidth(data, layout);
  const perfHwInset = Math.max(12, Math.min(20, openHoleHwMargin * 0.65));
  const prePerfHW = Math.max(10, ohHW - perfHwInset);
  // Same shared interval as Open Hole / Gravel Pack (docs/plan/logic.md Rules 1-3),
  // extended to reach the liner's own rendered shoe curve (see resolveCompletionShoeCurvePx).
  const perfTopPx = scale(resolveCompletionTopDepth(data.casings, data.drlgCasings));
  const perfBottomPx = resolveCompletionBottomPx(data, scale)
    + resolveCompletionShoeCurvePx(data.casings, data.drlgCasings, layout);

  const clipId = 'dyn-perf-clip';
  const clipRect = defsEl.append('clipPath').attr('class', 'dyn-clip').attr('id', clipId).append('rect')
    .attr('x', centerX - prePerfHW - 6).attr('y', perfTopPx - 5)
    .attr('width', (prePerfHW + 6) * 2).attr('height', 0);

  const perfGroup = rootG.append('g')
    .attr('class', 'perf-wall-group')
    .attr('clip-path', `url(#${clipId})`);

  // Same outline as open hole (left wall, bottom shoe, right wall in one continuous path) —
  // just a bigger dash gap and a thicker, darker stroke so it reads as its own element.
  perfGroup.append('path')
    .attr('class', 'pre-perf-liner')
    .attr('d', buildOpenHolePath(centerX, prePerfHW, perfTopPx, perfBottomPx));
  // linerOdSz comes straight from DRLG_CSG and may already carry a trailing inch mark
  // (e.g. `7"`) — strip it so the tooltip never doubles up as `7"" Pre Perf Liner`. Null
  // (Rule 1 / Rule 3 fallback — no single liner anchors the interval) falls back to a
  // generic label.
  const perfTitle = perfInfo.linerOdSz
    ? `${perfInfo.linerOdSz.replace(/["']/g, '').trim()}" Pre Perf Liner`
    : (wellDesign.perforatedRemarks ?? 'Perforated Interval');
  perfGroup.append('title').text(perfTitle);

  clipRect.transition()
    .delay(ohStart)
    .duration(ANIM.OH_DURATION)
    .ease(easeCubicInOut)
    .attr('height', perfBottomPx - perfTopPx + 10);

  // Visible on-diagram label — every sibling completion element (Open Hole, Gravel Pack,
  // Liner Screen) gets a leader-line text label; this one previously only carried a native
  // <title> tooltip, which is invisible until hover, so perforatedRemarks effectively never
  // showed up even when populated. Prefer the remarks text itself over perfTitle's generic
  // "Pre Perf Liner"/"Perforated Interval" fallback.
  const perfLabel = wellDesign.perforatedRemarks ?? perfTitle;
  const perfLY = perfTopPx + (perfBottomPx - perfTopPx) * 0.7;
  const perfLG = rootG.append('g').attr('class', 'perf-label');
  const perfLineX1 = centerX - prePerfHW;
  const perfLineX2 = perfLineX1 - 33;

  perfLG.append('line')
    .attr('class', 'oh-label-line')
    .attr('x1', perfLineX1).attr('x2', perfLineX2).attr('y1', perfLY).attr('y2', perfLY);
  perfLG.append('path')
    .attr('class', 'oh-label-arrow')
    .attr('d', buildArrowHeadLeft(perfLineX2, perfLY, 6));
  perfLG.append('text')
    .attr('class', 'open-hole-text')
    .attr('x', perfLineX2 - 6).attr('y', perfLY + 4).attr('text-anchor', 'end').text(perfLabel);

  animateLabel(perfLG, ohLabelStart, animation);
}

export function renderScreenHanger({
  rootG,
  layout,
  data,
  centerX,
  scale,
  hangerStart,
}: RenderCompletionElementsOptions): void {
  if (!data.casings.length && !data.drlgCasings.length) return;
  // Liner hangers are drawn per-liner by liner.renderer (for both DRLG and IR sources).
  // This function no longer draws a hanger for the liner itself — that produced a
  // duplicate OUTER hanger set. It only draws the well-design liner-screen hanger
  // (lsFlg='Y') for a screen that has no corresponding liner geometry.
  if (!shouldRenderLinerScreen(data.wellDesign)) return;
  // Only when no liner qualifies as the 7" completion target: liner.renderer already
  // draws a hanger for that target liner itself (isScreen), so this generic bracket would
  // duplicate it. When no liner qualifies (no liner at all, or a fallback well with an
  // ordinary liner / no 7" match), there's no liner geometry for the implied screen, so
  // this draws its hanger in place of one.
  if (resolveCompletionRule(data.casings, data.drlgCasings).targetLiner !== null) return;

  const { baseHalfWidth, halfWidthIncrement, linerScreenInset } = layout;
  const innerHW = computeCasingHalfWidth(0, baseHalfWidth, halfWidthIncrement);
  const ohHW = resolveOhHalfWidth(data, layout);
  const screenHW = Math.min(innerHW, ohHW) - linerScreenInset;
  const hangerG = rootG.append('g').attr('class', 'screen-hangers').style('opacity', 0);

  const screenY = scale(resolveCompletionTopDepth(data.casings, data.drlgCasings));
  const { left, right } = buildScreenHanger(centerX, screenY, screenHW);
  hangerG.append('path').attr('class', 'screen-hanger').attr('stroke', '#000000').attr('stroke-width', '1.5').attr('fill', '#000000').attr('d', left);
  hangerG.append('path').attr('class', 'screen-hanger').attr('stroke', '#000000').attr('stroke-width', '1.5').attr('fill', '#000000').attr('d', right);

  hangerG.transition().delay(hangerStart).duration(ANIM.HANGER_DURATION).ease(easeCubicInOut).style('opacity', 1);
}
```

import {

  resolveCompletionBottomDepth,
  resolveCompletionBottomPx,
  resolveCompletionTopDepth,
  resolveOhHalfWidth,
  resolveOpenHoleBottomDepth,
  resolvePerforatedIntervalInfo,
  resolveShoeDepth,
  shouldRenderGravelPack,
  shouldRenderLinerScreen,
  shouldRenderOpenHole,
  shouldRenderPerforatedInterval,
} from './wellbore-depth.helpers';
import {
  DIAGRAM_LAYOUT,
  DiagramLayout,
  FULLSCREEN_DIAGRAM_LAYOUT,
} from '@models/well-design/wellbore-diagram.model';
import { createDepthScale } from '@shared/utils/wellbore-math.util';
import { WellboreTestDataUtil } from '@shared/utils/testing/wellbore-test-data.util';

describe('wellbore-depth.helpers', () => {
  it('uses matching actual casing depth for the deepest structural casing', () => {
    const plannedCasing = WellboreTestDataUtil.casing({ csgSize: '13 3/8', csgDepth: 2020 });
    const actualCasing = WellboreTestDataUtil.drlgCasing({
      wCsgOdSz: '13 3/8',
      wCsgBotDpth: 2220,
    });

    expect(resolveShoeDepth([plannedCasing], [actualCasing])).toBe(2220);
  });

  it('falls back to planned casing depth when actual casing depth is missing', () => {
    const plannedCasing = WellboreTestDataUtil.casing({ csgSize: '13 3/8', csgDepth: 2020 });
    const actualCasing = WellboreTestDataUtil.drlgCasing({
      wCsgOdSz: '13 3/8',
      wCsgBotDpth: 0,
    });

    expect(resolveShoeDepth([plannedCasing], [actualCasing])).toBe(2020);
  });

  it('anchors completion top at the last casing shoe when a 7" liner is the completion target', () => {
    // Rule 2 standard evaluation ('single'): start depth is the casing shoe the liner
    // hangs from, not the liner's own bottom — the liner is drawn within that interval,
    // not below it.
    const liner = WellboreTestDataUtil.liner({ csgDepth: 3430 });
    const actualLiner = WellboreTestDataUtil.drlgCasing({
      wCsgOdSz: '13 3/8',
      wCsgBotDpth: 2020,
      wLnrOdSz: WellboreTestDataUtil.linerSize,
      wLnrBotDepth: 3600,
    });

    expect(resolveCompletionTopDepth([liner], [actualLiner])).toBe(2020);
  });

  it('anchors completion top at the casing shoe for a DRLG-only liner when IR has no Liner-typed casing', () => {
    // Mirrors real EXAD_GWD_IR_CASING data where only structural casings are planned
    // (no 'Liner' row), but DRLG_CSG reports an as-drilled liner.
    const structuralCasing = WellboreTestDataUtil.casing({ csgSize: '13 3/8', csgType: 'Casing', csgDepth: 2004 });
    const drlgLinerRow = WellboreTestDataUtil.drlgCasing({
      wCsgOdSz: '13 3/8',
      wCsgBotDpth: 2004,
      wLnrOdSz: '7',
      wLnrTopDepth: 1872,
      wLnrBotDepth: 3146,
    });

    expect(resolveCompletionTopDepth([structuralCasing], [drlgLinerRow])).toBe(2004);
  });

  it('draws completion to total depth when current depth does not exceed planned target', () => {
    // A non-7" liner takes the 'single-fallback' rule (Rule 2's non-target liner), the
    // only kind that runs the total-depth/overrun logic under test here — the default
    // fixture's 7" liner would instead resolve to its own fixed shoe depth.
    const data = WellboreTestDataUtil.diagramData({
      casings: [WellboreTestDataUtil.liner({ csgSize: '9 5/8' })],
      drlgCasings: [],
      totalDepth: WellboreTestDataUtil.plannedTargetDepthFt,
      displayDepth: WellboreTestDataUtil.plannedTargetDepthFt + WellboreTestDataUtil.displayDepthPaddingFt,
      currentDepth: WellboreTestDataUtil.plannedTargetDepthFt,
    });

    expect(resolveCompletionBottomDepth(data)).toBe(WellboreTestDataUtil.plannedTargetDepthFt);
  });

  it('uses a static completion span when current depth exceeds planned target depth', () => {
    const data = WellboreTestDataUtil.diagramData({
      casings: [WellboreTestDataUtil.liner({ csgSize: '9 5/8' })],

      totalDepth: WellboreTestDataUtil.currentDepthBeyondTargetFt,

    expect(resolveCompletionBottomDepth(data)).toBe(expectedBottomDepth);

    const irLayout: DiagramLayout = { ...DIAGRAM_LAYOUT, openHoleRunsToTargetDepth: true };

    // Open Hole is the drilled hole itself: a liner hangs INSIDE it, so it does not stop where the

    it('clamps open hole to the drawing domain', () => {

    it('falls back to total depth when no target depth has been entered yet', () => {
      const data = WellboreTestDataUtil.diagramData({

        totalDepth: 4600,
        currentDepth: 0,
        displayDepth: 5100,
      });

      expect(resolveOpenHoleBottomDepth(data, irLayout)).toBe(4600);

    it('leaves the shared default untouched, so Presentation draws what it always did', () => {
      // The guard that keeps this change inside wwell-ir: without the opt-in the helper must return the
      // shared completion bottom exactly, liner shoe and all.
      const data = WellboreTestDataUtil.diagramData();

      expect(resolveOpenHoleBottomDepth(data, FULLSCREEN_DIAGRAM_LAYOUT)).toBe(resolveCompletionBottomDepth(data));
      expect(resolveOpenHoleBottomDepth(data, DIAGRAM_LAYOUT)).toBe(WellboreTestDataUtil.linerDepthFt);

    });

    it('returns false when wellDesign is null', () => {
      expect(shouldRenderGravelPack(null)).toBe(false);
    });

  describe('shouldRenderLinerScreen', () => {
    it('returns true when lsFlg is Y and lsRemarks has text', () => {
      expect(shouldRenderLinerScreen(WellboreTestDataUtil.wellDesign({ lsFlg: 'Y', lsRemarks: 'Screen' }))).toBe(true);
    });

    // lsRemarks is free text used only for label copy — it must never gate visibility.
    it('returns true when lsRemarks is null', () => {
      expect(shouldRenderLinerScreen(WellboreTestDataUtil.wellDesign({ lsFlg: 'Y', lsRemarks: null }))).toBe(true);
    });

    it('returns false when lsFlg is N', () => {
      expect(shouldRenderLinerScreen(WellboreTestDataUtil.wellDesign({ lsFlg: 'N', lsRemarks: 'Screen' }))).toBe(false);
    });

    it('returns false when wellDesign is null', () => {
      expect(shouldRenderLinerScreen(null)).toBe(false);
    });
  });

  describe('resolvePerforatedIntervalInfo', () => {
    it('resolves the qualifying 7" liner OD when perforatedFlg is Y and a 7" liner is the completion target', () => {
      const wellDesign = WellboreTestDataUtil.wellDesign({
        perforatedFlg: 'Y',
        perforatedRemarks: '+/- 400 ft of 7" pre-perforated liner',

      const casings = [WellboreTestDataUtil.liner({ csgSize: '7', csgDepth: 3830 })];

      expect(resolvePerforatedIntervalInfo(wellDesign, casings, [])).toEqual({ linerOdSz: '7' });
    });

    it('resolves a null liner OD when perforatedFlg is Y but no liner qualifies as the 7" target', () => {
      const wellDesign = WellboreTestDataUtil.wellDesign({
        perforatedFlg: 'Y',
        perforatedRemarks: '7" perforated liner',
      });
      const casings = [WellboreTestDataUtil.casing({ csgSize: '13 3/8', csgDepth: 2020 })];

      expect(resolvePerforatedIntervalInfo(wellDesign, casings, [])).toEqual({ linerOdSz: null });
    });

    it('returns null when perforatedFlg is not Y', () => {
      const wellDesign = WellboreTestDataUtil.wellDesign({
        perforatedFlg: 'N',
        perforatedRemarks: '7" perforated liner',
      });
      const casings = [WellboreTestDataUtil.liner({ csgSize: '7', csgDepth: 3830 })];

      expect(resolvePerforatedIntervalInfo(wellDesign, casings, [])).toBeNull();
    });

    it('returns null when perforatedFlg is null', () => {
      const wellDesign = WellboreTestDataUtil.wellDesign({
        perforatedFlg: null,
        perforatedRemarks: '7" perforated liner',
      });

      expect(resolvePerforatedIntervalInfo(wellDesign, [], [])).toBeNull();
    });

    it('returns null when wellDesign is null', () => {
      expect(resolvePerforatedIntervalInfo(null, [], [])).toBeNull();
    });
  });

  describe('shouldRenderPerforatedInterval', () => {
    it('returns true when perforatedFlg is Y and a 7" liner is the completion target', () => {
      const wellDesign = WellboreTestDataUtil.wellDesign({
        perforatedFlg: 'Y',
        perforatedRemarks: '7" perforated liner',
      });
      const casings = [WellboreTestDataUtil.liner({ csgSize: '7', csgDepth: 3830 })];

      expect(shouldRenderPerforatedInterval(wellDesign, casings, [])).toBe(true);
    });

    // perforatedRemarks is free text used only for label copy downstream — it must never
    // gate visibility, so this stays true even though the remarks don't mention 7".
    it('returns true when perforatedRemarks has text but does not reference 7"', () => {
      const wellDesign = WellboreTestDataUtil.wellDesign({
        perforatedFlg: 'Y',
        perforatedRemarks: 'Perforated',
      });
      const casings = [WellboreTestDataUtil.liner({ csgSize: '7', csgDepth: 3830 })];

      expect(shouldRenderPerforatedInterval(wellDesign, casings, [])).toBe(true);
    });

    it('returns true when no liner qualifies as the 7" target, since the flag alone gates visibility', () => {
      const wellDesign = WellboreTestDataUtil.wellDesign({
        perforatedFlg: 'Y',
        perforatedRemarks: '7" perforated liner',
      });

      expect(shouldRenderPerforatedInterval(wellDesign)).toBe(true);
    });

    it('returns false when perforatedFlg is null', () => {
      const casings = [WellboreTestDataUtil.liner({ csgSize: '7', csgDepth: 3830 })];

      expect(shouldRenderPerforatedInterval(
        WellboreTestDataUtil.wellDesign({ perforatedFlg: null, perforatedRemarks: '7" perforated liner' }),
        casings,
      )).toBe(false);
    });

    it('returns false when wellDesign is null', () => {
      expect(shouldRenderPerforatedInterval(null)).toBe(false);
    });
  });
});

## [M] src/app/features/presentation/well-bore-view/renderers/wellbore-depth.helpers.ts

```ts
import { DiagramLayout, WellboreDiagramData } from '@models/well-design/wellbore-diagram.model';
import { ICasingIR } from '@shared/models/wwell/casing-ir.model';
import { IDrlgCsg } from '@shared/models/wwell/drlg-csg.model';
import { IWellDesign } from '@shared/models/wwell/well-design.model';
import { computeCasingHalfWidth, parseInchSize } from '@shared/utils/wellbore-math.util';
import { LinerRule, resolveLinerDepths, resolveLinerRule, resolveLiners } from './liner.renderer';
import { DepthScale } from './wellbore-renderer.types';

const OVERRUN_COMPLETION_LENGTH_FT = 1200;

export function resolveShoeDepth(casings: ICasingIR[], drlgCasings: IDrlgCsg[]): number {
  const irNonLiner = casings.filter(c => c.csgType !== 'Liner' && c.csgType !== 'Gravel Pack');
  const deepest = irNonLiner[0];
  if (!deepest) return drlgCasings[0]?.wCsgBotDpth ?? 0;

  const actual = drlgCasings.find(d => d.wCsgOdSz === deepest.csgSize) ?? null;
  return (actual !== null && actual.wCsgBotDpth > 0 ? actual.wCsgBotDpth : null) ?? deepest.csgDepth;
}

/** Central entry point for docs/plan/logic.md Rules 1-3 (see liner.renderer's resolveLinerRule). */
export function resolveCompletionRule(casings: ICasingIR[], drlgCasings: IDrlgCsg[]): LinerRule {
  const liners = resolveLinerDepths(casings, drlgCasings);
  const lastCasingShoe = resolveShoeDepth(casings, drlgCasings);
  return resolveLinerRule(liners, lastCasingShoe);
}

/** Start depth Open Hole / Gravel Pack / Perforated Interval share, per Rules 1-3. */
export function resolveCompletionTopDepth(casings: ICasingIR[], drlgCasings: IDrlgCsg[]): number {
  return resolveCompletionRule(casings, drlgCasings).startDepth;
}

export function resolveCompletionTopPx(
  casings: ICasingIR[],
  drlgCasings: IDrlgCsg[],
  scale: DepthScale,
): number {
  return scale(resolveCompletionTopDepth(casings, drlgCasings));
}

export function resolveCompletionBottomDepth(data: WellboreDiagramData): number {
  const rule = resolveCompletionRule(data.casings, data.drlgCasings);

  let naturalBottom: number;
  switch (rule.kind) {
    case 'none':
    case 'single-fallback': {
      // Rule 1, and Rule 2's non-7" liner: end = total depth, extended past it while
      // actively drilling beyond target.
      const isCurrentDepthBeyondTarget = data.targetDepth > 0 && data.currentDepth > data.targetDepth;
      naturalBottom = isCurrentDepthBeyondTarget
        ? Math.max(rule.startDepth, Math.min(data.totalDepth, rule.startDepth + OVERRUN_COMPLETION_LENGTH_FT))
        : data.totalDepth;
      break;
    }
    case 'single':
    case 'multi-target':
      // Rule 2 / Rule 3 standard evaluation: end = the 7" target liner's own shoe depth.
      naturalBottom = rule.structuralEndDepth ?? rule.startDepth;
      break;
    case 'multi-fallback': {
      // Rule 3 fallback: end = max(current depth, target depth), clamped to [start, TD].
      const fallbackEnd = Math.max(data.currentDepth || 0, data.targetDepth || 0);
      naturalBottom = Math.max(rule.startDepth, Math.min(data.totalDepth, fallbackEnd));
      break;
    }
  }

  return Math.min(naturalBottom, data.displayDepth);
}

/**
 * Bottom depth for the Open Hole element specifically.
 *
 * Open Hole is the drilled hole itself, and a liner hangs *inside* it — so unlike the elements that
 * belong to the liner (gravel pack, perforated interval), it does not stop where the liner stops. Under
 * Rule 2 / Rule 3 standard evaluation `resolveCompletionBottomDepth` returns the 7" target liner's own
 * shoe, which is correct for those elements and wrong for the hole around them: it left the liner and
 * screen drawn correctly but the open hole cut off at the hanger, with bare rock below.
 *
 * So open hole carries on to the well's target depth. Two guards:
 *  - `Math.max` against the liner bottom, so a target depth shallower than the shoe can never pull the
 *    hole up *inside* the liner and leave the liner sitting in undrilled rock;
 *  - `Math.min` against `displayDepth`, the drawing's own vertical domain, same as every other element.
 *
 * Every rule that already ends in open rock (Rule 1, and the fallbacks where no liner qualifies) is
 * unchanged — there is no liner shoe cutting it short, so there is nothing to extend past.
 *
 * Opt-in via `layout.openHoleRunsToTargetDepth`: without it this returns `resolveCompletionBottomDepth`
 * untouched, so Presentation and every other caller of the shared renderer draw exactly what they always
 * have. Only the wwell-ir layouts ask for it (see wwell-ir-preview-diagram-layout.ts).
 */
export function resolveOpenHoleBottomDepth(
  data: WellboreDiagramData,
  layout: DiagramLayout,
): number {
  const completionBottom = resolveCompletionBottomDepth(data);
  if (!layout.openHoleRunsToTargetDepth) return completionBottom;

  const kind = resolveCompletionRule(data.casings, data.drlgCasings).kind;
  if (kind !== 'single' && kind !== 'multi-target') return completionBottom;

  const target = data.targetDepth > 0 ? data.targetDepth : data.totalDepth;
  return Math.min(Math.max(completionBottom, target), data.displayDepth);
}

export function resolveCompletionBottomPx(data: WellboreDiagramData, scale: DepthScale): number {
  return scale(resolveCompletionBottomDepth(data));
}

/**
 * Extra pixels Open Hole / Gravel Pack / Perforated Interval should extend past their
 * resolved bottom depth so their border reaches the liner's own rendered shoe curve.
 * buildCasingPath draws the liner's shoe as a bulge that dips shoeCurveOffset px below
 * its flat bottom depth (see liner.renderer's renderLinerShapes) — without this, those
 * flat-bottomed completion paths visually stop short, leaving a bare gap at the tip.
 *
 * Only applies when the interval's END is bounded by a liner's own shoe (Rule 2 / Rule 3
 * standard evaluation, resolveLinerRule kind 'single' | 'multi-target'). The other kinds
 * all terminate in open rock (total depth, or current/target depth) with no liner shoe
 * to match at the bottom, so no curve applies there.
 */
export function resolveCompletionShoeCurvePx(
  casings: ICasingIR[],
  drlgCasings: IDrlgCsg[],
  layout: DiagramLayout,
): number {
  const kind = resolveCompletionRule(casings, drlgCasings).kind;
  return kind === 'single' || kind === 'multi-target' ? layout.shoeCurveOffset : 0;
}

function isFlagYes(flg: string | null | undefined): boolean {
  return (flg ?? '').trim().toUpperCase() === 'Y';
}

// NOTE: none of the shouldRender* gates below check *Remarks text. Remarks are free-form
// and frequently blank/null even when the well design has explicitly opted in via the
// flag — gating visibility on them would silently hide a feature the flag turned on.
// Remarks are only ever used for label copy downstream, with sensible fallback text when
// missing (see renderOpenHoleAndScreen / renderGravelPackDesign in completion.renderer).

export function shouldRenderOpenHole(wellDesign: IWellDesign | null | undefined): boolean {
  return isFlagYes(wellDesign?.ohFlg);
}

export function shouldRenderGravelPack(wellDesign: IWellDesign | null | undefined): boolean {
  return isFlagYes(wellDesign?.gpFlg);
}

export function shouldRenderLinerScreen(wellDesign: IWellDesign | null | undefined): boolean {
  return isFlagYes(wellDesign?.lsFlg);
}

export interface PerforatedIntervalInfo {
  /** OD of the is7InchLiner target the interval is drawn around (Rule 2 / Rule 3
   *  standard evaluation). Null in Rule 1 (no liner) and Rule 3's fallback, where the
   *  interval isn't anchored to any single liner. */
  readonly linerOdSz: string | null;
}

/**
 * Resolves whether the Perforated Interval element (EXAD_GWD_WELL_DESIGN.perforatedFlg)
 * should render, and which liner (if any) to reference in its label.
 *
 * perforatedRemarks is free text and only affects label copy elsewhere — it must never
 * gate visibility (see isPrePerforatedLiner in liner.renderer for the matching rationale).
 *
 * Per docs/plan/logic.md, perforatedFlg is drawn across the same shared interval as
 * ohFlg/gpFlg (resolveCompletionTopDepth/resolveCompletionBottomDepth) — every liner shape
 * (Rules 1-3) resolves to some interval, so this only gates on the flag itself.
 */
export function resolvePerforatedIntervalInfo(
  wellDesign: IWellDesign | null | undefined,
  casings: ICasingIR[],
  drlgCasings: IDrlgCsg[],
): PerforatedIntervalInfo | null {
  if (!isFlagYes(wellDesign?.perforatedFlg)) return null;

  const rule = resolveCompletionRule(casings, drlgCasings);
  return { linerOdSz: rule.targetLiner?.odSz ?? null };
}

export function shouldRenderPerforatedInterval(
  wellDesign: IWellDesign | null | undefined,
  casings: ICasingIR[] = [],
  drlgCasings: IDrlgCsg[] = [],
): boolean {
  return resolvePerforatedIntervalInfo(wellDesign, casings, drlgCasings) !== null;
}

export function resolveOhHalfWidth(data: WellboreDiagramData, layout: DiagramLayout): number {
  const { baseHalfWidth, halfWidthIncrement, openHoleHwMargin } = layout;
  const innerHW = computeCasingHalfWidth(0, baseHalfWidth, halfWidthIncrement);

  if (!isFlagYes(data.wellDesign?.ohFlg)) return innerHW + openHoleHwMargin;

  const ohDiameter = parseInchSize(data.wellDesign?.ohRemarks);
  if (ohDiameter === null) return innerHW + openHoleHwMargin;

  // innerHW is the on-screen half-width of the deepest structural casing (tier 0) — the
  // casing the anchor liner hangs from — so its real OD is the outer bound OH must stay inside.
  const structuralCasings = data.casings.filter(
    c => c.csgType !== 'Liner' && c.csgType !== 'Gravel Pack',
  );
  const outerOD = parseInchSize(structuralCasings[0]?.csgSize ?? null);

  // When liners exist, OH must render strictly outside the boundary liner it surrounds and
  // inside the casing it hangs from (e.g. a 12 1/4" OH must sit outside a 7" liner and
  // inside a 13 3/8" casing). Per-liner on-screen widths (anchorLiner.halfWidth, innerHW)
  // are a cosmetic decrement scheme with no fixed relationship to real inches, so scaling
  // one of them by a raw diameter ratio can overshoot the casing or undershoot the liner.
  // Instead, interpolate between the two known on-screen widths using the real diameter
  // ratio as the blend factor — this guarantees OH always lands between them.
  // boundaryLiner (see resolveLinerRule) is the liner immediately above the completion
  // interval's start — for Rule 3's fallback that's the deepest liner itself.
  const liners = resolveLiners(data.casings, data.drlgCasings, layout, data.wellDesign);
  const boundary = resolveCompletionRule(data.casings, data.drlgCasings).boundaryLiner;
  const anchorLiner = boundary !== null
    ? liners.find(l => l.odSz === boundary.odSz && l.botDepth === boundary.botDepth) ?? null
    : null;

  if (anchorLiner !== null) {
    const linerOD = parseInchSize(anchorLiner.odSz);
    if (linerOD !== null && linerOD > 0) {
      // No 7" target liner (single-fallback / multi-fallback): OH continues below the
      // boundary liner's own shoe rather than surrounding it, so it's normally narrower
      // than that liner — same telescoping-down relationship as the no-liner branch below.
      // The blend formula further down only handles OH wider than the liner; left
      // unguarded it clamps to 0 here and renders OH at the liner's own width instead of
      // nested inside it.
      if (ohDiameter < linerOD) {
        return anchorLiner.halfWidth * (ohDiameter / linerOD);
      }
      if (outerOD !== null && outerOD > linerOD) {
        const blend = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (ohDiameter - linerOD) / (outerOD - linerOD)));
        return anchorLiner.halfWidth + blend * (innerHW - anchorLiner.halfWidth);
      }
      // No known casing OD to blend against: fall back to a direct ratio, clamped so OH
      // never renders outside the innermost casing's on-screen width.
      return Math.min(anchorLiner.halfWidth * (ohDiameter / linerOD), innerHW);
    }
  }

  // No liners: scale relative to innermost structural casing
  if (outerOD !== null && outerOD > 0 && ohDiameter < outerOD) {
    return innerHW * (ohDiameter / outerOD);
  }

  return innerHW + openHoleHwMargin;
}

export function getCasingTier(csg: ICasingIR, casings: ICasingIR[]): number {
  const structuralCasings = casings.filter(item => item.csgType !== 'Gravel Pack');
  const idx = structuralCasings.indexOf(csg);
  return idx === -1 ? 0 : idx;
}
```

    }
    <text x="0" y="39" text-anchor="middle" [attr.opacity]="explodedOpacity()" class="muted">Core</text>
  </g>
  @for (s of strings(); track s.id; let i = $index) {

        <text [attr.x]="s.x" [attr.y]="s.bottomY+27" text-anchor="middle" class="number">{{s.csgDepth | number}}</text>
      </g>

  }
  <g [attr.opacity]="labelsOpacity()">
    @if (store.profile().water !== null) {
      <path [attr.d]="'M300 '+y(store.profile().water!)+' H401'" class="water-leader"/>
      <text x="292" [attr.y]="y(store.profile().water!)+4" text-anchor="end" class="water-text">Est. WL {{store.profile().water | number}}</text>

    @if (store.profile().pump !== null) {
      <line x1="310" x2="375" [attr.y1]="y(store.profile().pump!)" [attr.y2]="y(store.profile().pump!)" class="cable"/>
      <text x="302" [attr.y]="y(store.profile().pump!)+4" text-anchor="end" class="water-text">Pump {{store.profile().pump | number}}</text>

  <line x1="70" x2="740" [attr.y1]="y(store.depth())" [attr.y2]="y(store.depth())" class="depth-cursor"/>
  <circle cx="62" [attr.cy]="y(store.depth())" r="3" class="accent-fill"/>
  <text x="380" y="647" text-anchor="middle" class="muted">{{store.spread() > .5 ? 'Lateral separation · Depth positions unchanged' : 'Closed shells · Width exaggerated'}}</text>
</svg>

## [A] src/app/features/well-profile/well-profile-diagram.component.ts

```ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, computed, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { DecimalPipe } from '@angular/common';
import { WellProfileStore } from './well-profile.store';
import { depthY, projectStrings } from './well-profile.geometry';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-well-profile-diagram', standalone: true, imports: [DecimalPipe],
  templateUrl: './well-profile-diagram.component.html',
  styleUrl: './well-profile-svg.scss', changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class WellProfileDiagramComponent {
  readonly store = inject(WellProfileStore);
  readonly y = (depth: number) => depthY(depth, this.store.totalDepth());
  readonly formations = this.store.formations;
  readonly ticks = computed(() => Array.from({ length: 11 }, (_, i) => this.store.totalDepth() * i / 10));
  readonly strings = computed(() => projectStrings(this.store.strings(), this.store.spread(), this.store.totalDepth()));
  readonly drawOrder = computed(() => [...this.strings()].reverse());
  readonly coreX = computed(() => 380 + 270 * this.store.spread());
  readonly labelsOpacity = computed(() => Math.max(0, 1 - this.store.spread() * 2));
  readonly explodedOpacity = computed(() => Math.max(0, this.store.spread() * 2 - 1));
  readonly pumpSlots = [4, 7, 10];
}
```

    @if (x(f.bottom)-x(f.planTvdDepth) > 35) { <text [attr.x]="(x(f.planTvdDepth)+x(f.bottom))/2" [attr.y]="rowY(strings().length)+(large() ? 34 : 25)" text-anchor="middle" class="muted">{{f.stLongCd}}</text> }
  }
  <line [attr.x1]="x(store.depth())" [attr.x2]="x(store.depth())" [attr.y1]="rowY(0)-16" [attr.y2]="rowY(strings().length)+(large() ? 47 : 30)" class="depth-cursor"/>
  @if (large()) { <text x="12" [attr.y]="height()-12" class="muted">Shared ruler · True interval lengths · Overlap remains visible</text> }
</svg>

## [A] src/app/features/well-profile/well-profile-intervals.component.ts

```ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, computed, inject, input } from '@angular/core';
import { DecimalPipe } from '@angular/common';
import { WellProfileStore } from './well-profile.store';
import { intervalX } from './well-profile.geometry';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-well-profile-intervals', standalone: true, imports: [DecimalPipe],
  templateUrl: './well-profile-intervals.component.html', styleUrl: './well-profile-svg.scss',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class WellProfileIntervalsComponent {
  readonly large = input(false);
  readonly store = inject(WellProfileStore);
  readonly strings = this.store.strings;
  readonly formations = this.store.formations;
  readonly ticks = computed(() => [0, .25, .5, .75, 1].map(n => n * this.store.totalDepth()));
  readonly x = (depth: number) => intervalX(depth, this.store.totalDepth());
  readonly height = computed(() => this.rowY(this.strings().length)+100);
  rowY(index: number): number { return (this.large() ? 76 : 40) + index * (this.large() ? 65 : 23); }
}

import { TestBed } from '@angular/core/testing';
import { of, Observable, Subject, throwError } from 'rxjs';
import { mapIrHeaderSummaryToIntegratedRequirement } from '../wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-summary.mapper';
import { WwellIrService } from '../wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir.service';
import { WwellIrDetailTableBlock } from '../wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-payload.model';
import { WellProfileStore } from './well-profile.store';
import { buildProfile } from './well-profile.data';

const ir = mapIrHeaderSummaryToIntegratedRequirement({ irSeq: 1, prewapSeq: null, ltrNo: null, cmplType: null, testType: 'pump-test', publishFlg: 'N', createdDt: null, updatedDt: null });

describe('live well profile', () => {
  it('uses actual target depth, fractional diameters and explicit liner top and marks fallback liner attachment separately from actual TOL', () => {
    const p = buildProfile({ ...ir, requestReference: { ...ir.requestReference, targetDepthFt: 5200 }, casing: [
      { type: 'Production', sizeIn: '13 3/8', depthFtBgl: 3000, tolFtBgl: null, remarks: null },
      { type: 'Liner', sizeIn: '9 5/8', depthFtBgl: 5000, tolFtBgl: 2800, remarks: null },
      { type: 'Liner', sizeIn: '7', depthFtBgl: 5200, tolFtBgl: null, remarks: null },
    ] });
    expect(p.totalDepth).toBe(5200);
    expect(p.strings.map(s => s.top)).toEqual([0, 2800, 5000]);
    expect(p.warnings.length).toBe(1);
    expect(p.strings[2].topInferred).toBe(true);
    expect(p.pump).toBeNull();
    expect(p.water).toBeNull();
  });
  it('surfaces list failures and can retry without introducing sample records', () => {
    const service = { getIrs: jest.fn((): Observable<typeof ir[]> => throwError(() => new Error('offline'))) };
    TestBed.configureTestingModule({ providers: [WellProfileStore, { provide: WwellIrService, useValue: service }] });
    const s = TestBed.inject(WellProfileStore);
    s.loadIrs();
    expect(s.listLoading()).toBe(false);
    expect(s.listError()).toBeTruthy();
    expect(s.irs()).toEqual([]);
    service.getIrs.mockImplementation(() => of([ir]));
    s.loadIrs();
    expect(s.listError()).toBe('');
    expect(s.irs()[0].irSeq).toBe(1);
    expect(s.record()).toBeNull();
  });
  it('cancels old selections and clears stale data on detail failure', () => {
    const first = new Subject<readonly WwellIrDetailTableBlock[]>();
    const second = new Subject<readonly WwellIrDetailTableBlock[]>();
    const service = { getIrs: jest.fn(() => of([ir, { ...ir, irSeq: 2, id: 'IR-2', code: 'IR-2' }])), getIrDetailBlocks: jest.fn((id: number): Observable<readonly WwellIrDetailTableBlock[]> => id === 1 ? first : second) };
    TestBed.configureTestingModule({ providers: [WellProfileStore, { provide: WwellIrService, useValue: service }] });
    const s = TestBed.inject(WellProfileStore);
    s.loadIrs(); s.loadIr(1); s.loadIr(2);
    second.next([]); first.next([]);
    expect(s.record()?.irSeq).toBe(2);
    service.getIrDetailBlocks.mockImplementation(() => throwError(() => new Error('offline')));
    s.loadIr(1);
    expect(s.record()).toBeNull();
    expect(s.detailError()).toBeTruthy();
    expect(s.strings()).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

:host { display: block; width: 100%; height: 100%; min-width: 0; min-height: 0; }
svg { display: block; width: 100%; height: 100%; }
text { font: 12px 'Geist Variable', Arial, sans-serif; fill: var(--ink); stroke: none; }
.number { font-family: 'Geist Mono Variable', monospace; font-size: 11px; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
.muted { fill: var(--muted); } .muted-fill { fill: var(--muted); }
.axis, .rim { stroke: var(--muted); fill: none; }
.grid-line { stroke: var(--line); stroke-dasharray: 2 5; }
.formation-band { fill: var(--rock); opacity: .24; } .target { fill: var(--accent); }
.rock-fill { fill: var(--rock); } .steel-fill { fill: var(--steel); }
.screen-lines { stroke: var(--muted); stroke-width: .7; }
.open-hole { fill: none; stroke: var(--muted); stroke-dasharray: 4 3; }
.gravel { stroke: var(--rock); stroke-width: .7; }
.screen-body { fill: var(--paper); stroke: var(--muted); stroke-width: .8; }
.shell { stroke-width: .85; cursor: pointer; }
.shell.selected { stroke: var(--accent); stroke-width: 1.5; }
.inner-rim { opacity: .7; stroke-width: .6; }
.core { pointer-events: none; }
.water-column { fill: var(--water); fill-opacity: .07; }
.water-surface { fill: var(--water); fill-opacity: .22; stroke: var(--water); }
.riser { stroke: var(--muted); stroke-width: 2; }
.cable { stroke: var(--water); stroke-width: .8; }
.pump { fill: var(--steel); stroke: var(--ink); stroke-width: .8; }
.callout { fill: var(--muted); stroke: var(--muted); stroke-width: 1; pointer-events: none; }
.callout path { fill: none; }.callout text { fill: var(--muted); }
.callout-selected { fill: var(--accent); stroke: var(--accent); }.callout-selected text { fill: var(--accent); }
.water-leader { fill: none; stroke: var(--water); stroke-dasharray: 4 3; }.water-text { fill: var(--water); }
.depth-cursor { stroke: var(--accent); stroke-dasharray: 3 4; opacity: .65; pointer-events: none; }.accent-fill { fill: var(--accent); }
.interval { cursor: pointer; }.interval.active rect { stroke: var(--accent); stroke-width: 1.5; }.interval.active text { fill: var(--accent); }
.completion-interval { fill: var(--rock); stroke: var(--muted); stroke-dasharray: 2 2; }
g[tabindex]:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--accent); outline-offset: 4px; }

.intervals-svg, .intervals-svg.large { height: 100%; }

## [A] src/app/features/well-profile/well-profile.component.html

```html
<section class="well-profile" [class.dark]="theme.isDark()">
  <header class="heading">
    <div><div class="eyebrow">WELL PLANNING / WELL PROFILE</div><h1>{{store.record()?.requestReference?.wellName || 'Well Profile'}}</h1><p>{{store.record()?.code || 'Select an integrated requirement to review its well design'}}</p></div>
    <mat-form-field appearance="outline" subscriptSizing="dynamic" class="ir-selector"><mat-label>Integrated requirement</mat-label>
      <mat-select panelClass="well-profile-ir-panel" [value]="store.irSeq()" (selectionChange)="selectIr($event.value)" [disabled]="store.listLoading()">
        @for (ir of store.irs(); track ir.irSeq) { @if (ir.irSeq !== null && ir.irSeq !== undefined) { <mat-option [value]="ir.irSeq">{{ir.code}}{{ir.requestReference.wellName ? ' · '+ir.requestReference.wellName : ''}}</mat-option> } }
      </mat-select>
    </mat-form-field>
  </header>
  @if (store.listLoading() || store.detailLoading()) { <mat-progress-bar mode="indeterminate" aria-label="Loading integrated requirements"/> }
  @if (store.listError()) { <div class="status" role="alert">{{store.listError()}} <button mat-button (click)="store.loadIrs()">Retry list</button></div> }
  @if (store.detailError()) { <div class="status" role="alert">{{store.detailError()}} <button mat-button (click)="selectIr(store.irSeq()!)">Retry detail</button></div> }
  @if (!store.listLoading() && !store.listError() && !store.irs().length) { <p class="status">No integrated requirements are available.</p> }
  @if (!store.record() && !store.detailLoading() && store.irs().length && !store.detailError()) { <p class="status">Select an IR above to load its casing program, formations and estimated hydrology.</p> }
  @if (store.record(); as ir) {
    <div class="summary">
      <div><span>Profile depth</span><strong>{{store.totalDepth() ? (store.totalDepth() | number) : 'Not specified'}} <small>ft bgl</small></strong></div>
      <div><span>Estimated water level</span><strong>{{store.profile().water !== null ? (store.profile().water | number) : 'Not specified'}} <small>ft bgl</small></strong></div>
      <div><span>Estimated pump setting</span><strong>{{store.profile().pump !== null ? (store.profile().pump | number) : 'Not specified'}} <small>ft bgl</small></strong></div>
      <div><span>Target formation</span><strong>{{ir.requestReference.targetAquifer || ir.hydrogeology.aquifer || 'Not specified'}}</strong></div>
    </div>
    @if (store.totalDepth() > 0) {
  <div class="toolbar">
    <div class="modes"><mat-button-toggle-group [hideSingleSelectionIndicator]="true" aria-label="Well visualization" [value]="store.mode()" (change)="store.setMode($event.value)">
      @for (mode of modes; track mode.value) { <mat-button-toggle [value]="mode.value">{{mode.label}}</mat-button-toggle> }
    </mat-button-toggle-group>
    <button mat-stroked-button (click)="explode()" [attr.aria-pressed]="store.exploded()"><mat-icon>{{store.exploded() ? 'layers' : 'view_in_ar'}}</mat-icon>{{store.exploded() ? 'Reassemble' : 'Explode'}}</button></div>
  <div class="fills"><label>Fill casing <input #casingColor type="color" [value]="store.casingFill()" (input)="store.setFill('casingFill', casingColor.value)"></label><label>Fill liners <input #linerColor type="color" [value]="store.linerFill()" (input)="store.setFill('linerFill', linerColor.value)"></label></div>
    @if (store.mode() === 'transparent') { <div class="opacity"><span>Shell opacity</span><mat-slider min="10" max="70"><input matSliderThumb aria-label="Shell opacity" [value]="store.opacity()" (valueChange)="store.setOpacity($event)"></mat-slider><span>{{store.opacity()}}%</span></div> }
  </div>
    <div class="review-grid">
      <section class="canvas">
        <div class="panel-head"><h2>{{viewTitle()}}</h2><span>Depth & horizons · ft bgl</span></div>
        <div class="diagram-scroll">@if (store.mode() === 'profile') { <app-well-profile-intervals [large]="true"/> } @else { <app-well-profile-diagram/> }</div>
        <div class="depth-control"><div>Inspect a depth <strong>{{store.depth() | number}} ft</strong></div><mat-slider min="0" [max]="store.totalDepth()" step="1"><input matSliderThumb aria-label="Inspect a depth in feet" [value]="store.depth()" (valueChange)="store.setDepth($event)"></mat-slider><p aria-live="polite">{{store.reading()}}</p></div>
        <div class="canvas-foot">{{store.mode() === 'solid' ? '45% shell opacity · Intact walls · Width exaggerated' : 'Shared depth scale · Actual diameter ratios · Width exaggerated'}}</div>
      </section>

        @if (store.selected(); as selected) {
          <section class="panel selected-panel" aria-label="Selected casing details"><div class="eyebrow">SELECTED STRING</div><h3 class="selection" aria-live="polite">{{selectedLabel()}}</h3><div class="kvs">
            <div><span>{{selected.topInferred ? 'Attachment (inferred)' : 'Top'}} · ft bgl</span><b>{{selected.top | number}}</b></div><div><span>Shoe · ft bgl</span><b>{{selected.csgDepth | number}}</b></div>
            <div><span>Length · ft</span><b>{{selected.csgDepth - selected.top | number}}</b></div><div><span>Outer diameter</span><b>{{selected.csgSize}} in</b></div>
          </div><p class="note">{{selected.topInferred ? 'Liner attachment shown at the preceding shoe; actual TOL is not recorded.' : ''}}</p><p class="note">{{selected.csgRemarks || 'No casing remarks recorded.'}}</p></section>
        }
      <section class="panel program" aria-label="Casing program"><h3>Casing program</h3><ag-grid-angular [modules]="modules" [theme]="gridTheme()" [rowData]="store.strings()" [columnDefs]="columns" [headerHeight]="30" [rowHeight]="34" domLayout="normal" [rowSelection]="rowSelection" (gridReady)="onGridReady($event)" (rowClicked)="store.select($event.data?.id ?? '')" (cellKeyDown)="onCellKeyDown($event)"/><p class="note">Depths in ft bgl. Select a row to inspect.</p></section>

      <section class="panel formations" aria-label="Formation intersections"><h3>Formation intersections</h3><p class="note">Across the selected string</p><div class="formation-list">@for (f of store.coverage(); track $index) { <div class="coverage"><span>{{f.name}}</span><span>{{f.length | number}} ft</span><div class="track"><i [style.width.%]="f.length / f.total * 100"></i></div></div> } @empty { <p class="note">No formation intersections available.</p> }</div></section>

      <section class="panel details" aria-label="Completion and hydrology"><h3>Completion & hydrology</h3><p class="completion-text">{{store.profile().completion}}</p><p class="note">{{store.profile().completionNote}}</p>
      <div class="kvs"><div><span>Test program</span><b>{{ir.testProgram.testType}}</b></div><div><span>Estimated rate · gpm</span><b>{{ir.testProgram.estimatedRateGpm ?? 'Not specified'}}</b></div><div><span>Estimated TDS · ppm</span><b>{{ir.hydrogeology.estimatedTdsPpm ?? 'Not specified'}}</b></div><div><span>H₂S · ppm</span><b>{{ir.hydrogeology.h2sPpm ?? 'Not specified'}}</b></div></div>
    </section>


    </div>
    } @else { <p class="status">No valid target depth or casing depths are recorded for this IR.</p> }
    <footer>@if (store.profile().warnings.length) { <button mat-button [matTooltip]="store.profile().warnings.join('\n')" aria-label="View data notes"><mat-icon>info_outline</mat-icon>{{store.profile().warnings.length}} data notes</button> }<span>{{ir.code}} · {{ir.status}}</span><span>Saved IR design · Pump and water levels are estimates</span></footer>
  }
</section>
```

:host { display: block; min-width: 0; }
.well-profile {
  --paper: #fff; --page: #f7f8fa; --ink: #182633; --muted: #5e6c7c; --line: #dde3e9;
  --soft: #edf2f8; --accent: #2858c8; --water: #087e9c; --steel: #899ba8; --rock: #c6bca6;
  --radius-panel: 8px; --mat-button-toggle-height: 36px;
  --mat-button-toggle-text-color: var(--ink); --mat-button-toggle-background-color: var(--paper);
  --mat-button-toggle-selected-state-background-color: var(--soft); --mat-button-toggle-selected-state-text-color: var(--accent);
  --mat-button-toggle-divider-color: var(--line); --mat-button-toggle-shape: 6px;
  --mat-button-toggle-label-text-font: 'Geist Variable', sans-serif;
  --mat-button-toggle-label-text-size: 12px; --mat-sys-label-large-font: 'Geist Variable', sans-serif;
  --mat-sys-primary: var(--accent); --mat-sys-on-surface: var(--ink); --mat-sys-outline: var(--line);
  color-scheme: light; background: var(--page); color: var(--ink); padding: 24px; font: 13px/1.45 'Geist Variable', Arial, sans-serif;
  &.dark { --page: #111a23; --ink: #e5edf4; --muted: #a6b6c6; --line: #344250; --soft: #243344; --accent: #92b1ff; --water: #62cce6; --steel: #93a9bb; --rock: #918775; color-scheme: dark; }
}
* { box-sizing: border-box; }
h1 { margin: 4px 0; font-size: 26px; font-weight: 650; letter-spacing: -.035em; } h2 { font-size: 14px; margin: 0; font-weight: 600; } h3 { font-size: 13px; margin: 0 0 12px; }
p { margin: 6px 0; }.eyebrow, .note { color: var(--muted); font-size: 11px; }.eyebrow { letter-spacing: .05em; }
.heading, .theme-controls, .toolbar, .modes, .opacity { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.heading, .toolbar { justify-content: space-between; }.heading p { color: var(--muted); font-size: 12px; }
.badge { display: inline-block; background: var(--soft); border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 4px; padding: 3px 7px; margin-left: 10px; font-size: 11px; }
.tone { width: 158px; } .tone small { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; color: var(--muted); font-size: 10px; } .tone mat-slider { width: calc(100% - 16px); margin-bottom: -10px; }
.summary { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 32px; padding: 14px 0; margin: 18px 0; border-block: 1px solid var(--line); }
.summary span, .kvs span { display: block; font-size: 11px; color: var(--muted); }.summary strong { font: 600 16px 'Geist Mono Variable', monospace; }.summary small { font-size: 11px; }
.toolbar { margin-bottom: 10px; }.modes { gap: 8px; }button { border-radius: 6px; }.opacity { color: var(--muted); font-size: 11px; gap: 6px; }.opacity mat-slider { width: 90px; }
.fills { display: flex; gap: 18px; margin-bottom: 14px; }.fills label { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; color: var(--muted); font-size: 12px; }.fills input { width: 32px; height: 28px; padding: 2px; border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 5px; background: var(--paper); cursor: pointer; }
.review-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) 320px; gap: 14px; align-items: start; }.canvas, .panel, .intervals { background: var(--paper); border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: var(--radius-panel); overflow: hidden; min-width: 0; }
.panel-head { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; gap: 8px; flex-wrap: wrap; padding: 12px 14px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); }.panel-head span { font-size: 11px; color: var(--muted); }.diagram-scroll { overflow-x: auto; }
.inspector { display: grid; gap: 12px; }.panel { padding: 14px; }.selection { font-size: 20px; margin: 6px 0 14px; }.kvs { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 12px; margin-bottom: 12px; }.kvs b { font: 500 13px 'Geist Mono Variable', monospace; }.note { line-height: 1.6; }.program { padding-inline: 8px; }.program h3, .program .note { margin-inline: 6px; }ag-grid-angular { display: block; width: 100%; }
.coverage { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr auto; gap: 6px; margin: 8px 0; font-size: 11px; }.track { grid-column: 1/-1; height: 4px; background: var(--soft); border-radius: 2px; }.track i { display: block; height: 100%; background: var(--accent); border-radius: inherit; }.relationship { font: 500 20px 'Geist Mono Variable', monospace; color: var(--accent); }
.depth-control { padding: 12px 14px; border-top: 1px solid var(--line); }.depth-control > div { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; }.depth-control mat-slider { width: calc(100% - 16px); }.depth-control p { font-size: 11px; color: var(--muted); }.canvas-foot { padding: 10px 14px; border-top: 1px solid var(--line); font-size: 11px; color: var(--muted); }
.intervals { margin-top: 14px; }footer { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px; margin-top: 14px; font-size: 11px; color: var(--muted); }
@media (max-width: 1100px) { .review-grid { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr); }.inspector { grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; } }
@media (max-width: 600px) { .well-profile { padding: 12px; }.inspector { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr); }.summary { gap: 18px; }.theme-controls { width: 100%; }.modes { max-width: 100%; }mat-button-toggle-group { font-size: 11px; }.heading { align-items: start; } }

.well-profile, .well-profile.dark {
  --paper: var(--wp-surface, #fff); --page: var(--wp-page, #f7f8fa); --ink: var(--wp-text, #182633);
  --muted: var(--wp-muted, #5e6c7c); --line: var(--wp-line, #dde3e9); --soft: var(--wp-surface-alt, #edf2f8); --accent: var(--wp-accent, #2858c8);
}
.ir-selector { width: min(100%, 360px); }.status { padding: 24px 0; color: var(--muted); }.details { margin-top: 14px; }.completion-text { white-space: pre-line; }

// One workspace fills the shell; only long detail datasets scroll within their panel.
:host { height: 100%; min-height: 0; overflow: hidden; }
.well-profile { height: 100%; min-height: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; padding: 12px 16px; overflow: hidden; }
.heading { flex: 0 0 auto; flex-wrap: nowrap; gap: 16px; }
h1 { font-size: 21px; margin: 1px 0; }.heading p { margin: 0; font-size: 11px; }.eyebrow { font-size: 10px; }
.ir-selector { width: 280px; flex-shrink: 0; --mat-form-field-container-height: 44px; --mat-form-field-container-vertical-padding: 10px; }
.summary { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap: 10px; margin: 0; padding: 0; border: 0; flex-shrink: 0; }
.summary > div { background: var(--paper); border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: var(--radius-panel); padding: 7px 12px; min-width: 0; }
.summary strong { font-size: 14px; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }.summary span { font-size: 10px; }
.toolbar { margin: 0; gap: 8px; flex-shrink: 0; }.modes { gap: 6px; }.fills { gap: 12px; margin: 0; }.fills label { font-size: 11px; }.fills input { height: 24px; width: 28px; }
.opacity mat-slider { width: 70px; }.opacity { gap: 2px; }
.review-grid { flex: 1 1 0; min-height: 0; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) 330px; align-items: stretch; gap: 10px; }
.canvas { min-height: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; }
.panel-head { padding: 7px 12px; flex-shrink: 0; }h2 { font-size: 12px; }.panel-head span { font-size: 10px; }
.diagram-scroll { flex: 1 1 0; min-height: 0; min-width: 0; overflow: hidden; }
.depth-control { flex-shrink: 0; display: grid; grid-template-columns: 170px minmax(70px, 1fr); align-items: center; column-gap: 12px; padding: 0 12px 5px; }
.depth-control > div { gap: 8px; font-size: 11px; }.depth-control mat-slider { margin-block: 0; }.depth-control p { grid-column: 1 / -1; margin: 0; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; }
.canvas-foot { padding: 4px 12px; font-size: 10px; flex-shrink: 0; }
// All four information panels remain visible while the diagram keeps full height.
.review-grid {
  grid-template-columns: minmax(270px, 0.9fr) minmax(300px, 2fr) minmax(230px, 0.8fr);
  grid-template-rows: minmax(0, 0.85fr) minmax(0, 1.15fr);
  grid-template-areas: 'selection diagram formations' 'program diagram completion';
}
.canvas { grid-area: diagram; }
.review-grid > .panel { min-width: 0; min-height: 0; padding: 10px 12px; margin: 0; overflow: auto; }
.selected-panel { grid-area: selection; }
.program { grid-area: program; display: flex; flex-direction: column; }
.review-grid > .program { overflow: hidden; }
.program h3 { margin-bottom: 6px; }
.program ag-grid-angular { flex: 1 1 0; min-height: 0; }
.program .note { flex-shrink: 0; }
.formations { grid-area: formations; display: flex; flex-direction: column; }
.formation-list { min-height: 0; overflow: auto; }
.details { grid-area: completion; }
.selection { font-size: 17px; margin: 2px 0 8px; }
.kvs { gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 6px; }
.kvs b { overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.note { margin: 4px 0; font-size: 10px; }
footer { flex-shrink: 0; align-items: center; margin: 0; font-size: 10px; gap: 8px; }footer button { --mat-text-button-container-height: 24px; font-size: 10px; }footer mat-icon { font-size: 15px; height: 15px; width: 15px; }
@media (max-width: 1100px) {
  .review-grid {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(280px, 1.25fr);
    grid-template-rows: minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(160px, 0.8fr);
    grid-template-areas: 'diagram diagram selection' 'formations completion program';
  }
  .toolbar { justify-content: flex-start; }
  .summary { gap: 6px; }
  .summary > div { padding: 6px 8px; }
}
@media (max-width: 700px) {
  :host { overflow: auto; }
  .well-profile { height: auto; min-height: 100%; overflow: visible; padding: 8px; gap: 6px; }
  .heading { gap: 8px; }
  .heading .eyebrow { display: none; }
  .heading h1 { font-size: 16px; }
  .ir-selector { width: 190px; }
  .summary { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)); }
  .review-grid { flex: none; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr); grid-template-rows: 420px auto 240px auto auto; grid-template-areas: 'diagram' 'selection' 'program' 'formations' 'completion'; }
  .depth-control { display: block; }
  .depth-control mat-slider { width: calc(100% - 16px); }
  .panel-head span, .canvas-foot, footer > span:last-child { display: none; }
}
