## [A] src/app/features/well-profile/well-profile.component.ts

```ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, DestroyRef, computed, effect, inject, viewChild } from '@angular/core';
import { DecimalPipe } from '@angular/common';
import { MatButtonModule } from '@angular/material/button';
import { MatButtonToggleModule } from '@angular/material/button-toggle';
import { MatIconModule } from '@angular/material/icon';
import { MatSliderModule } from '@angular/material/slider';
import { MatFormFieldModule } from '@angular/material/form-field';
import { MatSelectModule } from '@angular/material/select';
import { MatTooltipModule } from '@angular/material/tooltip';
import { MatProgressBarModule } from '@angular/material/progress-bar';
import { WwellThemeService } from '../wwell-planning/shared/wwell-theme.service';
import { AgGridAngular } from 'ag-grid-angular';
import { AllCommunityModule, CellKeyDownEvent, ColDef, FullWidthCellKeyDownEvent, GridReadyEvent, themeQuartz } from 'ag-grid-community';
import { WellProfileStore } from './well-profile.store';
import { ProfileString } from './well-profile.data';
import { WellProfileDiagramComponent } from './well-profile-diagram.component';
import { WellProfileIntervalsComponent } from './well-profile-intervals.component';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-well-profile', standalone: true,
  imports: [DecimalPipe, MatButtonModule, MatButtonToggleModule, MatIconModule, MatSliderModule, MatFormFieldModule, MatSelectModule, MatProgressBarModule, MatTooltipModule,
    AgGridAngular, WellProfileDiagramComponent, WellProfileIntervalsComponent],
  providers: [WellProfileStore], templateUrl: './well-profile.component.html',
  styleUrl: './well-profile.component.scss', changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class WellProfileComponent {
  readonly store = inject(WellProfileStore);
  private readonly destroyRef = inject(DestroyRef);
  private frame = 0;
  private readonly grid = viewChild(AgGridAngular<ProfileString>);
  readonly rowSelection = { mode: 'singleRow' as const, checkboxes: false, enableClickSelection: false };
  readonly theme = inject(WwellThemeService);
  readonly modules = [AllCommunityModule];
  readonly modes = [{ value: 'solid', label: '3D' }, { value: 'transparent', label: 'Transparent' },
    { value: 'xray', label: 'X-ray' }, { value: 'profile', label: 'Profile' }];
  readonly columns: ColDef<ProfileString>[] = [
    { headerName: 'String / OD', flex: 1, minWidth: 110, valueGetter: p => `${p.data?.csgSize}″ ${p.data?.csgType}` },
    { field: 'top', headerName: 'Top', width: 60 },
    { field: 'csgDepth', headerName: 'Shoe', width: 64 },
  ];
  readonly gridTheme = computed(() => themeQuartz.withParams({
    backgroundColor: 'var(--wp-surface)', foregroundColor: 'var(--wp-text)',
    headerBackgroundColor: 'var(--wp-surface-alt)',
    borderColor: 'var(--wp-line)', fontSize: 11, spacing: 4,
    selectedRowBackgroundColor: 'var(--wp-accent-soft)',
  }));
  readonly selectedLabel = computed(() => `${this.store.selected()?.csgSize}″ ${this.store.selected()?.csgType}`);
  readonly viewTitle = computed(() => this.store.exploded() && this.store.mode() !== 'profile' ? 'Exploded assembly · depth aligned' :
    ({ solid: '3D · translucent casing assembly', transparent: 'Nested casing assembly', xray: 'X-ray · all string extents', profile: 'Construction interval analysis' })[this.store.mode()]);

  constructor() {
    this.store.loadIrs();
    this.destroyRef.onDestroy(() => cancelAnimationFrame(this.frame));
    effect(() => {
      const id = this.store.selectedId();
      const api = this.grid()?.api;
      if (api && !api.isDestroyed()) api.forEachNode(node => node.setSelected(node.data?.id === id));
    });
  }

  selectIr(irSeq: number): void {
    cancelAnimationFrame(this.frame);
    this.store.loadIr(irSeq);
  }

  onGridReady(event: GridReadyEvent<ProfileString>): void {
    event.api.forEachNode(node => node.setSelected(node.data?.id === this.store.selectedId()));
  }

  onCellKeyDown(event: CellKeyDownEvent<ProfileString> | FullWidthCellKeyDownEvent<ProfileString>): void {
    const key = (event.event as KeyboardEvent | undefined)?.key;
    if (key === 'Enter' || key === ' ') {
      event.event?.preventDefault();
      this.store.select(event.data?.id ?? '');
    }
  }

  explode(): void {
    cancelAnimationFrame(this.frame);
    this.store.toggleExploded();
    const start = this.store.spread(), target = this.store.exploded() ? 1 : 0;
    if (window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) { this.store.setSpread(target); return; }
    const begin = performance.now();
    const animate = (now: number): void => {
      const t = Math.min(1, (now - begin) / 1050);
      const ease = t < 0.5 ? 4*t*t*t : 1 - Math.pow(-2*t+2, 3)/2;
      this.store.setSpread(start + (target-start)*ease);
      if (t < 1) this.frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    };
    this.frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
  }
}
```

```ts
import { ICasingIR } from '@shared/models/wwell/casing-ir.model';
import { ITopsIR } from '@shared/models/wwell/tops-ir.model';

/** Drawing metadata supplements the existing IR fields; no assumed liner overlap. */
export interface ProfileString extends ICasingIR {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly top: number;
  readonly topInferred?: boolean;
}
export type ProfileFormation = Pick<ITopsIR, 'stLongCd' | 'planTvdDepth'> & { readonly bottom: number };
export type ProfileMode = 'solid' | 'transparent' | 'xray' | 'profile';
export const REFERENCE_DEPTH = 3800;
export const REFERENCE_STRINGS: ProfileString[] = [
  { id: 'conductor', csgType: 'Conductor', csgSize: '30', top: 0, csgDepth: 120 },
  { id: 'surface', csgType: 'Surface', csgSize: '24', top: 0, csgDepth: 400 },
  { id: 'intermediate', csgType: 'Intermediate', csgSize: '18', top: 0, csgDepth: 1500 },
  { id: 'production', csgType: 'Production', csgSize: '13', top: 0, csgDepth: 2000 },
  { id: 'liner', csgType: 'Liner', csgSize: '9', top: 2000, csgDepth: 3300 },
];
export const REFERENCE_FORMATIONS: ProfileFormation[] = [
  { stLongCd: 'Above DMM', planTvdDepth: 0, bottom: 120 },
  { stLongCd: 'DMM', planTvdDepth: 120, bottom: 480 },
  { stLongCd: 'RUS', planTvdDepth: 480, bottom: 760 },
  { stLongCd: 'UMER', planTvdDepth: 760, bottom: 1450 },
  { stLongCd: 'ARUM', planTvdDepth: 1450, bottom: 2150 },
  { stLongCd: 'WASI', planTvdDepth: 2150, bottom: 3800 },
];

import { IntegratedRequirement } from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';
import { parseInchSize } from '@shared/utils/wellbore-math.util';
import { deriveCompletionElementFlags } from '../wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-wellbore-diagram.mapper';

/** Uses the existing IR schematic convention for missing TOL, explicitly marked as inferred. */
export function buildProfile(ir: IntegratedRequirement | null) {
  const warnings: string[] = [];
  const strings: ProfileString[] = [];
  let chainTop = Math.max(0, ...(ir?.casing ?? []).filter(row => !/liner/i.test(row.type) && Number.isFinite(row.depthFtBgl)).map(row => row.depthFtBgl));
  const ordered = (ir?.casing ?? []).map((row,index) => ({row,index})).sort((a,b) => a.row.depthFtBgl-b.row.depthFtBgl);
  for (const {index, row} of ordered) {
    const liner = /liner/i.test(row.type);
    const topInferred = liner && row.tolFtBgl === null;
    const top = liner ? row.tolFtBgl ?? (chainTop > 0 ? chainTop : null) : 0;
    const diameter = parseInchSize(row.sizeIn);
    if (top == null || !Number.isFinite(top) || top < 0 || !Number.isFinite(row.depthFtBgl) || row.depthFtBgl <= top || !diameter || diameter <= 0) {
      warnings.push(`${row.sizeIn}″ ${row.type}: incomplete or invalid diameter/depth${liner ? ' / liner top' : ''}; omitted from diagram.`);
      continue;
    }
    if (topInferred) warnings.push(`${row.sizeIn}″ liner: attached schematically at ${top} ft, the preceding shoe; actual TOL is not recorded.`);
    if (liner) chainTop = row.depthFtBgl;
    strings.push({ topInferred, id: `string-${index}`, csgType: row.type, csgSize: row.sizeIn, csgDepth: row.depthFtBgl, csgRemarks: row.remarks, top });
  }
  strings.sort((a, b) => (parseInchSize(b.csgSize) ?? 0) - (parseInchSize(a.csgSize) ?? 0));
  const target = ir?.requestReference.targetDepthFt ?? 0;
  const totalDepth = Math.max(Number.isFinite(target) ? target : 0, ...strings.map(s => s.csgDepth), 0);
  const tops = (ir?.formationTops ?? []).filter(f => Number.isFinite(f.depthFt) && f.depthFt >= 0 && f.depthFt < totalDepth).slice().sort((a,b) => a.depthFt-b.depthFt);
  const formations: ProfileFormation[] = tops.map((f,i) => ({ stLongCd: f.name, planTvdDepth: f.depthFt, bottom: tops[i+1]?.depthFt ?? totalDepth }));
  if (tops[0]?.depthFt > 0) formations.unshift({ stLongCd: 'Above '+tops[0].name, planTvdDepth: 0, bottom: tops[0].depthFt });
  const validLevel = (n: number | null | undefined): number | null => n != null && Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0 && n <= totalDepth ? n : null;
  const pump = ir?.testProgram.testType === 'pump-test' ? validLevel(ir.hydrogeology.estimatedPumpDepthFt) : null;
  const water = validLevel(ir?.testProgram.estimatedWaterLevelFt);
  const flags = ir ? ir.programDetails.wellDesignFlags ?? deriveCompletionElementFlags(ir.programDetails.completion, ir.programDetails.openHoleSizeIn) : null;
  // The IR stores completion choices, not separate screen/gravel intervals. Only open-hole
  // below the deepest structural casing has a defined extent; don't invent screen lengths.
  const shoe = Math.max(0, ...strings.filter(s => !/liner/i.test(s.csgType)).map(s => s.csgDepth));
  const openHole = flags?.ohFlg === 'Y' && shoe > 0 && totalDepth > shoe ? { top: shoe, bottom: totalDepth } : null;
  return { strings, formations, totalDepth, pump, water, openHole, warnings,
    completion: ir?.programDetails.completion || 'Not specified',
    completionNote: flags?.gpFlg === 'Y' || flags?.lsFlg === 'Y' || flags?.perforatedFlg === 'Y' ? 'Screen / gravel / perforation intervals are not specified in this IR; shown in completion details only.' : null,
  };
}
```

## [A] src/app/features/well-profile/well-profile.geometry.ts

```ts
import { parseInchSize } from '@shared/utils/wellbore-math.util';
import { ProfileFormation, ProfileString, REFERENCE_DEPTH } from './well-profile.data';

export const depthY = (depth: number, totalDepth = REFERENCE_DEPTH): number => 60 + depth / Math.max(1, totalDepth) * 554;
export const intervalX = (depth: number, totalDepth = REFERENCE_DEPTH): number => 140 + depth / Math.max(1, totalDepth) * 580;
export function stringsAtDepth(strings: readonly ProfileString[], depth: number): ProfileString[] {
  return strings.filter(s => depth >= s.top && depth < s.csgDepth);
}
export function formationAtDepth(formations: readonly ProfileFormation[], depth: number): ProfileFormation | undefined {
  return formations.find(f => depth >= f.planTvdDepth && depth < f.bottom) ?? formations.at(-1);
}
/** Same scale for every OD; explosion changes only x, never the depth coordinates. */
export function projectStrings(strings: readonly ProfileString[], spread: number, totalDepth = REFERENCE_DEPTH) {
  const maxOd = Math.max(1, ...strings.map(s => parseInchSize(s.csgSize) ?? 0));
  const sum = strings.reduce((n,s) => n + (parseInchSize(s.csgSize) ?? 0) / maxOd, 0);
  const explodedRadius = Math.min(57, (440 - Math.max(0, strings.length-1)*12) / Math.max(1, 2*sum));
  let cursor = 186;
  return strings.map(s => {
    const radius = (parseInchSize(s.csgSize) ?? 0) / maxOd * (57 + (Math.max(3, explodedRadius)-57)*spread);
    const x = 380 + (cursor + radius - 380) * spread;
    cursor += radius * 2 + 12;
    const topY = depthY(s.top, totalDepth), bottomY = depthY(s.csgDepth, totalDepth), ry = Math.min(12, radius * 0.23);
    return { ...s, x, radius, ry, topY, bottomY,
      path: `M${x-radius},${topY} A${radius},${ry} 0 0 0 ${x+radius},${topY} L${x+radius},${bottomY} A${radius},${ry} 0 0 1 ${x-radius},${bottomY} Z`,
    };
  });
}
```

## [A] src/app/features/well-profile/well-profile.spec.ts

```ts
import { of } from 'rxjs';
import { WwellIrService } from '../wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir.service';
import { TestBed } from '@angular/core/testing';
import { WellProfileStore } from './well-profile.store';
import { projectStrings, stringsAtDepth, formationAtDepth } from './well-profile.geometry';
import { REFERENCE_STRINGS, REFERENCE_FORMATIONS } from './well-profile.data';

describe('Well Profile construction relationships', () => {
  it('keeps conductor outside surface casing and liner at production shoe in every spread', () => {
    for (const spread of [0, 0.5, 1]) {
      const strings = projectStrings(REFERENCE_STRINGS, spread);
      expect(strings[0].radius / strings[1].radius).toBeCloseTo(30 / 24);
      expect(strings.slice(0, 4).map(s => s.topY)).toEqual([60, 60, 60, 60]);
      expect(strings[4].topY).toBe(strings[3].bottomY);
      expect(strings[0].bottomY).toBeCloseTo(77.4947, 3);
    }
  });
  it('reports all overlapping strings and moves into the liner at the shoe boundary', () => {
    expect(stringsAtDepth(REFERENCE_STRINGS, 5).map(s => s.csgSize)).toEqual(['30', '24', '18', '13']);
    expect(stringsAtDepth(REFERENCE_STRINGS, 2000).map(s => s.csgSize)).toEqual(['9']);
    expect(stringsAtDepth(REFERENCE_STRINGS, 3300)).toEqual([]);
    expect(formationAtDepth(REFERENCE_FORMATIONS, 2150)?.stLongCd).toBe('WASI');
  });
});

describe('WellProfileStore', () => {
  it('clamps depth and preserves valid selection when asked for an unknown string', () => {
    TestBed.configureTestingModule({ providers: [WellProfileStore, { provide: WwellIrService, useValue: { getIrs: () => of([]) } }] });
    const store = TestBed.inject(WellProfileStore);
    store.setDepth(9000);
    expect(store.depth()).toBe(0);
    store.select('unknown');
    expect(store.selected()).toBeNull();
    store.setDepth(2500);
    expect(store.strings()).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('switches profile to 3D on explode and uses a translucent default', () => {
    TestBed.configureTestingModule({ providers: [WellProfileStore, { provide: WwellIrService, useValue: { getIrs: () => of([]) } }] });
    const store = TestBed.inject(WellProfileStore);
    store.setMode('profile');
    store.toggleExploded();
    expect(store.mode()).toBe('solid');
    expect(store.exploded()).toBe(true);
    expect(store.shellOpacity()).toBe(0.45);
    store.toggleExploded();
    expect(store.exploded()).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

## [A] src/app/features/well-profile/well-profile.store.ts

```ts
import { computed, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { patchState, signalStore, withComputed, withMethods, withState } from '@ngrx/signals';
import { rxMethod } from '@ngrx/signals/rxjs-interop';
import { tapResponse } from '@ngrx/operators';
import { EMPTY, pipe, switchMap, tap } from 'rxjs';
import { IntegratedRequirement } from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';
import { WwellIrService } from '../wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir.service';
import { mergeIrDetailBlocksIntoIntegratedRequirement } from '../wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-detail-response.mapper';
import { ProfileMode, buildProfile } from './well-profile.data';
import { formationAtDepth, stringsAtDepth } from './well-profile.geometry';

export const WellProfileStore = signalStore(
  withState({ mode: 'transparent' as ProfileMode, depth: 0, selectedId: '', opacity: 25, exploded: false, spread: 0,
    casingFill: '#b8bec6', linerFill: '#969fa9', irs: [] as IntegratedRequirement[], record: null as IntegratedRequirement | null,
    irSeq: null as number | null, listLoading: false, detailLoading: false, listError: '', detailError: '' }),
  withComputed(s => ({ profile: computed(() => buildProfile(s.record())) })),
  withComputed(s => ({
    strings: computed(() => s.profile().strings), formations: computed(() => s.profile().formations), totalDepth: computed(() => s.profile().totalDepth),
    selected: computed(() => s.profile().strings.find(d => d.id === s.selectedId()) ?? null),
    shellOpacity: computed(() => s.mode() === 'solid' ? .45 : s.mode() === 'xray' ? .04 : s.opacity()/100),
    reading: computed(() => {
      const p = s.profile(), active = stringsAtDepth(p.strings, s.depth()), formation = formationAtDepth(p.formations, s.depth());
      return `${formation?.stLongCd ?? 'Formation not specified'} · ${active.length ? active.map(d => `${d.csgSize}″ ${d.csgType.toLowerCase()}`).join(' + ') : s.depth() === p.totalDepth ? 'Total depth' : p.openHole && s.depth() >= p.openHole.top ? 'Open hole' : 'No casing specified at this depth'}`;
    }),
    coverage: computed(() => {
      const selected = s.profile().strings.find(d => d.id === s.selectedId());
      return selected ? s.profile().formations.map(f => ({ name: f.stLongCd,
        length: Math.max(0, Math.min(selected.csgDepth, f.bottom)-Math.max(selected.top, f.planTvdDepth)), total: selected.csgDepth-selected.top,
      })).filter(f => f.length > 0) : [];
    }),
  })),
  withMethods((s, service = inject(WwellIrService)) => ({
    loadIrs: rxMethod<void>(pipe(tap(() => patchState(s, { listLoading: true, listError: '' })), switchMap(() => service.getIrs().pipe(tapResponse({
      next: irs => patchState(s, { irs, listLoading: false }), error: () => patchState(s, { listLoading: false, listError: 'Unable to load integrated requirements. Please retry.' }),
    }))))),
    loadIr: rxMethod<number>(pipe(tap(irSeq => patchState(s, { irSeq, record: null, detailLoading: true, detailError: '', selectedId: '', depth: 0, exploded: false, spread: 0 })), switchMap(irSeq => {
      const summary = s.irs().find(ir => ir.irSeq === irSeq);
      if (!summary) { patchState(s, { detailLoading: false, detailError: 'This IR is not available in the list.' }); return EMPTY; }
      return service.getIrDetailBlocks(irSeq).pipe(tapResponse({
        next: blocks => {
          const record = mergeIrDetailBlocksIntoIntegratedRequirement(summary, blocks);
          patchState(s, { record, detailLoading: false, selectedId: buildProfile(record).strings[0]?.id ?? '' });
        }, error: () => patchState(s, { detailLoading: false, detailError: 'Unable to load this IR. Please retry.' }),
      }));
    }))),
    setMode(mode: ProfileMode) { patchState(s, { mode }); },
    setOpacity(opacity: number) { patchState(s, { opacity: Math.max(10, Math.min(70, opacity)) }); },
    setDepth(depth: number) { if (Number.isFinite(depth)) patchState(s, { depth: Math.max(0, Math.min(s.totalDepth(), depth)) }); },
    select(selectedId: string) { if (s.strings().some(d => d.id === selectedId)) patchState(s, { selectedId }); },
    setFill(kind: 'casingFill' | 'linerFill', value: string) { if (/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(value)) patchState(s, { [kind]: value }); },
    toggleExploded() { patchState(s, { exploded: !s.exploded(), mode: s.mode() === 'profile' ? 'solid' : s.mode() }); },
    setSpread(spread: number) { patchState(s, { spread: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, spread)) }); },
  })),
);
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/shared/_wwell-material.scss

```scss
@use '@angular/material' as mat;
@use './wwell-design-tokens' as tokens;

// Material token overrides for the module's look ("Aurora Glass"). Two mixins:
//   buttons     — an aurora-gradient primary action, hairline outlined secondary, sky text buttons,
//                 muted icon buttons
//   form-fields — hairline control outlines that step to muted on hover and cobalt on focus
//
// The app only calls `mat.core()` globally (no full M3 theme), so unthemed Material buttons fall
// back to a transparent fill and a pure-black label — invisible on a dark surface. Include this at a
// component's root: custom properties inherit down the DOM, so every button inside (child
// components, dialog content) follows without restating it. It reads only `--wp-*` tokens, so it
// flips with the theme.
//
//   .my-root { @include wmat.buttons; }
@mixin buttons {
  @include mat.button-overrides(
    (
      filled-container-color: tokens.$cta,
      filled-label-text-color: tokens.$on-cta,
      filled-ripple-color: tokens.alpha(tokens.$on-cta, 18%),
      filled-disabled-container-color: tokens.alpha(tokens.$navy-deep, 12%),
      filled-disabled-label-text-color: tokens.alpha(tokens.$navy-deep, 38%),
      filled-container-shape: tokens.$radius-btn,
      filled-label-text-weight: 600,
      outlined-label-text-color: tokens.$navy-deep,
      outlined-outline-color: tokens.$border-strong,
      outlined-ripple-color: tokens.alpha(tokens.$accent, 18%),
      outlined-disabled-label-text-color: tokens.alpha(tokens.$navy-deep, 38%),
      outlined-disabled-outline-color: tokens.$border,
      outlined-container-shape: tokens.$radius-btn,
      outlined-label-text-weight: 600,
      text-label-text-color: tokens.$accent,
      text-ripple-color: tokens.alpha(tokens.$accent, 18%),
      text-disabled-label-text-color: tokens.alpha(tokens.$navy-deep, 38%),
      text-container-shape: tokens.$radius-btn,
      text-label-text-weight: 600,
    )
  );

  @include mat.icon-button-overrides(
    (
      icon-color: tokens.$muted,
      disabled-icon-color: tokens.alpha(tokens.$navy-deep, 38%),
    )
  );

  // Material's tokens only take a colour, and the primary action is a gradient. The gradient look is written once, in
  // styles.scss (`.mat-mdc-unelevated-button`), reading these; a page that does not include this mixin leaves them unset,
  // and every one of those declarations then falls back to Material's own value — so other pages are untouched.
  --wp-btn-fill: #{tokens.$cta-bg};
  --wp-btn-fill-hover: #{tokens.$cta-bg-hover};
  --wp-btn-fill-shadow: #{tokens.$cta-shadow};
  --wp-btn-fill-shadow-hover: #{tokens.$cta-shadow-hover};
  --wp-btn-fill-lift: translateY(-1px);
  --wp-btn-fill-press: translateY(0) scale(0.975);
  --wp-btn-fill-transition:
    transform #{tokens.$duration-fast} #{tokens.$ease-out},
    box-shadow #{tokens.$duration-base} #{tokens.$ease-out};
}

// Outlined form fields. Outline colour is the 3:1 `--wp-line-control` (a field has to read as a
// field), stepping to muted on hover and cobalt on focus; errors use the danger token. Without this,
// Material's default outline is a bright grey that fights the module's hairline language in dark.
@mixin form-fields {
  @include mat.form-field-overrides(
    (
      outlined-container-shape: tokens.$radius-md,
      outlined-outline-color: tokens.$border-control,
      outlined-hover-outline-color: tokens.$muted,
      outlined-focus-outline-color: tokens.$accent,
      outlined-disabled-outline-color: tokens.$border,
      outlined-error-outline-color: tokens.$danger,
      outlined-error-hover-outline-color: tokens.$danger,
      outlined-error-focus-outline-color: tokens.$danger,
      outlined-label-text-color: tokens.$muted,
      outlined-hover-label-text-color: tokens.$navy-deep,
      outlined-focus-label-text-color: tokens.$accent,
      outlined-error-label-text-color: tokens.$danger,
      outlined-error-hover-label-text-color: tokens.$danger,
      outlined-error-focus-label-text-color: tokens.$danger,
      outlined-disabled-label-text-color: tokens.$muted,
      outlined-input-text-color: tokens.$navy-deep,
      outlined-input-text-placeholder-color: tokens.$subtle,
      outlined-disabled-input-text-color: tokens.$navy-deep,
      outlined-caret-color: tokens.$accent,
      outlined-error-caret-color: tokens.$danger,
      error-text-color: tokens.$danger,
      leading-icon-color: tokens.$muted,
      trailing-icon-color: tokens.$muted,
      enabled-select-arrow-color: tokens.$muted,
      focus-select-arrow-color: tokens.$accent,
      disabled-select-arrow-color: tokens.alpha(tokens.$navy-deep, 38%),
    )
  );

  // Material has no token for hint text, and unresolved it falls back to black — unreadable on dark.
  // `::ng-deep` because the hint belongs to Material's own template, outside this component's scope.
  ::ng-deep .mat-mdc-form-field-hint {
    color: tokens.$muted;
  }
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/shared/_wwell-theme.scss

```scss
// wwell-planning theme — the single source of truth for every `--wp-*` custom property.
//
// Global on purpose (`@use`d once from src/styles.scss): component-scoped SCSS can't target `:root`,
// and dialogs / select panels / tooltips render on <body>, outside the module's own DOM, so the
// theme has to reach them by inheritance from <html>. Everything is `--wp-` prefixed, so no other
// feature is affected — none of them read these tokens.
//
// Light is the default (no attribute). `WwellThemeService` writes `data-wp-theme` on <html>.
// Direction: "Aurora Glass" — deep navy / cool paper grounds lit by soft aurora colour, frosted glass panels, hairline
// rings, and one loud thing: the sky-to-cobalt gradient of the primary action. Sky/cobalt is also the signal colour
// (focus, links, selection).

// Both palettes ride a contrast dial. `WwellThemeService` writes `--wp-contrast-dark` and
// `--wp-contrast-light` (each 0–100%) on <html>, and a dialed token is
//   color-mix(in srgb, HIGH <dial>, SOFT)
// so 100% is its High value and 0% its Soft one.
//   Dark:  High is the original dark palette; Soft lifts the page/surface steps to a lighter charcoal
//          and eases the text tiers down, so the whole scale compresses. Default 50%.
//   Light: High is the standard light palette (the default, 100%); Soft dims the whites to a cool
//          grey to cut glare, and darkens the fixed-colour text (accent, semantic, wellbore labels)
//          to keep pace with the darker grounds.
// Every text tier stays ≥ 4.5:1 across each range and no fixed-colour pairing gets worse than it is
// at High (scripted at 0/25/50/75/100%, then swept live). The fallbacks match DEFAULT_CONTRAST_* in
// the service.
@function dark-tone($high, $soft) {
  @return color-mix(in srgb, #{$high} var(--wp-contrast-dark, 50%), #{$soft});
}

// `$dialed: false` (the `.wp-paper` scope) hands back the High value untouched, so paper never
// follows the dial.
@function light-tone($high, $soft, $dialed) {
  @if not $dialed {
    @return $high;
  }
  @return color-mix(in srgb, #{$high} var(--wp-contrast-light, 100%), #{$soft});
}

// The two token sets are mixins so a subtree can re-declare either one regardless of the page
// theme — see `.wp-paper` below.
@mixin light-tokens($dialed: true) {
  --wp-scheme: light;

  // Surfaces
  --wp-page: #{light-tone(#e8eef8, #d5deee, $dialed)};
  --wp-surface: #{light-tone(#fbfcff, #e9eef8, $dialed)};
  --wp-surface-alt: #{light-tone(#f3f6fc, #dfe6f2, $dialed)}; // inset panels, table heads, read-only fields
  --wp-surface-hover: #{light-tone(#ebf1fb, #d6dfee, $dialed)};
  --wp-surface-raised: #{light-tone(#ffffff, #f1f5fb, $dialed)}; // dialogs, popovers (separated by shadow in light)
  --wp-surface-sunken: #{light-tone(#e3eaf6, #cdd7e9, $dialed)}; // tracks, skeleton bars, wells

  // Lines
  --wp-line: #{light-tone(#d6deed, #bbc6db, $dialed)};
  --wp-line-soft: #{light-tone(#e2e8f4, #cbd4e6, $dialed)}; // row dividers
  --wp-line-strong: #{light-tone(#bac6dc, #9dabc7, $dialed)}; // hover borders, emphasis dividers
  --wp-line-control: #{light-tone(#7787a6, #5a6a8a, $dialed)}; // input / control outlines — 3:1 so a field reads as a field (WCAG 1.4.11)

  // Text
  --wp-ink: #{light-tone(#0b1730, #22304a, $dialed)}; // headings
  --wp-text: #{light-tone(#22304a, #34435f, $dialed)}; // body
  --wp-muted: #{light-tone(#52617d, #465570, $dialed)}; // secondary
  --wp-subtle: #{light-tone(#586783, #4c5a75, $dialed)}; // tertiary, placeholders, icons
  --wp-on-accent: #ffffff;

  // Accent — cobalt, a signal not a fill
  --wp-accent: #{light-tone(#0b62c4, #0a52a6, $dialed)}; // links, focus, selected text/icons
  --wp-accent-hover: #{light-tone(#0a52a6, #08448a, $dialed)};
  --wp-accent-fill: #{light-tone(#1f55d4, #1a47b3, $dialed)}; // solid backgrounds (selection bars, progress)
  --wp-accent-soft: rgba(11, 98, 196, 0.08);
  --wp-accent-line: rgba(11, 98, 196, 0.3);

  // Primary action. The flat value is the gradient's own mid colour, so the Material fill underneath and the
  // few places that cannot take a gradient stay the same blue as the button itself.
  --wp-cta: #{light-tone(#205bbd, #1b4c9c, $dialed)};
  --wp-cta-hover: #{light-tone(#2664c6, #1d54ad, $dialed)};
  --wp-on-cta: #ffffff;
  --wp-cta-shadow: 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.4) inset, 0 1px 2px rgba(20, 50, 160, 0.4), 0 12px 24px -10px rgba(32, 91, 189, 0.6);

  // Semantic
  --wp-danger: #{light-tone(#cc271b, #ad2016, $dialed)};
  --wp-danger-ink: #{light-tone(#b42318, #97190f, $dialed)};
  --wp-danger-soft: rgba(217, 45, 32, 0.06);
  --wp-danger-line: rgba(217, 45, 32, 0.3);
  --wp-success: #{light-tone(#15803d, #116a32, $dialed)};
  --wp-success-soft: rgba(21, 128, 61, 0.07);
  --wp-success-line: rgba(21, 128, 61, 0.3);
  --wp-warning: #{light-tone(#b45309, #8f4106, $dialed)};
  --wp-warning-soft: rgba(217, 119, 6, 0.08);
  --wp-warning-line: rgba(180, 83, 9, 0.32);

  // Status pills (bg / ink / dot) — mirrors wwell-status.config.ts
  --wp-st-slate-bg: #{light-tone(#f1f5f9, #d9dee6, $dialed)};
  --wp-st-slate-ink: #3f4b5e;
  --wp-st-slate-dot: #7a869a;
  --wp-st-slate-muted-bg: #{light-tone(#f1f5f9, #d9dee6, $dialed)};
  --wp-st-slate-muted-ink: #566073;
  --wp-st-slate-muted-dot: #a0aabb;
  --wp-st-indigo-bg: #e0e7ff;
  --wp-st-indigo-ink: #4338ca;
  --wp-st-indigo-dot: #6366f1;
  --wp-st-purple-bg: #f3e8ff;
  --wp-st-purple-ink: #7e22ce;
  --wp-st-purple-dot: #a855f7;
  --wp-st-blue-bg: #dbeafe;
  --wp-st-blue-ink: #1d4ed8;
  --wp-st-blue-dot: #3b82f6;
  --wp-st-green-bg: #dcfce7;
  --wp-st-green-ink: #15803d;
  --wp-st-green-dot: #16a34a;
  --wp-st-amber-bg: #fef3c7;
  --wp-st-amber-ink: #b45309;
  --wp-st-amber-dot: #d97706;
  --wp-st-red-bg: #fee2e2;
  --wp-st-red-ink: #b91c1c;
  --wp-st-red-dot: #ef4444;

  // Depth — hairline + soft shadow in light
  --wp-shadow-1: 0 1px 2px rgba(20, 40, 90, 0.07);
  --wp-shadow-2: 0 1px 2px rgba(20, 40, 90, 0.06), 0 8px 22px rgba(20, 40, 90, 0.1);
  --wp-shadow-3: 
    0 1px 2px rgba(20, 40, 90, 0.08), 0 14px 34px rgba(20, 40, 90, 0.16),
    0 34px 68px rgba(20, 40, 90, 0.12);
  --wp-scrim: rgba(8, 16, 36, 0.45);

  // Frosted glass: a translucent fill, the light rim a pane catches along its top edge, the sheen across it,
  // and the soft shadow it throws. Overlays, not palette colours, so the contrast dial leaves them alone.
  --wp-glass-fill: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.62);
  --wp-glass-rim: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.95);
  --wp-glass-sheen: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.7);
  --wp-glass-shadow: 0 12px 30px -16px rgba(20, 40, 90, 0.32);

  // Chips over the page (tooltips)
  --wp-tooltip-bg: #{light-tone(#0b1730, #22304a, $dialed)};
  --wp-tooltip-ink: #ffffff;
  --wp-tooltip-ring: rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.22);

  // Loading shimmers
  --wp-shimmer-a: #{light-tone(#e3eaf6, #cdd7e9, $dialed)};
  --wp-shimmer-b: #{light-tone(#f3f6fc, #dfe6f2, $dialed)};

  // Wellbore diagram — domain colours keep their hue; luminance shifts per theme so labels and lines
  // read on the surface behind them. Text-bearing ones (ground/target/actual/water) meet 4.5:1.
  --wp-wb-outline: #{light-tone(#0b1730, #22304a, $dialed)}; // casing outlines, axes, ticks, arrow heads
  --wp-wb-ground: #{light-tone(#b94a12, #9b3d0e, $dialed)};
  --wp-wb-target: #{light-tone(#b45309, #8f4106, $dialed)};
  --wp-wb-actual: #{light-tone(#15803d, #116a32, $dialed)};
  --wp-wb-water: #{light-tone(#0369a1, #025a88, $dialed)};
  --wp-wb-pad: #{light-tone(#f0f0f0, #d3d8e0, $dialed)};

  // Well profile ("Strata") — the formation bands are one ordinal ramp, shallow → deep: a sandstone hue, every step
  // ≥0.06 lighter/darker than its neighbour, the first ≥2:1 against the card. `-ink` is the label colour on each step
  // (≥4.5:1). The target aquifer is the one band that is not rock: emerald, a muted fill under a bright top edge.
  // Fixed colours, like the wellbore's domain colours: the contrast dial does not move them.
  --wp-profile-rock-1: #cbab88;
  --wp-profile-rock-2: #b7946f;
  --wp-profile-rock-3: #a27e58;
  --wp-profile-rock-4: #8d6a43;
  --wp-profile-rock-ink-1: #04060a;
  --wp-profile-rock-ink-2: #04060a;
  --wp-profile-rock-ink-3: #04060a;
  --wp-profile-rock-ink-4: #ffffff;
  --wp-profile-aquifer: #8fd7b0;
  --wp-profile-aquifer-edge: #007f4e;
  --wp-profile-aquifer-ink: #04060a;
  // The wash a water selection lays over a band: deep, so a band's label keeps its contrast under it.
  --wp-profile-water-wash: #0369a1;
  --wp-profile-on-water: #ffffff;
  // How much of a band a selection tints: faint, so the label on it keeps its contrast (measured ≥4.5:1 in every state).
  --wp-profile-select-wash: 10%;

  // Primary action: one blue, lit from the top. It was a 135° sweep across three stops from sky to cobalt to
  // indigo — a lot of hue travel for a button ~200px wide, which read as a gradient rather than as a surface.
  // A single hue shading down the vertical instead still gives the key its dimensionality, without the rainbow.
  --wp-cta-bg: linear-gradient(180deg, #2668c6, #1a4eb4);
  --wp-cta-bg-hover: linear-gradient(180deg, #2d71cf, #1f57bd);
  --wp-cta-shadow-hover: 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.5) inset, 0 1px 2px rgba(20, 50, 160, 0.4), 0 18px 32px -10px rgba(32, 91, 189, 0.75);

  // Atmosphere: three soft pools of colour the glass sits on (painted once, by the app shell), and how much grain rides them.
  --wp-atmosphere:
    radial-gradient(58% 48% at 12% -6%, rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.34), transparent 62%),
    radial-gradient(50% 44% at 98% 4%, rgba(99, 124, 255, 0.26), transparent 64%),
    radial-gradient(60% 55% at 56% 120%, rgba(45, 212, 191, 0.24), transparent 62%);
  --wp-grain-opacity: 0.07;

  // A glass panel: a spotlight that leans in from the top right, over a frosted fill, ringed by a hairline.
  --wp-panel-bg:
    radial-gradient(360px circle at 84% -10%, rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.2), transparent 62%),
    linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.88), rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.66));
  --wp-panel-ring: rgba(20, 40, 90, 0.1);
  --wp-panel-rim: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.95);
}

@mixin dark-tokens {
  --wp-scheme: dark;

  // Surfaces — depth comes from lighter steps, not drop shadows.
  //
  // The dial runs between two ends chosen so it reads the way an editor theme does: at High (100%) every value
  // is a pure neutral — R, G and B are equal, so there is no hue in the ground at all and the page is true
  // black. At Soft (0%) the same ramp lifts to a little slate, a blue-grey of only 11–17/255 saturation: enough
  // to warm a dark room, not enough to read as a colour. Default 50% sits between them.
  --wp-page: #{dark-tone(#000000, #1b1f27)};
  --wp-surface: #{dark-tone(#0f0f0f, #262b35)};
  --wp-surface-alt: #{dark-tone(#090909, #20242d)};
  --wp-surface-hover: #{dark-tone(#1d1d1d, #333944)};
  --wp-surface-raised: #{dark-tone(#151515, #2b313b)};
  --wp-surface-sunken: #{dark-tone(#040404, #161a21)};

  --wp-line: #{dark-tone(#242424, #353b46)};
  --wp-line-soft: #{dark-tone(#171717, #272c35)};
  --wp-line-strong: #{dark-tone(#383838, #4a5160)};
  --wp-line-control: #{dark-tone(#707070, #7e8797)};

  --wp-ink: #{dark-tone(#ffffff, #e4e6ea)};
  --wp-text: #{dark-tone(#d6d6d6, #c2c6cd)};
  --wp-muted: #{dark-tone(#a2a2a2, #a8adb6)};
  --wp-subtle: #{dark-tone(#8e8e8e, #a4aab4)};
  --wp-on-accent: #ffffff;

  --wp-accent: #7dd3fc;
  --wp-accent-hover: #a5e3fe;
  --wp-accent-fill: #2f6bea;
  --wp-accent-soft: rgba(125, 211, 252, 0.12);
  --wp-accent-line: rgba(125, 211, 252, 0.34);

  // Flat value is the gradient's mid colour; the glow follows the fill rather than staying cyan.
  --wp-cta: #3ea4ec;
  --wp-cta-hover: #54b3f4;
  --wp-on-cta: #03131f;
  --wp-cta-shadow: 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.55) inset, 0 0 0 1px rgba(77, 184, 247, 0.25), 0 10px 26px -8px rgba(77, 184, 247, 0.55);

  --wp-danger: #f87171;
  --wp-danger-ink: #fca5a5;
  --wp-danger-soft: rgba(248, 113, 113, 0.13);
  --wp-danger-line: rgba(248, 113, 113, 0.34);
  --wp-success: #4ade80;
  --wp-success-soft: rgba(74, 222, 128, 0.12);
  --wp-success-line: rgba(74, 222, 128, 0.32);
  --wp-warning: #fbbf24;
  --wp-warning-soft: rgba(251, 191, 36, 0.12);
  --wp-warning-line: rgba(251, 191, 36, 0.34);

  // Slate is the "no particular status" pill — its colour carries no meaning, so it is a neutral grey here
  // rather than a blue-grey. Every other pill keeps its hue, because those hues ARE the meaning.
  --wp-st-slate-bg: rgba(168, 168, 168, 0.16);
  --wp-st-slate-ink: #c9c9c9;
  --wp-st-slate-dot: #8a8a8a;
  --wp-st-slate-muted-bg: rgba(168, 168, 168, 0.11);
  --wp-st-slate-muted-ink: #a8a8a8;
  --wp-st-slate-muted-dot: #737373;
  --wp-st-indigo-bg: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.18);
  --wp-st-indigo-ink: #b4bdff;
  --wp-st-indigo-dot: #818cf8;
  --wp-st-purple-bg: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.18);
  --wp-st-purple-ink: #dcbcff;
  --wp-st-purple-dot: #c084fc;
  --wp-st-blue-bg: rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.18);
  --wp-st-blue-ink: #a5cbff;
  --wp-st-blue-dot: #60a5fa;
  --wp-st-green-bg: rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.15);
  --wp-st-green-ink: #8ef0b0;
  --wp-st-green-dot: #4ade80;
  --wp-st-amber-bg: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.16);
  --wp-st-amber-ink: #fdd670;
  --wp-st-amber-dot: #fbbf24;
  --wp-st-red-bg: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.17);
  --wp-st-red-ink: #fdb0b0;
  --wp-st-red-dot: #f87171;

  --wp-shadow-1: inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04);
  --wp-shadow-2: 0 0 0 1px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05), 0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45);
  --wp-shadow-3: 0 0 0 1px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.07), 0 24px 64px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7);
  --wp-scrim: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.62);

  --wp-glass-fill: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
  --wp-glass-rim: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.18);
  --wp-glass-sheen: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.14);
  --wp-glass-shadow: 0 12px 30px -16px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8);

  // Inverse chip: a dark tooltip would vanish on a dark surface
  --wp-tooltip-bg: #{dark-tone(#ffffff, #e4e6ea)};
  --wp-tooltip-ink: #060b14;
  --wp-tooltip-ring: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12);

  --wp-shimmer-a: #{dark-tone(#141414, #2a2f39)};
  --wp-shimmer-b: #{dark-tone(#1f1f1f, #363c47)};

  --wp-wb-outline: #{dark-tone(#b4b4b4, #a9aeb7)};
  --wp-wb-ground: #f0894a;
  --wp-wb-target: #fbbf24;
  --wp-wb-actual: #4ade80;
  --wp-wb-water: #38bdf8;
  --wp-wb-pad: #{dark-tone(#1f1f1f, #363c47)};

  --wp-profile-rock-1: #5b4226;
  --wp-profile-rock-2: #725433;
  --wp-profile-rock-3: #896742;
  --wp-profile-rock-4: #a27e58;
  --wp-profile-rock-ink-1: #ffffff;
  --wp-profile-rock-ink-2: #ffffff;
  --wp-profile-rock-ink-3: #ffffff;
  --wp-profile-rock-ink-4: #04060a;
  --wp-profile-aquifer: #0c5537;
  --wp-profile-aquifer-edge: #4ad496;
  --wp-profile-aquifer-ink: #ffffff;
  --wp-profile-water-wash: #0b6fa6;
  --wp-profile-on-water: #04141d;
  --wp-profile-select-wash: 14%;

  // One blue, lit from the top — see the light set for why the three-stop cyan-to-indigo sweep went.
  --wp-cta-bg: linear-gradient(180deg, #4db8f7, #2f8fe0);
  --wp-cta-bg-hover: linear-gradient(180deg, #68c6fa, #3f9fee);
  --wp-cta-shadow-hover: 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.65) inset, 0 0 0 1px rgba(104, 198, 250, 0.5), 0 16px 34px -8px rgba(77, 184, 247, 0.72);

  // No aurora in dark. Light frosts its glass over three soft pools of colour; here the ground is meant to be
  // black, and a wash of cyan and indigo across it is exactly the hue this theme is not supposed to have.
  // The grain stays, at a whisper: it is greyscale, so it adds no colour, and it keeps a large flat dark field
  // from banding on an 8-bit panel at the slate end of the dial.
  --wp-atmosphere: none;
  --wp-grain-opacity: 0.025;

  // A panel is a neutral film over the ground, held by a hairline. The spotlight it used to catch was a cyan
  // radial, which tinted every pane on the page.
  --wp-panel-bg: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.055), rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.025));
  --wp-panel-ring: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.09);
  --wp-panel-rim: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.14);
}

:root {
  // ── Theme-independent ─────────────────────────────────────────
  --wp-radius-sm: 8px; // small controls, chips
  --wp-radius-md: 12px; // cards, panels, inputs
  --wp-radius-lg: 18px; // dialogs, popovers, the big glass panes
  --wp-radius-btn: 12px; // buttons
  --wp-radius-pill: 999px;

  // A glass pane's edge and blur, once for both themes: the hairline ring and top rim come from the theme's `--wp-panel-*`.
  --wp-panel-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--wp-panel-ring), inset 0 1px 0 var(--wp-panel-rim), var(--wp-shadow-2);
  --wp-panel-blur: blur(22px) saturate(150%);

  // The faces, for the places that cannot `@use` the design tokens (a component outside the module, the wellbore canvas chips).
  --wp-font-ui: 'Geist Variable', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, 'Helvetica Neue', Arial, sans-serif;
  --wp-font-display: 'Outfit Variable', 'Geist Variable', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif;
  --wp-font-mono: 'Geist Mono Variable', ui-monospace, 'SFMono-Regular', Menlo, Consolas, monospace;

  // Solid fills that carry white text/glyphs — same in both themes so the contrast holds either way.
  --wp-success-fill: #15803d;
  --wp-danger-fill: #d92d20;

  // Well profile: steel stays metallic in both themes, like the pipe the diagram draws. The size stamped on it is a fixed
  // dark ink — a themed (near-white in dark) one would vanish into the steel.
  --wp-profile-steel-a: #c3cbd8;
  --wp-profile-steel-b: #8f9aad;
  --wp-profile-liner-a: #dde3ec;
  --wp-profile-liner-b: #a4aebe;
  --wp-profile-on-steel: #0a0d14;

  @include light-tokens;
}

:root[data-wp-theme='dark'] {
  @include dark-tokens;
}

// IR uses the same opaque black/charcoal surfaces as Well Profile. Keep the
// shared contrast dial and light palette; avoid stacking translucent glass.
:root[data-wp-theme='dark'] :is(app-wwell-ir, app-wwell-ir-editor, app-wwell-ir-view) {
  --wp-page-bg: var(--wp-page);
  --wp-panel-bg: var(--wp-surface);
  --wp-panel-blur: none;
  --wp-panel-ring: var(--wp-line);
  --wp-panel-rim: var(--wp-line-soft);
  --wp-panel-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--wp-panel-ring);
}

// A printed page. The IR letter previews the PDF the backend generates, and that PDF is always white
// paper — so this scope re-declares the LIGHT set on its subtree, whatever the app theme is. It also
// keeps the wellbore-diagram PNG that ships inside the PDF correct: that snapshot inlines each SVG
// element's computed colours onto a white canvas, so a dark-themed diagram would export as
// light-on-white (unreadable) if it were not pinned to light here.
.wp-paper {
  @include light-tokens($dialed: false);
  color-scheme: light;
}

// ── Switching between the themes ─────────────────────────────────────────────
//
// A plain cross-fade — the browser's own default view transition, deliberately not customised. This is what
// most products do for a theme swap (many do nothing at all), and it is the one thing here that needs no
// explaining to anyone reading it later.
//
// There are no keyframes below because there is nothing to write: `startViewTransition()` snapshots the page
// before and after the attribute flips and cross-fades the two by itself. Because it is a snapshot, everything
// crosses together — gradients, backdrop-filters, the wellbore canvas — which no property-by-property CSS
// transition can manage, `background-image` not being animatable.
//
// The one rule that IS needed: ArcGIS's stylesheet, which angular.json loads BEFORE this one, declares
// `:root { view-transition-name: none }`, and an author declaration beats the UA's own `root` name. Without
// this the root is captured nameless, there are no `::view-transition-*(root)` pseudo-elements for the browser
// to fade, and the theme snaps. It is restored only while the class is on — the service adds it before calling
// `startViewTransition()`, so the name is there when the first snapshot is taken — and ArcGIS keeps its opt-out
// everywhere else.
:root.wp-theme-crossfade {
  view-transition-name: root;
}

// ── Fallback ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Browsers without view transitions (Firefox before 144, Safari before 18) get the nearest equivalent a
// stylesheet can give: the same colours moving over the same time. Held to colour-only properties, so nothing
// reflows, and to a short duration, because `*` means every element on screen repaints on every frame of it.
//
// Its own class, not shared with the one above: where view transitions DO exist this must not run, or the
// second snapshot would be taken mid-fade and the transition would cross onto a half-finished picture.
:root.wp-theme-switching,
:root.wp-theme-switching *,
:root.wp-theme-switching *::before,
:root.wp-theme-switching *::after {
  transition:
    background-color var(--wp-theme-ms, 250ms) ease,
    background-image var(--wp-theme-ms, 250ms) ease,
    color var(--wp-theme-ms, 250ms) ease,
    border-color var(--wp-theme-ms, 250ms) ease,
    outline-color var(--wp-theme-ms, 250ms) ease,
    fill var(--wp-theme-ms, 250ms) ease,
    stroke var(--wp-theme-ms, 250ms) ease !important;
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  :root.wp-theme-switching,
  :root.wp-theme-switching *,
  :root.wp-theme-switching *::before,
  :root.wp-theme-switching *::after {
    transition: none !important;
  }
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/shared/wwell-approvals/wwell-approval-timeline/wwell-approval-timeline.component.scss

```scss
@use '../../wwell-design-tokens' as tokens;

:host {
  display: block;
  font-family: tokens.$font-family;
}

.approval-timeline {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.9rem;
}

.approval-timeline__header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  font-size: 0.82rem;
  font-weight: 650;
  color: tokens.$navy-deep;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.05em;
}

// A request code like REQ-00252 must not break at its hyphen when the header wraps in the narrow column.
.approval-timeline__req {
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.approval-timeline__header-icon {
  display: inline-flex;
  color: tokens.$accent;
}

.approval-timeline__rail {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0.35rem 0 0;
  list-style: none;
}

.approval-timeline__item {
  position: relative;
  flex: 1;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  min-width: 0;

  &[data-status='approved'] {
    .approval-timeline__track::after {
      background: var(--wp-success-fill);
    }

    .approval-timeline__node {
      background: var(--wp-success-fill);
      border-color: var(--wp-success-fill);
      color: var(--wp-on-accent);
    }
  }

  &[data-status='in-progress'] .approval-timeline__node {
    background: var(--wp-st-blue-bg);
    border-color: tokens.$accent;
  }

  &[data-status='rejected'] .approval-timeline__node {
    background: var(--wp-danger-fill);
    border-color: var(--wp-danger-fill);
    color: var(--wp-on-accent);
  }

  &:last-child .approval-timeline__track::after {
    display: none;
  }
}

.approval-timeline__track {
  position: relative;
  display: flex;
  justify-content: center;
  width: 100%;

  &::after {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    top: 15px;
    left: 50%;
    width: 100%;
    height: 2px;
    background: tokens.$border;
    z-index: 0;
    transition: background 200ms ease;
  }
}

.approval-timeline__node {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1;
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  width: 32px;
  height: 32px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: tokens.$surface-alt;
  border: 2px solid var(--wp-line-strong);
  color: tokens.$subtle;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  transition:
    background 200ms ease,
    border-color 200ms ease;
}

.approval-timeline__node-icon {
  width: 16px;
  height: 16px;
}

.approval-timeline__pulse {
  width: 9px;
  height: 9px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: tokens.$accent;
  animation: approval-timeline-pulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite;
}

@keyframes approval-timeline-pulse {
  0%,
  100% {
    transform: scale(0.85);
    opacity: 0.7;
  }
  50% {
    transform: scale(1.15);
    opacity: 1;
  }
}

.approval-timeline__card {
  margin-top: 0.65rem;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 4px;
  text-align: center;
  padding: 0 6px;
  max-width: 160px;
}

.approval-timeline__role {
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  font-weight: 650;
  color: tokens.$muted;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.04em;
}

.approval-timeline__name {
  font-size: 0.92rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: tokens.$navy-deep;
  line-height: 1.25;
}

.approval-timeline__badge {
  display: inline-flex;
  padding: 3px 10px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.approval-timeline__date {
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  color: tokens.$subtle;
}

/*
 * Stacked layout — shared by the explicit `orientation="vertical"` mode (e.g. the IR detail
 * sidebar) and the automatic narrow-viewport fallback for the default horizontal mode
 * (WLDA / Land-Affairs approval-table rows).
 */
@media (max-width: 720px) {
  .approval-timeline__rail {
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 0;
  }

  .approval-timeline__item {
    flex-direction: row;
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 0.85rem;
    padding-bottom: 1.25rem;

    &:last-child {
      padding-bottom: 0;
    }
  }

  .approval-timeline__track {
    width: auto;

    &::after {
      top: 32px;
      left: 15px;
      width: 2px;
      height: calc(100% - 16px);
    }
  }

  .approval-timeline__card {
    margin-top: 0;
    align-items: flex-start;
    text-align: left;
    max-width: none;
    padding: 2px 0 0;
  }
}

/*
 * Skinny rail: this is the variant used in the IR detail sidebar, a fixed ~168px column, so
 * every metric here is deliberately smaller than the horizontal/default variant (WLDA /
 * Land-Affairs approval-table) to read as a compact status rail instead of a bento card.
 */
:host(.approval-timeline--vertical) {
  .approval-timeline {
    gap: 0.55rem;
  }

  .approval-timeline__header {
    font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
    gap: 4px;
  }

  .approval-timeline__rail {
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 0;
    padding-top: 0.2rem;
  }

  .approval-timeline__item {
    flex-direction: row;
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 0.5rem;
    padding-bottom: 0.9rem;

    &:last-child {
      padding-bottom: 0;
    }
  }

  .approval-timeline__track {
    width: auto;

    &::after {
      top: 20px;
      left: 8px;
      width: 2px;
      height: calc(100% - 6px);
    }
  }

  .approval-timeline__node {
    width: 18px;
    height: 18px;
  }

  .approval-timeline__node-icon {
    width: 10px;
    height: 10px;
  }

  .approval-timeline__pulse {
    width: 6px;
    height: 6px;
  }

  .approval-timeline__card {
    margin-top: 0;
    align-items: flex-start;
    text-align: left;
    max-width: none;
    gap: 2px;
    padding: 0;
    min-width: 0;
  }

  // A host can raise the type through `--wtl-fs` / `--wtl-name-fs` (the IR view's sidebar does); left unset, the rail keeps
  // its compact sizes for the approval tables.
  .approval-timeline__role {
    font-size: var(--wtl-fs, #{tokens.$text-2xs});
  }

  .approval-timeline__name {
    font-size: var(--wtl-name-fs, #{tokens.$text-2xs});
    line-height: 1.2;
    overflow-wrap: break-word;
  }

  .approval-timeline__badge {
    padding: 1px 7px;
    font-size: var(--wtl-fs, #{tokens.$text-2xs});
  }

  .approval-timeline__date {
    font-size: var(--wtl-fs, #{tokens.$text-2xs});
  }
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/shared/wwell-design-tokens.scss

```scss
// Shared design tokens for the wwell-planning module (list/detail pages, approval
// timeline, request/approval lists, dialogs). Single source of truth so every
// component's local `$navy`/`$muted`/`$border`/`$accent` block references the same
// values instead of re-declaring its own copy — keeps typography and color changes
// module-wide instead of a per-file hunt.
//
// Every color here is a `var(--wp-*)` reference, NOT a literal. The actual light/dark values
// live in `_wwell-theme.scss` (global, `@use`d from styles.scss) and flip with the
// `data-wp-theme` attribute on <html>. The Sass names are kept so existing component styles
// become theme-aware without being rewritten. Because these are runtime CSS variables, Sass
// colour functions (`rgba($accent, .1)`, `darken()`, `color.adjust()`) cannot operate on them —
// use `alpha()` below, or add a dedicated token.

// ── Typography ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Geist / Geist Mono / Outfit are self-hosted via @fontsource-variable (loaded in styles.scss); the OS UI
// stack is the fallback while the file loads or if it is blocked. Scoped to this module only — the
// shell keeps its own font.
$font-family:
  'Geist Variable',
  -apple-system,
  BlinkMacSystemFont,
  'Segoe UI',
  Roboto,
  'Helvetica Neue',
  Arial,
  sans-serif;

// Titles, the brand, big numbers: Outfit, a geometric display face with a little more personality than the UI face.
$font-display:
  'Outfit Variable',
  'Geist Variable',
  -apple-system,
  BlinkMacSystemFont,
  'Segoe UI',
  Roboto,
  sans-serif;

// Identifiers, coordinates, depths, timestamps — anything the reader compares character by character.
$font-mono:
  'Geist Mono Variable',
  ui-monospace,
  'SFMono-Regular',
  Menlo,
  Consolas,
  monospace;

// ── Color (theme-aware) ──────────────────────────────────────────
$navy: var(--wp-text); // primary body text
$navy-deep: var(--wp-ink); // headings — one step stronger than body for hierarchy
$muted: var(--wp-muted); // secondary text
$subtle: var(--wp-subtle); // tertiary text / icons
$border: var(--wp-line);
$border-soft: var(--wp-line-soft);
$border-strong: var(--wp-line-strong);
$border-control: var(--wp-line-control); // input / control outlines

$accent: var(--wp-accent); // links, focus, selected text/icons (a signal, not a fill)
$accent-deep: var(--wp-accent-hover); // hover/active states
$accent-soft: var(--wp-accent-soft);
$accent-fill: var(--wp-accent-fill); // solid backgrounds: selection bars, progress, filled chips
$on-accent: var(--wp-on-accent); // text on $accent-fill / danger fills

$cta: var(--wp-cta); // primary action, flat: the gradient's mid colour, for the odd place that cannot take a gradient
$cta-hover: var(--wp-cta-hover);
$on-cta: var(--wp-on-cta);
$cta-bg: var(--wp-cta-bg); // primary action: the aurora gradient — a `background`, not a colour
$cta-bg-hover: var(--wp-cta-bg-hover);
$cta-shadow: var(--wp-cta-shadow);
$cta-shadow-hover: var(--wp-cta-shadow-hover);

$surface: var(--wp-surface);
$surface-alt: var(--wp-surface-alt);
$surface-hover: var(--wp-surface-hover);
$surface-raised: var(--wp-surface-raised);
$surface-sunken: var(--wp-surface-sunken);
$page-bg: var(--wp-page-bg); // a `background`: transparent over the shell's atmosphere, the page colour elsewhere

$danger: var(--wp-danger);
$danger-ink: var(--wp-danger-ink);
$danger-soft: var(--wp-danger-soft);
$success: var(--wp-success);
$success-soft: var(--wp-success-soft);
$success-fill: var(--wp-success-fill); // solid green behind white text/glyphs
$danger-fill: var(--wp-danger-fill); // solid red behind white text/glyphs
$warning: var(--wp-warning);
$warning-soft: var(--wp-warning-soft);

// ── Shape & depth ────────────────────────────────────────────────
$radius-sm: var(--wp-radius-sm); // small controls, chips
$radius-md: var(--wp-radius-md); // cards, panels, inputs
$radius-btn: var(--wp-radius-btn); // buttons
$radius-lg: var(--wp-radius-lg); // dialogs, popovers, the big glass panes
$radius-pill: var(--wp-radius-pill); // count badges, capsule bars
$shadow-1: var(--wp-shadow-1);
$shadow-2: var(--wp-shadow-2);
$shadow-3: var(--wp-shadow-3);

// Translucent tint of a theme colour — the runtime replacement for `rgba($color, $a)`.
//   background: tokens.alpha($accent, 12%);
@function alpha($color, $percent) {
  @return color-mix(in srgb, #{$color} $percent, transparent);
}

// ── Typography scale ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// Hierarchy comes from weight, tracking and line-height (see the role mixins below), not from
// shrinking the type: nothing on these screens is set under 12px, which is the floor for a label
// that has to be read at a glance on a dense tool. Anything that used to be 0.62–0.74rem
// (9.9–11.8px) now takes `$text-2xs`.
$text-2xs: 0.75rem; // 12px — badges, eyebrows, table heads
$text-xs: 0.8125rem; // 13px — meta text, captions
$text-sm: 0.875rem; // 14px — secondary body text
$text-base: 0.9375rem; // 15px — primary body text, list-row names
$text-md: 1.05rem; // card/section sub-headings
$text-lg: 1.3rem; // detail-panel titles
$text-xl: 1.55rem; // page titles

// Type roles. Use these for the recurring roles so weight/tracking/leading stay consistent
// module-wide; one-off sizes can stay as plain declarations.
@mixin type-title {
  font-family: $font-display;
  font-size: $text-xl;
  font-weight: 500;
  letter-spacing: -0.03em;
  line-height: 1.15;
  color: $navy-deep;
}

@mixin type-panel-title {
  font-family: $font-display;
  font-size: $text-lg;
  font-weight: 500;
  letter-spacing: -0.024em;
  line-height: 1.2;
  color: $navy-deep;
}

@mixin type-section {
  font-size: $text-md;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
  line-height: 1.3;
  color: $navy-deep;
}

@mixin type-eyebrow {
  font-size: $text-2xs;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  line-height: 1.2;
  color: $muted;
}

@mixin type-label {
  font-size: $text-xs;
  font-weight: 500;
  line-height: 1.3;
  color: $muted;
}

@mixin type-value {
  font-size: $text-base;
  font-weight: 500;
  line-height: 1.4;
  color: $navy;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

@mixin type-mono {
  font-family: $font-mono;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
}

// A frosted pane: the spotlit fill and hairline ring of `--wp-panel-*`, over whatever is behind it (the shell's aurora),
// blurred. Put it on a page's big panels; small controls keep flat surfaces so they stay legible on top of one.
@mixin glass-panel {
  background: var(--wp-panel-bg);
  box-shadow: var(--wp-panel-shadow);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter: var(--wp-panel-blur);
  backdrop-filter: var(--wp-panel-blur);
}

// The read-only view's card: a glass pane with room to breathe, headed by an uppercase display-face title.
@mixin view-card {
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  border-radius: $radius-lg;
  padding: 1.15rem 1.4rem;
  @include glass-panel;
}

@mixin view-card-title {
  font-family: $font-display;
  font-size: $text-base;
  font-weight: 650;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
  line-height: 1.25;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: $accent;
}

// Keyboard focus: 2px cobalt ring separated from the control by a surface-coloured gap, so it
// reads on both light and dark surfaces.
@mixin focus-ring {
  outline: none;
  box-shadow:
    0 0 0 2px $surface,
    0 0 0 4px $accent;
}

// ── Motion ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
$ease-out: cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
$duration-fast: 160ms;
$duration-base: 240ms;
$duration-slow: 340ms;
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/shared/wwell-theme-toggle/wwell-theme-toggle.component.scss

```scss
@use '../wwell-design-tokens' as tokens;

// The control lives in the shell's top bar, so its two buttons are drawn from the shell tokens (one
// look per shell, scoped like the bar itself: `data-shell` on <html>). The popover further down is a
// planning surface and keeps the `--wp-*` tokens.
:host {
  display: inline-flex;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  --mat-icon-button-icon-size: 18px;
}

.wtt-group {
  display: inline-flex;
}

.wtt {
  position: relative;
  transition:
    background-color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out,
    border-color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out,
    color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out;

  &:focus-visible {
    z-index: 2;
  }
}

// ── Mono shell: a joined pair of square buttons under one hairline ───────────
:host-context([data-shell='mono']) {
  --mat-icon-button-state-layer-size: 34px;

  .wtt-group {
    overflow: hidden;
    border: 1px solid var(--shell-hair-strong);
    border-radius: 5px;
  }

  .wtt {
    --mat-icon-button-icon-color: var(--shell-muted);
    border-radius: 0;

    &:hover,
    &.wtt--open {
      background: var(--shell-press);
      --mat-icon-button-icon-color: var(--shell-text);
    }

    &:focus-visible {
      outline: 2px solid var(--shell-text);
      outline-offset: -2px;
    }
  }

  .wtt--tune {
    border-left: 1px solid var(--shell-hair-strong);
  }
}

// ── Legacy shell: the same two buttons as the old navy bar's bell ────────────
:host-context([data-shell='legacy']) {
  --mat-icon-button-state-layer-size: 40px;

  .wtt-group {
    gap: 8px;
  }

  .wtt {
    --mat-icon-button-icon-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.55);
    border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
    border-radius: 10px;
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);

    &:hover,
    &.wtt--open {
      border-color: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.4);
      background: rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.14);
      box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.2);
      --mat-icon-button-icon-color: #38bdf8;
    }

    &:focus-visible {
      outline: 2px solid #38bdf8;
      outline-offset: 2px;
    }
  }
}

// Both glyphs are stacked and cross-rotate with transform + opacity only (compositor-friendly).
// Light theme shows the moon (pressing goes dark); dark theme shows the sun.
// `.wtt .wtt__icon` (not `.wtt__icon`): Material sets its own position/size on `.mat-icon`, which would
// otherwise win and let the second glyph wrap out of the button.
.wtt .wtt__icon {
  position: absolute;
  top: 50%;
  left: 50%;
  width: 18px;
  height: 18px;
  margin: -9px 0 0 -9px;
  font-size: 18px;
  line-height: 18px;
  transition:
    transform tokens.$duration-base tokens.$ease-out,
    opacity tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out;
}

.wtt .wtt__icon--sun {
  opacity: 0;
  transform: rotate(-90deg) scale(0.5);
}

.wtt--dark {
  .wtt__icon--sun {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: none;
  }

  .wtt__icon--moon {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: rotate(90deg) scale(0.5);
  }
}

// ── Appearance popover ───────────────────────────────────────────
// Rendered in the CDK overlay on <body>, but it is this component's own template, so these scoped
// rules still apply; the `--wp-*` tokens arrive by inheritance from <html>.
.wtt-pop {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 16px;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  width: 272px;
  padding: 16px;
  border: 1px solid tokens.$border-strong;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-lg;
  background: tokens.$surface-raised;
  box-shadow: tokens.$shadow-3;
  color: tokens.$navy;
  font-family: tokens.$font-family;
  transform-origin: top right;
  animation: wtt-pop-in tokens.$duration-base tokens.$ease-out;
}

@keyframes wtt-pop-in {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(-4px) scale(0.98);
  }
}

.wtt-pop__title {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: tokens.$text-sm;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
  color: tokens.$navy-deep;
}

.wtt-field {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 8px;
  min-width: 0;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  border: 0;
}

.wtt-field__label {
  padding: 0;
  @include tokens.type-eyebrow;
}

// A fieldset's legend is not a flex item, so the column's `gap` doesn't reach it.
legend.wtt-field__label {
  margin-bottom: 8px;
}

// The label wraps the slider so the whole row is one click target; the head text and the range
// share a two-column grid: name left, value right, slider full width beneath.
.wtt-field__head {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr auto;
  align-items: center;
  row-gap: 8px;

  .wtt-range {
    grid-column: 1 / -1;
  }
}

.wtt-field__value {
  @include tokens.type-mono;
  font-size: tokens.$text-xs;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: tokens.$navy-deep;
}

// Segmented System/Light/Dark control. The native radios stay (arrow-key roving, form semantics)
// and are stretched invisibly over their face, so the whole segment is the hit target.
.wtt-seg {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  gap: 2px;
  padding: 2px;
  border: 1px solid tokens.$border;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-md;
  background: tokens.$surface-sunken;
}

.wtt-seg__opt {
  position: relative;
  display: block;
}

.wtt-seg__input {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  margin: 0;
  opacity: 0;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.wtt-seg__face {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 5px;
  height: 30px;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  border-radius: calc(#{tokens.$radius-md} - 2px);
  font-size: tokens.$text-xs;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: tokens.$muted;
  transition:
    background-color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out,
    border-color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out,
    color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out;

  mat-icon {
    width: 15px;
    height: 15px;
    font-size: 15px;
    line-height: 15px;
  }
}

.wtt-seg__opt:hover .wtt-seg__face {
  color: tokens.$navy-deep;
}

.wtt-seg__input:checked + .wtt-seg__face {
  border-color: tokens.$border-strong;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  box-shadow: tokens.$shadow-1;
  color: tokens.$navy-deep;
}

.wtt-seg__input:focus-visible + .wtt-seg__face {
  @include tokens.focus-ring;
}

// Native range, restyled. The filled stretch stops at the thumb's centre, which travels from half a
// thumb in to half a thumb short of the end — hence the calc rather than a bare percentage.
.wtt-range {
  --wtt-thumb: 16px;
  --wtt-stop: calc(var(--wtt-thumb) / 2 + (100% - var(--wtt-thumb)) * var(--wtt-p, 0.5));

  appearance: none;
  -webkit-appearance: none;
  width: 100%;
  height: 20px;
  margin: 0;
  background: transparent;
  cursor: pointer;

  &::-webkit-slider-runnable-track {
    height: 4px;
    border-radius: tokens.$radius-sm;
    background: linear-gradient(to right, tokens.$accent var(--wtt-stop), tokens.$border-strong var(--wtt-stop));
  }

  &::-moz-range-track {
    height: 4px;
    border-radius: tokens.$radius-sm;
    background: linear-gradient(to right, tokens.$accent var(--wtt-stop), tokens.$border-strong var(--wtt-stop));
  }

  &::-webkit-slider-thumb {
    -webkit-appearance: none;
    box-sizing: border-box;
    width: var(--wtt-thumb);
    height: var(--wtt-thumb);
    margin-top: -6px; // (track 4px − thumb 16px) / 2
    border: 2px solid tokens.$surface-raised;
    border-radius: 50%;
    background: tokens.$navy-deep;
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px tokens.$border-control;
    transition: transform tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out;
  }

  &::-moz-range-thumb {
    box-sizing: border-box;
    width: var(--wtt-thumb);
    height: var(--wtt-thumb);
    border: 2px solid tokens.$surface-raised;
    border-radius: 50%;
    background: tokens.$navy-deep;
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px tokens.$border-control;
    transition: transform tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out;
  }

  &:hover::-webkit-slider-thumb,
  &:active::-webkit-slider-thumb {
    transform: scale(1.12);
  }

  &:hover::-moz-range-thumb,
  &:active::-moz-range-thumb {
    transform: scale(1.12);
  }

  &:focus-visible {
    outline: none;
  }

  &:focus-visible::-webkit-slider-thumb {
    box-shadow:
      0 0 0 1px tokens.$border-control,
      0 0 0 3px tokens.$surface-raised,
      0 0 0 5px tokens.$accent;
  }

  &:focus-visible::-moz-range-thumb {
    box-shadow:
      0 0 0 1px tokens.$border-control,
      0 0 0 3px tokens.$surface-raised,
      0 0 0 5px tokens.$accent;
  }
}

.wtt-range__ends {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  @include tokens.type-label;
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
}

.wtt-foot {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 12px;
  padding-top: 12px;
  border-top: 1px solid tokens.$border; // $border-soft vanishes against the raised surface
}

.wtt-foot__hint {
  margin: 0;
  @include tokens.type-label;
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  line-height: 1.4;
}

.wtt-reset {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  padding: 4px 8px;
  border: 1px solid tokens.$border;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-sm;
  background: transparent;
  font: inherit;
  font-size: tokens.$text-xs;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: tokens.$navy-deep;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition:
    background-color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out,
    border-color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out;

  &:hover:not(:disabled) {
    border-color: tokens.$border-strong;
    background: tokens.$surface-hover;
  }

  &:focus-visible {
    @include tokens.focus-ring;
  }

  &:disabled {
    cursor: default;
    opacity: 0.45;
  }
}

// ── Legacy layout switch ─────────────────────────────────────────
// Swaps the redesigned top bar and left nav for the old navy ones; light/dark is untouched.
.wtt-legacy {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
  padding-top: 12px;
  border-top: 1px solid tokens.$border;
}

.wtt-legacy__text {
  display: flex;
  flex: 1;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 2px;
  min-width: 0;
}

.wtt-legacy__title {
  font-size: tokens.$text-sm;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: tokens.$navy-deep;
}

.wtt-legacy__hint {
  @include tokens.type-label;
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  line-height: 1.4;
}

.wtt-switch {
  position: relative;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  width: 38px;
  height: 22px;
  padding: 0;
  border: 1px solid tokens.$border-control;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-pill;
  background: tokens.$surface-sunken;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition:
    background-color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out,
    border-color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out;

  &:focus-visible {
    @include tokens.focus-ring;
  }

  &[aria-checked='true'] {
    border-color: tokens.$cta;
    background: tokens.$cta-bg;

    .wtt-switch__thumb {
      background: tokens.$on-cta;
      transform: translateX(16px);
    }
  }
}

.wtt-switch__thumb {
  position: absolute;
  top: 3px;
  left: 3px;
  width: 14px;
  height: 14px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: tokens.$muted;
  transition:
    transform tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out,
    background-color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out;
}

// Vendor thumb pseudo-elements get their own rules: one unknown selector in a list drops the whole rule.
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .wtt .wtt__icon,
  .wtt-pop,
  .wtt-switch__thumb {
    transition: none;
    animation: none;
  }

  .wtt-range::-webkit-slider-thumb {
    transition: none;
  }

  .wtt-range::-moz-range-thumb {
    transition: none;
  }
}
```
