```ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, Signal, computed, inject, input, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { MatButtonModule } from '@angular/material/button';
import { MatIconModule } from '@angular/material/icon';
import { MatProgressSpinnerModule } from '@angular/material/progress-spinner';
import { WellboreDiagramData } from '@models/well-design/wellbore-diagram.model';
import { IntegratedRequirement, IntegratedRequirementStatus } from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';
import { WwellIrStore } from '../../store/wwell-ir.store';
import { buildHydrogeologyRows } from '../../wwell-ir-editor/wwell-ir-detail-rows.util';
import { WWELL_IR_EXPANDED_DIAGRAM_LAYOUT } from '../../wwell-ir-editor/wwell-ir-preview-diagram-layout';
import { mapIntegratedRequirementToRawFormValue } from '../../wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-edit.mapper';
import { mapIrFormValueToSubmission } from '../../wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-form.mapper';
import { buildWwellIrBulkSavePayload } from '../../wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-payload.mapper';
import {
  buildWellboreDiagramDataFromIntegratedRequirement,
  wellboreDiagramDataEqual,
  hasWellboreDiagramDataForIntegratedRequirement,
} from '../../wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-wellbore-diagram.mapper';
import { WwellIrWellborePanelComponent } from '../../wwell-ir-wellbore-panel/wwell-ir-wellbore-panel.component';
import { WwellIrViewKvCardComponent } from '../wwell-ir-view-kv-card/wwell-ir-view-kv-card.component';

/** "Well Design" step of the read-only IR view: the same submitted-record wellbore diagram
 *  WwellIrEditorComponent's own "Wellbore Preview" card renders (via
 *  buildWellboreDiagramDataFromIntegratedRequirement — no live FormArray override here, this
 *  page never edits), plus read-only Formation Tops / Casing tables and an Estimated Hydrology
 *  card (the same KvRow builder the Details step's card also uses — the record only has one set
 *  of hydrogeology values, shown in both places per the "Well Design" step's own spec).
 *
 *  The one exception is the Formation Tops remarks note: it gets its own lightweight inline edit
 *  (pencil → textarea → Save/Cancel) rather than requiring "Edit" up top to switch the whole panel
 *  into WwellIrEditorComponent just to touch one field — mirrors how WwellIrViewDetailsComponent's
 *  "Request Approval" button already calls the store directly from view mode. Persists through the
 *  exact same mapIntegratedRequirementToRawFormValue → mapIrFormValueToSubmission →
 *  buildWwellIrBulkSavePayload → store.createIr pipeline WwellIrEditorComponent's own
 *  saveMergedRaw uses, so this reaches the backend identically (same ir_seq threading, same
 *  existing-record diff for every other section) — see wwell-ir-edit.mapper.ts's own doc comment
 *  on mapIntegratedRequirementToRawFormValue for why it exists precisely for this kind of reuse.
 *  This page is only ever reachable for an already-persisted record (see WwellIrComponent's
 *  `viewIr`, gated on a real `irSeq`), so `current.irSeq` below is never null. */
@Component({
  selector: 'app-wwell-ir-view-well-design',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MatButtonModule, MatIconModule, MatProgressSpinnerModule, WwellIrWellborePanelComponent, WwellIrViewKvCardComponent],
  templateUrl: './wwell-ir-view-well-design.component.html',
  styleUrl: './wwell-ir-view-well-design.component.scss',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class WwellIrViewWellDesignComponent {
  readonly ir = input.required<IntegratedRequirement>();

  private readonly store = inject(WwellIrStore);

  // The maximised view's layout (its leader labels and casing remarks all have room), drawn inside a panel that fills
  // the visible page — see `fit` on the panel and `.ir-well-design__view` in the stylesheet.
  protected readonly diagramLayout = WWELL_IR_EXPANDED_DIAGRAM_LAYOUT;

  /** Structural `equal` for the same reason as the editor's: the store hands this component a fresh
   *  `IntegratedRequirement` whenever the record is re-fetched (a status refresh, say), and without it
   *  every one of those re-ran `well-bore-view`'s redraw effect and replayed the whole animation even
   *  though nothing the diagram draws had moved. */
  protected readonly wellboreDiagramData: Signal<WellboreDiagramData> = computed(
    () => buildWellboreDiagramDataFromIntegratedRequirement(this.ir()),
    { equal: wellboreDiagramDataEqual },
  );
  protected readonly hasWellboreData: Signal<boolean> = computed(() => hasWellboreDiagramDataForIntegratedRequirement(this.ir()));
  // Hides the D3-rendered "TOTAL DEPTH: 0 FT" label — same gate the editor's own preview uses
  // (wwell-ir-editor.component.ts's `hasTargetDepth`) — for the rare submitted record whose
  // target depth never actually got filled in.
  protected readonly hasTargetDepth: Signal<boolean> = computed(() => (this.ir().requestReference.targetDepthFt ?? 0) > 0);
  protected readonly hydrogeologyRows = computed(() => buildHydrogeologyRows(this.ir()));

  /** Depths read as figures, not raw numbers: same thousands separators the panel's own tables use. */
  protected formatDepth(depthFt: number): string {
    return depthFt.toLocaleString('en-US');
  }

  /** Surface elevation is a decimal (605.2 ft asl), so the subtraction carries binary-float noise
   *  (485.20000000000005); one decimal is all the precision the figure has. */
  protected subSeaFt(depthFt: number): string | null {
    const elevation = this.ir().requestReference.surfaceElevationFtAsl;
    if (elevation === null) return null;
    return (elevation - depthFt).toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 1, maximumFractionDigits: 1 });
  }

  // ── Inline edit — Formation Tops remarks only, see this component's own doc comment. ─────────
  protected readonly editingRemarks = signal(false);
  protected readonly remarksDraft = signal('');
  protected readonly savingRemarks = this.store.creatingIr;

  protected openRemarksEdit(): void {
    this.remarksDraft.set(this.ir().topsRemarks ?? '');
    this.editingRemarks.set(true);
  }

  protected cancelRemarksEdit(): void {
    this.editingRemarks.set(false);
  }

  protected onRemarksInput(event: Event): void {
    this.remarksDraft.set((event.target as HTMLTextAreaElement).value);
  }

  /** Resubmits every other section unchanged (via mapIntegratedRequirementToRawFormValue) with
   *  only the Formation Tops remarks field overridden — same "resend the untouched sections too"
   *  approach WwellIrEditorComponent.saveMergedRaw uses for a single open section. */
  protected saveRemarksEdit(): void {
    const current = this.ir();
    const raw = mapIntegratedRequirementToRawFormValue(current);
    const updatedRaw = {
      ...raw,
      subsurface: { ...raw.subsurface, remarks: { topsRemarks: this.remarksDraft() } },
    };
    const status: Extract<IntegratedRequirementStatus, 'draft' | 'in-progress'> =
      current.status === 'draft' || current.status === 'in-progress' ? current.status : 'in-progress';
    const { payload } = mapIrFormValueToSubmission(updatedRaw, status);
    const irBulkPayload = buildWwellIrBulkSavePayload(updatedRaw, {
      irSeq: current.irSeq ?? null,
      existing: current,
      geologistEmpNumByName: this.store.geologistEmpNumByName(),
    });
    this.store.createIr(payload, irBulkPayload);
    this.editingRemarks.set(false);
  }
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-view/wwell-ir-view.component.scss

```scss
@use '@angular/material' as mat;
@use '../../shared/wwell-design-tokens' as tokens;
@use '../../shared/wwell-material' as wmat;

$navy: tokens.$navy-deep;
$muted: tokens.$muted;
$border: tokens.$border;
$accent: tokens.$accent;

:host {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  height: 100%;
  min-height: 0;
  font-family: tokens.$font-family;
}

// Same rationale as wwell-ir-editor.component.scss's identical block — the app only calls
// mat.core() globally with no full M3 theme, so `mat-stroked-button`/`mat-icon` here need their
// own scoped theme. A plain class (not ViewEncapsulation.None) is enough: `@include mat.theme`
// only sets CSS custom properties, which inherit through emulated-encapsulation boundaries same
// as any other property.
.irv {
  color-scheme: var(--wp-scheme);

  @include mat.theme(
    (
      color: (
        theme-type: color-scheme,
        primary: mat.$blue-palette,
        tertiary: mat.$azure-palette,
      ),
      typography: (
        plain-family: tokens.$font-family,
        brand-family: tokens.$font-family,
      ),
      density: -2,
    )
  );

  @include wmat.buttons;
}

.irv {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  height: 100%;
  min-height: 0;
  gap: var(--wwell-gutter, 0.75rem);
}

/* ── Header: mirrors wwell-ir-editor's `.ire__header` so swapping edit ⇄ view in the same
 * detail-panel slot never jars — same card chrome, same eyebrow/title/badge language. ── */
.irv__header {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 1rem;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-lg;
  padding: 0.7rem 1.1rem;
  @include tokens.glass-panel;
}

.irv__eyebrow {
  margin: 0 0 2px;
  font-size: tokens.$text-xs;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: $accent;
  font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

.irv__title {
  font-family: tokens.$font-display;
  margin: 0;
  font-size: tokens.$text-lg;
  font-weight: 650;
  line-height: 1.2;
  color: $navy;
  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
}

.irv__header-actions {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.6rem;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

/* ── Section-completeness stat: mirrors the editor's Timeline-view completion rule
 * (wwellIrTimelineStepStatus) so this reading never disagrees with what Timeline mode shows. ── */
.irv__progress {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 4px;
  min-width: 148px;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.irv__progress-label {
  font-size: tokens.$text-xs;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: $muted;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.irv__progress-track {
  width: 100%;
  height: 6px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: tokens.$surface-alt;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.irv__progress-fill {
  height: 100%;
  border-radius: inherit;
  background: $accent;
  transition: width 300ms ease;
}

.irv__badge {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  padding: 5px 13px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  font-size: tokens.$text-xs;
  font-weight: 600;
  white-space: nowrap;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.irv__badge-dot {
  width: 7px;
  height: 7px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.irv__edit-btn {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  font-size: tokens.$text-base;
}

/* ── Tabs row — the flat tab bar plus, only while the Letter tab is open, the Download PDF
 * button in the same top-right corner it used to occupy inside the Letter tab's own (scrolling)
 * content. Hoisting it here means it stays in view and in the same spot regardless of scroll
 * position or which tab is active. ── */
.irv__tabs-row {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 0.75rem;
}

.irv__letter-download-wrap {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  display: inline-flex;
}

.irv__letter-download {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  width: 44px;
  height: 44px;
  padding: 0;
  border: 1px solid $border;
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  color: $muted;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition:
    background-color 150ms ease,
    border-color 150ms ease,
    color 150ms ease;

  mat-spinner {
    display: block;
  }

  &:hover:not(:disabled) {
    background: tokens.$surface-alt;
    border-color: $accent;
    color: $accent;
  }

  &:disabled {
    cursor: not-allowed;
    opacity: 0.5;
  }
}

.irv__letter-download-glyph {
  position: relative;
  display: inline-flex;

  mat-icon {
    font-size: 22px;
    width: 22px;
    height: 22px;
  }
}

.irv__letter-download-badge {
  position: absolute;
  right: -5px;
  bottom: -5px;
  font-size: 13px !important;
  width: 13px !important;
  height: 13px !important;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  color: $accent;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1.5px tokens.$surface;
}

/* ── Flat tab bar — Details / Well Design / Letter ─────────────────────────
 * `flex: 1` (not `flex-shrink: 0`) so the pill's own background/border stretches to fill the
 * whole row instead of hugging its three buttons and leaving a bare gap before the download
 * button — the tabs stay left-aligned inside it, but the bar itself now reads as one continuous
 * block the full width of the panel. ── */
.irv__tabs {
  flex: 1;
  display: flex;
  gap: 0.25rem;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-lg;
  padding: 0.35rem;
  @include tokens.glass-panel;
}

.irv__tab {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  padding: 0.55rem 1.1rem;
  border: none;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-sm;
  background: transparent;
  color: $muted;
  font: inherit;
  font-size: tokens.$text-base;
  font-weight: 600;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition:
    background-color 150ms ease,
    box-shadow 150ms ease,
    color 150ms ease;

  mat-icon {
    font-size: 20px;
    width: 20px;
    height: 20px;
  }

  &:hover {
    background: tokens.$surface-alt;
    color: $navy;
  }

  &--active {
    background: tokens.alpha($accent, 12%);
    box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px tokens.alpha($accent, 26%);
    color: $accent;

    &:hover {
      background: tokens.alpha($accent, 14%);
    }
  }
}

.irv__tab-panel {
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;
  overflow-y: auto;
  scrollbar-width: none;
  -ms-overflow-style: none;

  &::-webkit-scrollbar {
    display: none;
  }
}
```

```ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, input, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { ComponentFixture, TestBed } from '@angular/core/testing';
import { NoopAnimationsModule } from '@angular/platform-browser/animations';
import { IntegratedRequirement } from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';
import { NotificationService } from '@shared/components/notification/notification.service';
import { WwellIrService } from '../wwell-ir.service';
import { WwellIrViewComponent, WwellIrViewTab } from './wwell-ir-view.component';
import { WwellIrViewDetailsComponent } from './wwell-ir-view-details/wwell-ir-view-details.component';
import { WwellIrViewLetterComponent } from './wwell-ir-view-letter/wwell-ir-view-letter.component';
import { WwellIrViewWellDesignComponent } from './wwell-ir-view-well-design/wwell-ir-view-well-design.component';

/** The header's `sectionProgress` walks every rail section through wwellIrTimelineStepStatus, so
 *  the whole record has to be present even though this spec only exercises the tab bar. */
function buildIr(overrides: Partial<IntegratedRequirement> = {}): IntegratedRequirement {
  return {
    id: 'IR-9001',
    code: 'IR-9001',
    irSeq: 9001,
    updatedDate: '2026-08-01',
    status: 'draft',
    requestReference: {
      linkedRequestId: null,
      letterNumber: null,
      client: 'BI-12',
      supportingBusiness: 'Agriculture',
      wellName: 'HAIL-500',
      targetAquifer: 'Saq',
      targetDepthFt: 500,
      surfaceElevationFtAsl: 900,
      coordinates: '27.5114°N, 41.7208°E',
    },
    formationTops: [],
    topsRemarks: null,
    casing: [],
    programDetails: {
      completion: 'Open Hole',
      openHoleSizeIn: null,
      acidization: '',
      transmission: '',
      developmentTesting: { logging: '', mudLogging: '', sampling: '', flowTest: '', remarks: '' },
    },
    contacts: { geologistPrimary: 'Eng. A. Al-Mutairi', geologistSecondary: null },
    offsetWells: [],
    offsetWellsRemarks: null,
    hydrogeology: { aquifer: 'Saq', estimatedTdsPpm: null, h2sPpm: null, estimatedPumpDepthFt: null },
    testProgram: { testType: 'flow-test', estimatedRateGpm: null, estimatedWaterLevelFt: null, siwhpPsi: null },
    attachments: [],
    chain: [],
    sapWfId: null,
    sapWfStatusText: null,
    ...overrides,
  } as unknown as IntegratedRequirement;
}

// The three tab bodies are stubbed out: one renders a D3 wellbore diagram and another rasterises
// an SVG, neither of which this spec is about — it only cares which tab the bar has selected.
@Component({ selector: 'app-wwell-ir-view-details', standalone: true, template: 'details-body' })
class DetailsStub {
  readonly ir = input.required<IntegratedRequirement>();
  readonly linkedRequestWellOptions = input<readonly { readonly value: string; readonly label: string }[]>([]);
}

@Component({ selector: 'app-wwell-ir-view-well-design', standalone: true, template: 'well-design-body' })
class WellDesignStub {
  readonly ir = input.required<IntegratedRequirement>();
}

@Component({ selector: 'app-wwell-ir-view-letter', standalone: true, template: 'letter-body' })
class LetterStub {
  readonly ir = input.required<IntegratedRequirement>();
}

/** Stands in for WwellIrComponent, which is where the selected tab actually lives. */
@Component({
  standalone: true,
  imports: [WwellIrViewComponent],
  template: `<app-wwell-ir-view [ir]="ir()" [(activeTab)]="tab" />`,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
class HostComponent {
  readonly ir = signal<IntegratedRequirement>(buildIr());
  readonly tab = signal<WwellIrViewTab>('details');
}

describe('WwellIrViewComponent — tab selection', () => {
  let fixture: ComponentFixture<HostComponent>;

  function tabButton(label: string): HTMLButtonElement {
    const button = Array.from(fixture.nativeElement.querySelectorAll('.irv__tab')).find(
      (element): element is HTMLButtonElement => (element as HTMLElement).textContent?.includes(label) ?? false,
    );
    if (!button) throw new Error(`No tab button labelled "${label}"`);
    return button;
  }

  function activeTabLabel(): string {
    return fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('.irv__tab--active')?.textContent?.trim() ?? '';
  }

  beforeEach(async () => {
    await TestBed.configureTestingModule({
      imports: [HostComponent, NoopAnimationsModule],
      providers: [
        { provide: WwellIrService, useValue: { downloadLetterPdf: jest.fn() } },
        { provide: NotificationService, useValue: { error: jest.fn(), show: jest.fn(), notifications: jest.fn(() => []) } },
      ],
    })
      .overrideComponent(WwellIrViewComponent, {
        remove: { imports: [WwellIrViewDetailsComponent, WwellIrViewWellDesignComponent, WwellIrViewLetterComponent] },
        add: { imports: [DetailsStub, WellDesignStub, LetterStub] },
      })
      .compileComponents();

    fixture = TestBed.createComponent(HostComponent);
    fixture.detectChanges();
  });

  it('opens on Details', () => {
    expect(activeTabLabel()).toContain('Details');
    expect(fixture.nativeElement.textContent).toContain('details-body');
  });

  it('renders the body of whichever tab is selected', () => {
    tabButton('Well Design').click();
    fixture.detectChanges();

    expect(activeTabLabel()).toContain('Well Design');
    expect(fixture.nativeElement.textContent).toContain('well-design-body');
    expect(fixture.nativeElement.textContent).not.toContain('details-body');
  });

  it('writes the selected tab back out to its parent', () => {
    // The parent is the only thing that outlives a row switch, so the selection has to reach it —
    // a tab kept privately on this component would be lost the moment the panel is rebuilt.
    tabButton('Well Design').click();
    fixture.detectChanges();

    expect(fixture.componentInstance.tab()).toBe('well-design');
  });

  it('keeps the selected tab when the panel is destroyed and rebuilt for another record', () => {
    // The regression: picking another IR swaps in a loading skeleton, which tears this component
    // down; the replacement used to start back on Details no matter which tab you had open.
    tabButton('Well Design').click();
    fixture.detectChanges();
    expect(fixture.componentInstance.tab()).toBe('well-design');

    const carriedOver = fixture.componentInstance.tab();
    fixture.destroy();

    const rebuilt = TestBed.createComponent(HostComponent);
    rebuilt.componentInstance.tab.set(carriedOver);
    rebuilt.componentInstance.ir.set(buildIr({ id: 'IR-9002', code: 'IR-9002', irSeq: 9002 }));
    rebuilt.detectChanges();

    const active = rebuilt.nativeElement.querySelector('.irv__tab--active')?.textContent?.trim() ?? '';
    expect(active).toContain('Well Design');
    expect(rebuilt.nativeElement.textContent).toContain('well-design-body');
  });

  it('still lets a different tab be chosen on the newly-opened record', () => {
    tabButton('Well Design').click();
    fixture.detectChanges();

    tabButton('Letter').click();
    fixture.detectChanges();

    expect(fixture.componentInstance.tab()).toBe('letter');
    expect(fixture.nativeElement.textContent).toContain('letter-body');
  });
});
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-view/wwell-ir-view.component.ts

```ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, computed, inject, input, model, output, signal, viewChild } from '@angular/core';
import { MatButtonModule } from '@angular/material/button';
import { MatIconModule } from '@angular/material/icon';
import { MatProgressSpinnerModule } from '@angular/material/progress-spinner';
import { MatTooltipModule } from '@angular/material/tooltip';
import { IntegratedRequirement } from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';
import { NotificationService } from '@shared/components/notification/notification.service';
import { WWELL_IR_SECTION_RAIL } from '../wwell-ir-editor/wwell-ir-section-rail.config';
import { wwellIrTimelineStepStatus } from '../wwell-ir-editor/wwell-ir-timeline.util';
import { WWELL_IR_STATUS_META, hasKnownIrStatus } from '../wwell-ir.config';
import { WwellIrService } from '../wwell-ir.service';
import { WwellIrViewDetailsComponent } from './wwell-ir-view-details/wwell-ir-view-details.component';
import { WwellIrViewLetterComponent } from './wwell-ir-view-letter/wwell-ir-view-letter.component';
import { WwellIrViewWellDesignComponent } from './wwell-ir-view-well-design/wwell-ir-view-well-design.component';

export type WwellIrViewTab = 'details' | 'well-design' | 'letter';

const TABS: readonly { readonly key: WwellIrViewTab; readonly label: string; readonly icon: string }[] = [
  { key: 'details', label: 'Details', icon: 'description' },
  { key: 'well-design', label: 'Well Design', icon: 'architecture' },
  { key: 'letter', label: 'Letter', icon: 'mail_outline' },
];

/**
 * Read-only counterpart to `WwellIrEditorComponent`, swapped into the exact same detail-panel
 * slot in WwellIrComponent's master-detail layout (see that component's `detailMode` signal) —
 * never a separate route. The master list stays visible and clickable the whole time a record is
 * open here, so moving to another IR (or another View) is just another row click, same as edit
 * mode already works.
 *
 * A plain flat tab bar (Details / Well Design / Letter) — not a Material stepper — matches the
 * reference GWD/AGWA "Integrated Requirements" screen this was built to look like. The approval-
 * workflow sidebar lives one level up, in WwellIrComponent's own template — it sits beside this
 * panel (not inside it) so it reads as a property of the whole detail column, not of whichever
 * tab is currently open.
 */
@Component({
  selector: 'app-wwell-ir-view',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [
    MatButtonModule,
    MatIconModule,
    MatProgressSpinnerModule,
    MatTooltipModule,
    WwellIrViewDetailsComponent,
    WwellIrViewWellDesignComponent,
    WwellIrViewLetterComponent,
  ],
  templateUrl: './wwell-ir-view.component.html',
  styleUrl: './wwell-ir-view.component.scss',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class WwellIrViewComponent {
  readonly ir = input.required<IntegratedRequirement>();
  // Resolves `requestReference.linkedRequestId` to a well name — see the Details step's own
  // input of the same name / buildRequestReferenceRows' doc comment.
  readonly linkedRequestWellOptions = input<readonly { readonly value: string; readonly label: string }[]>([]);

  /** WwellIrComponent listens and flips its `detailMode` back to 'edit' for this same record —
   *  this component has no editing surface of its own. */
  readonly editRequested = output<void>();

  protected readonly statusMeta = WWELL_IR_STATUS_META;
  // Same rule the list badge uses (wwell-ir.config.ts) — the sidebar right beside this panel
  // (WwellIrComponent's own template) is what refreshes `sapWfStatusText` from SAP, on selection
  // and via its own "Refresh" button; this badge just mirrors whatever it last resolved.
  protected readonly hasKnownStatus = hasKnownIrStatus;

  protected readonly tabs = TABS;
  /** Owned by WwellIrComponent, not by this component: the whole detail panel is torn down and
   *  rebuilt on every row switch (the loading skeleton replaces it), so a tab held here reset to
   *  'details' each time you picked another record. Same fix, and the same reason, as the editor's
   *  `viewMode` — see the `viewTab` signal this is two-way bound to. */
  readonly activeTab = model<WwellIrViewTab>('details');

  private readonly wwellIrService = inject(WwellIrService);
  private readonly notify = inject(NotificationService);

  /** Only resolves while the `letter` tab is active — the `@switch` in the template below destroys
   *  the child otherwise. Used solely to reach `captureWellboreImage()`, the one piece of the
   *  download this component can't produce itself (rasterizing the diagram SVG the Letter tab
   *  renders). Everything else about the download — state, `ir`, the HTTP call — is handled right
   *  here instead of being driven through this query: an earlier version put `canDownloadPdf` and
   *  `downloadPdf()` on the child and read them from here via this same `viewChild`, which threw
   *  NG0950 ("Input is required but no value is available yet") — reading a query into a
   *  freshly-created child can race Angular's own input-binding for that child's `ir`, since view
   *  queries aren't guaranteed to resolve only after a child's inputs are bound. Reading `this.ir()`
   *  (this component's own long-lived input) instead sidesteps that race entirely. */
  private readonly letterView = viewChild(WwellIrViewLetterComponent);

  protected readonly downloadingPdf = signal(false);
  // Only a persisted record has anything for the backend to render (see IrLetterService, which
  // re-fetches the detail by ir_seq) — a local-only "New IR" draft has never reached the backend.
  protected readonly canDownloadPdf = computed(() => this.ir().irSeq !== null && this.ir().irSeq !== undefined);

  protected async downloadPdf(): Promise<void> {
    const irSeq = this.ir().irSeq;
    if (irSeq === null || irSeq === undefined || this.downloadingPdf()) return;

    this.downloadingPdf.set(true);
    const wellboreImageBase64 = await this.letterView()?.captureWellboreImage();
    this.wwellIrService.downloadLetterPdf(irSeq, wellboreImageBase64).subscribe({
      next: (blob: Blob) => {
        this.downloadingPdf.set(false);
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const link = document.createElement('a');
        link.href = url;
        link.download = `${this.ir().code}-letter.pdf`;
        link.click();
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.downloadingPdf.set(false);
        this.notify.error('Failed to download the integrated requirement letter PDF.');
      },
    });
  }

  /** Header stat: how many of the editor's 7 rail sections (Wellbore, General, Horizons, Casing,
   *  Hydrogeology, Area Water Wells, Attachments) hold meaningful data, per the same
   *  wwellIrTimelineStepStatus completeness rule the editor's Timeline view already uses — so this
   *  view's "N/7 sections" reading always agrees with what Timeline mode shows for the same record. */
  protected readonly sectionProgress = computed(() => {
    const ir = this.ir();
    const total = WWELL_IR_SECTION_RAIL.length;
    const completed = WWELL_IR_SECTION_RAIL.filter(group => wwellIrTimelineStepStatus(ir, group.key) === 'completed').length;
    return { completed, total, percent: Math.round((completed / total) * 100) };
  });

  protected setTab(tab: WwellIrViewTab): void {
    this.activeTab.set(tab);
  }
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-wellbore-panel/wwell-ir-wellbore-facts.util.ts

```ts
import { ICasingIR } from '@shared/models/wwell/casing-ir.model';
import { ITopsIR } from '@shared/models/wwell/tops-ir.model';
import { parseInchSize } from '@shared/utils/wellbore-math.util';

/** Everything the wellbore panel prints around the diagram, derived from the same data the diagram
 *  draws — nothing is estimated or filled in, so a figure appears only when its inputs exist. */
export interface WellboreFacts {
  readonly stats: readonly WellboreStat[];
  readonly casingRows: readonly WellboreCasingRow[];
  readonly topRows: readonly WellboreTopRow[];
}

export interface WellboreStat {
  readonly label: string;
  /** What the inline previews print instead: the same figure in the shorthand a well file uses. */
  readonly short: string;
  readonly value: string;
  /** A qualifier under the value, e.g. which string a shoe belongs to. */
  readonly hint?: string;
  readonly warn?: boolean;
  /** The pictogram the gauge marks its depth with. */
  readonly glyph: WellboreGlyphKind;
  /** How far down the well it is, surface to total depth. Absent until a total depth exists. */
  readonly gauge?: WellboreGauge;
}

export type WellboreGlyphKind = 'water' | 'pump' | 'shoe' | 'depth';

/** A stretch of the well drawn as a filled line: a depth is "how much below ground" (0 → the depth), the water
 *  column is where the water starts → total depth. The glyph sits on the line at `markPct`. */
export interface WellboreGauge {
  readonly fromPct: number;
  readonly toPct: number;
  readonly markPct: number;
}

export interface WellboreCasingRow {
  readonly size: string;
  readonly type: string;
  readonly depth: string;
  /** Set below the target depth: the diagram runs off its scale, and the row says why. */
  readonly belowTarget: boolean;
}

export interface WellboreTopRow {
  readonly code: string;
  readonly depth: string;
}

export interface WellboreFactsInput {
  /** 0 when no target depth has been set — the depth-dependent figures are then left out. */
  readonly totalDepth: number;
  readonly staticWaterLevel: number;
  readonly pumpSetting: number;
  readonly casings: readonly ICasingIR[];
  readonly tops: readonly ITopsIR[];
}

const INCH_MARK = '″';
const UNIT_MARKED = /["″”]|\bin\b/i;

export const formatFeet = (value: number): string => `${value.toLocaleString('en-US')} ft`;

/** "500–3,200 ft": the unit once, at the end. */
export const formatRange = (from: number, to: number): string => `${from.toLocaleString('en-US')}–${formatFeet(to)}`;

/** "18" → 18″, "9 5/8" → 9 5/8″; a size that already carries a unit mark is left alone. */
export function formatInches(size: string): string {
  const trimmed = size.trim();
  if (!trimmed) return '';
  return UNIT_MARKED.test(trimmed) ? trimmed : `${trimmed}${INCH_MARK}`;
}

export function buildWellboreFacts(input: WellboreFactsInput): WellboreFacts {
  const total = Math.max(input.totalDepth, 0);
  const casings = sortCasingsByDepth(input.casings);
  const deepest = casings.at(-1) ?? null;

  return {
    stats: buildStats(input, total, deepest),
    casingRows: casings.map(casing => ({
      size: formatInches(casing.csgSize),
      type: casing.csgType,
      depth: formatFeet(casing.csgDepth),
      belowTarget: total > 0 && casing.csgDepth > total,
    })),
    topRows: buildTopRows(input.tops),
  };
}

/** Shallow to deep — the order a casing program is read in. A string with no real depth is not a
 *  program entry, so it is left out; equal depths list the wider string first. */
export function sortCasingsByDepth(casings: readonly ICasingIR[]): ICasingIR[] {
  return casings
    .filter(casing => casing.csgDepth > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => a.csgDepth - b.csgDepth || (parseInchSize(b.csgSize) ?? 0) - (parseInchSize(a.csgSize) ?? 0));
}

/** A depth as a share of total depth, 0–100 (clamped: a shoe below TD sits at the end). */
export const percentOfTotal = (ft: number, total: number): number => Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (ft / total) * 100));

function buildStats(input: WellboreFactsInput, total: number, deepest: ICasingIR | null): WellboreStat[] {
  const { staticWaterLevel: water, pumpSetting: pump } = input;
  // "How much below ground": filled from the surface down to the depth, the glyph at its lower end.
  const below = (ft: number): WellboreGauge | undefined => {
    if (total <= 0) return undefined;
    const pct = percentOfTotal(ft, total);
    return { fromPct: 0, toPct: pct, markPct: pct };
  };

  const stats: WellboreStat[] = [];
  if (total > 0) stats.push({ label: 'Total depth', short: 'TD', value: formatFeet(total), glyph: 'depth', gauge: below(total) });
  if (water > 0) stats.push({ label: 'Static water level', short: 'Static WL', value: formatFeet(water), glyph: 'water', gauge: below(water) });
  if (deepest) {
    const belowTarget = total > 0 && deepest.csgDepth > total;
    const string = [formatInches(deepest.csgSize), deepest.csgType].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
    stats.push({
      label: 'Deepest shoe',
      short: 'Shoe',
      value: formatFeet(deepest.csgDepth),
      hint: [string, belowTarget ? 'below TD' : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' · '),
      warn: belowTarget,
      glyph: 'shoe',
      gauge: below(deepest.csgDepth),
    });
  }
  if (pump > 0) stats.push({ label: 'Pump setting', short: 'Pump', value: formatFeet(pump), glyph: 'pump', gauge: below(pump) });
  return stats;
}

function buildTopRows(tops: readonly ITopsIR[]): WellboreTopRow[] {
  return tops
    .filter(top => top.stLongCd.trim() !== '')
    .sort((a, b) => a.planTvdDepth - b.planTvdDepth)
    .map(top => ({ code: top.stLongCd.trim(), depth: top.planTvdDepth > 0 ? formatFeet(top.planTvdDepth) : '—' }));
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-wellbore-panel/wwell-ir-wellbore-glyph.component.ts

```ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, computed, input } from '@angular/core';
import { WellboreGlyphKind } from './wwell-ir-wellbore-facts.util';

/** The small pictograms the panel's readout tiles use, so a thing looks the same wherever it is shown: a drop
 *  for the static water level, a pump (volute, impeller, motor, outlet pipe) for the pump setting, a guide
 *  shoe for the deepest casing shoe, an arrow down from a ground bar for total depth.
 *  They take their colour from the surrounding `color` and are decorative — the text beside them says what
 *  they are. The pump is wider than the rest (16 × 14 against 10 × 14) and is drawn with its outlet pipe on
 *  the centre line, so it hangs from a pin by that pipe. */
@Component({
  selector: 'app-wwell-ir-wellbore-glyph',
  standalone: true,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  host: { '[attr.data-kind]': 'kind()' },
  template: `
    <svg [attr.viewBox]="viewBox()" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
      @switch (kind()) {
        @case ('water') {
          <path d="M5 .5C5 .5 .8 5.6 .8 9a4.2 4.2 0 0 0 8.4 0C9.2 5.6 5 .5 5 .5Z" />
          <path class="shine" d="M2.9 9.4a2.3 2.3 0 0 0 1.7 2.2" />
        }
        @case ('pump') {
          <rect x="6.6" y=".9" width="2.8" height="3.2" />
          <rect x="5.9" y="0" width="4.2" height="1.1" rx=".3" />
          <circle cx="8" cy="8.6" r="4.6" />
          <rect x="12" y="7.4" width="2" height="2.4" />
          <rect x="13.6" y="6.9" width="1.4" height="3.4" rx=".3" />
          <rect x="3.6" y="7.7" width="1.6" height="1.8" />
          <rect x=".4" y="5.6" width="3.8" height="6" rx="1.1" />
          <circle class="shine" cx="8" cy="8.6" r="2.3" />
          <path class="shine" d="M1.6 7.2v3M2.9 7.2v3" />
        }
        @case ('shoe') {
          <path d="M2 1h6v6.2L6.4 13H3.6L2 7.2Z" />
          <path class="shine" d="M3.6 2.6v4" />
        }
        @case ('depth') {
          <rect x="1" y="1" width="8" height="1.6" rx=".5" />
          <rect x="4.2" y="3.4" width="1.6" height="4.6" />
          <path d="M5 13 1.5 7.8h7Z" />
        }
      }
    </svg>
  `,
  styles: `
    :host {
      display: block;
      width: 12px;
      height: 16.8px;
      line-height: 0;
    }

    :host([data-kind='pump']) {
      width: 19px;
      height: 16.6px;
    }

    svg {
      display: block;
      width: 100%;
      height: 100%;
      overflow: visible;
      fill: currentColor;
    }

    /* Detail lines cut into the shape: the surface the glyph sits on, so they read as gaps in it. */
    .shine {
      fill: none;
      stroke: var(--wp-surface-alt);
      stroke-width: 0.8;
      stroke-linecap: round;
      opacity: 0.75;
    }
  `,
})
export class WwellIrWellboreGlyphComponent {
  readonly kind = input.required<WellboreGlyphKind>();
  protected readonly viewBox = computed(() => (this.kind() === 'pump' ? '0 0 16 14' : '0 0 10 14'));
}
```
