## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-wellbore-panel/wwell-ir-wellbore-panel.component.html

```html
<section
  class="wbp"
  [class.wbp--expanded]="isFilled()"
  [class.wbp--fit]="fit()"
  [class.wbp--compact]="compact()"
  [attr.aria-label]="title()"
>
  <header class="wbp__head">
    <div class="wbp__heading">
      <!-- The well's own name is the heading (the card is named for assistive tech by the section's aria-label);
           `title()` only stands in for a record with no well name yet. -->
      <h2 class="wbp__title">{{ diagramData().wellName || title() }}</h2>
      <p class="wbp__sub">Depths in ft below ground level</p>
    </div>
    @if (hasData()) {
      @if (expanded()) {
        <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="wbp__action" aria-label="Close expanded diagram" (click)="closed.emit()">
          <mat-icon>close</mat-icon>
        </button>
      } @else {
        <button
          type="button"
          mat-icon-button
          class="wbp__action"
          aria-label="Expand wellbore diagram"
          [matTooltip]="compact() ? 'Expand for full labels' : 'Expand'"
          matTooltipClass="wp-tooltip"
          (click)="expand()"
        >
          <mat-icon>open_in_full</mat-icon>
        </button>
      }
    }
  </header>

  @if (hasData()) {
    @let f = facts();

    <div class="wbp__stage">
      <div
        class="wbp__figure"
        [class.wbp__figure--no-depth]="!hasTargetDepth()"
        [style.--wbp-ratio]="ratio()"
        [style.--wbp-max-width]="maxWidthPx() + 'px'"
        [style.--wbp-max-height]="maxHeightPx() + 'px'"
      >
        @if (!isExpandedOpen()) {
          <app-wwell-ir-depth-scale
            [style.flex-basis.%]="scalePercent()"
            [totalDepth]="scaleDepth()"
            [targetDepth]="diagramData().targetDepth"
            [animTrigger]="0"
            [diagramLayout]="layout()"
          />
          <div class="wbp__plot">
            <svg class="wbp__grid" aria-hidden="true" preserveAspectRatio="none" [attr.viewBox]="gridViewBox()">
              @for (line of gridLines(); track line.depth) {
                <line x1="0" [attr.x2]="layout().wellboreViewWidth" [attr.y1]="line.y" [attr.y2]="line.y" />
              }
            </svg>
            <app-well-bore-view
              class="wbp__diagram"
              [diagramData]="diagramData()"
              [animTrigger]="0"
              [diagramLayout]="layout()"
              [isFullscreen]="isFilled()"
            />
          </div>
        }
      </div>
    </div>

    <section class="wbp__block wbp__block--legend" aria-label="Diagram legend">
      <h3 class="wbp__eyebrow">Legend</h3>
      <ul class="wbp__legend">
        @for (item of legend(); track item.key) {
          <li class="wbp__key">
            <span class="wbp__swatch wbp__swatch--{{ item.key }}" aria-hidden="true"></span>
            {{ item.label }}
          </li>
        }
      </ul>
    </section>

    <!-- The figures behind the drawing: key depths, the casing program, the formation tops — one card each,
         side by side where there is room. -->
    <div class="wbp__data">
      @if (f.stats.length > 0) {
        <section class="wbp__block wbp__block--stats" aria-label="Key depths">
          <h3 class="wbp__eyebrow">Key depths</h3>
          <dl class="wbp__stats">
            @for (stat of f.stats; track stat.label) {
              <div class="wbp__stat" [class.wbp__stat--warn]="stat.warn" [style.--wbp-i]="$index">
                <!-- The inline previews take the shorthand ("TD", "Static WL"): four figures beside a small drawing,
                     read at a glance. The maximised forms have the room for the full label. -->
                <dt>
                  {{ isFilled() ? stat.label : stat.short }}
                  @if (stat.hint) {
                    <span class="wbp__stat-hint">{{ stat.hint }}</span>
                  }
                </dt>
                <dd>{{ stat.value }}</dd>
                @if (stat.gauge; as gauge) {
                  <!-- How far down the well it is: the line is the well, surface to total depth, filled to the depth
                       (or, for the water column, over the stretch it fills), with the stat's own pictogram on it. -->
                  <span class="wbp__gauge wbp__gauge--{{ stat.glyph }}" aria-hidden="true">
                    <i class="wbp__gauge-fill" [style.left.%]="gauge.fromPct" [style.width.%]="gauge.toPct - gauge.fromPct"></i>
                    <app-wwell-ir-wellbore-glyph class="wbp__gauge-mark" [kind]="stat.glyph" [style.left.%]="gauge.markPct" />
                  </span>
                }
              </div>
            }
          </dl>
        </section>
      }

      @if (f.casingRows.length > 0 || f.topRows.length > 0) {
        <div class="wbp__facts">
          @if (f.casingRows.length > 0) {
            <section class="wbp__table wbp__table--casing" aria-label="Casing program">
              <h3 class="wbp__eyebrow">Casing program <span class="wbp__count">{{ f.casingRows.length }}</span></h3>
              <ul class="wbp__rows">
                @for (row of f.casingRows; track $index) {
                  <li class="wbp__row" [class.wbp__row--warn]="row.belowTarget">
                    <span class="wbp__row-size">{{ row.size }}</span>
                    <span class="wbp__row-type">{{ row.type }}</span>
                    <span class="wbp__row-depth">
                      {{ row.depth }}
                      @if (row.belowTarget) {
                        <span class="wbp__row-flag" title="Set deeper than the target depth">below TD</span>
                      }
                    </span>
                  </li>
                }
              </ul>
            </section>
          }
          @if (f.topRows.length > 0) {
            <section class="wbp__table wbp__table--tops" aria-label="Formation tops">
              <h3 class="wbp__eyebrow">Formation tops <span class="wbp__count">{{ f.topRows.length }}</span></h3>
              <ul class="wbp__rows wbp__rows--tops">
                @for (row of f.topRows; track $index) {
                  <li class="wbp__row">
                    <span class="wbp__row-type wbp__row-type--code">{{ row.code }}</span>
                    <span class="wbp__row-depth">{{ row.depth }}</span>
                  </li>
                }
              </ul>
            </section>
          }
        </div>
      }
    </div>
  } @else {
    <div class="wbp__empty">
      <span class="wbp__empty-icon"><mat-icon>architecture</mat-icon></span>
      <p class="wbp__empty-title">Nothing to draw yet</p>
      <p class="wbp__empty-hint">{{ emptyHint() }}</p>
    </div>
  }
</section>

<!-- Opened by Expand. The panel renders itself again, in its dialog form, so there is one
     implementation of the diagram, not two. -->
<ng-template #expandedTpl>
  <app-wwell-ir-wellbore-panel
    [diagramData]="diagramData()"
    [hasData]="hasData()"
    [hasTargetDepth]="hasTargetDepth()"
    [layout]="expandedLayout"
    [title]="title()"
    [expanded]="true"
    (closed)="collapse()"
  />
</ng-template>
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-wellbore-panel/wwell-ir-wellbore-panel.component.scss

```scss
@use '../../shared/wwell-design-tokens' as tokens;
@use '../../shared/wwell-material' as wmat;
@use '../wwell-ir-wellbore-theme' as wb;

// `ViewEncapsulation.None` (see the component) — everything is namespaced `.wbp`, and the
// `.wp-wellbore-dialog` block at the end styles the portalled Expand dialog's own surface.

app-wwell-ir-wellbore-panel {
  display: block;
  min-width: 0;
}

.wbp,
.wbp *,
.wbp *::before,
.wbp *::after {
  // The Expand dialog is portalled outside any page that sets this.
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

.wbp {
  // Depth gridlines: a quiet continuation of the axis ticks, never competing with the drawing.
  --wbp-grid: color-mix(in srgb, var(--wp-line-strong) 65%, transparent);
  // The sheen across a raised surface's upper corner, as light falls on glass.
  --wbp-sheen: linear-gradient(160deg, var(--wp-glass-sheen), transparent 55%);
  // The panel's type scale. Nothing is set smaller than `micro` (12px, upper-case labels only); prose and chips
  // sit on `small`, table rows and facts on `body`, the figures on `figure`.
  --wbp-fs-micro: 0.75rem;
  --wbp-fs-small: 0.8125rem;
  --wbp-fs-body: 0.875rem;
  --wbp-fs-figure: 1.125rem;
  // The rhythm between the panel's blocks, and between a block's label and its card.
  --wbp-block-gap: 0.95rem;

  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: var(--wbp-block-gap);
  padding: 0.85rem 0.9rem 0.9rem;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  border: 1px solid tokens.$border;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-lg;
  box-shadow: tokens.$shadow-1;
  color: tokens.$navy;
  color-scheme: var(--wp-scheme);
  @include wmat.buttons;
}

// ── Header ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
.wbp__head {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 0.5rem;
}

.wbp__heading {
  font-family: tokens.$font-display;
  min-width: 0;
}

// The well's name, in the mono face every other screen sets a well name in, and big: it is what the card is.
.wbp__title {
  position: relative;
  margin: 0;
  padding-left: 0.65rem;
  font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
  font-size: 1.375rem;
  font-weight: 650;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  letter-spacing: -0.012em;
  color: tokens.$navy;

  &::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    left: 0;
    top: 0.12em;
    bottom: 0.12em;
    width: 3px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    background: tokens.$accent;
  }
}

.wbp__sub {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 0.15rem 0.5rem;
  margin: 0.2rem 0 0 0.65rem;
  font-size: 0.8125rem;
  color: tokens.$muted;
}

.wbp__action {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  width: 30px;
  height: 30px;
  padding: 0;
  color: tokens.$muted;

  &:hover {
    color: tokens.$accent;
  }

  mat-icon {
    width: 18px;
    height: 18px;
    font-size: 18px;
  }
}

// ── Key depths ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Brief form (the inline previews, which is where a new IR is filled in): the four figures share one card as tiles —
// shorthand label over the figure, as many across as fit — with no gauges. The drawing beside them already shows
// where each one sits on the well, so the card is a read-out, not a second picture of it.
.wbp:not(.wbp--expanded) {
  // Two across, always: four figures make two even rows, where `auto-fit` would leave one alone on a ragged third.
  .wbp__stats {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }

  .wbp__stat {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
    row-gap: 0;
    padding: 0.35rem 0.7rem 0.4rem;
  }

  .wbp__stat dd {
    text-align: left;
  }

  .wbp__gauge {
    display: none;
  }
}

// A list, not a pile of tiles: every row has the same three parts on the same lines — label on the left, the
// figure right-aligned in one column, and under them the gauge (the well as a hairline, surface to total depth, at
// one length for every row so the rows compare). Rows are cut from one card by hairlines.
.wbp__block {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  min-width: 0;
}

.wbp__stats {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
  gap: 1px;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  background: var(--wp-line-soft);
  border: 1px solid var(--wp-line-soft);
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-md;
}

.wbp__stat {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
  align-items: baseline;
  column-gap: 0.75rem;
  row-gap: 0.1rem;
  min-width: 0;
  padding: var(--wbp-stat-pad, 0.5rem 0.8rem 0.4rem);
  background: var(--wbp-sheen), tokens.$surface;
  animation: wbp-rise tokens.$duration-slow tokens.$ease-out backwards;
  animation-delay: calc(var(--wbp-i, 0) * 45ms);

  dt {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    align-items: baseline;
    gap: 0 0.5rem;
    font-size: var(--wbp-fs-micro);
    font-weight: 600;
    letter-spacing: 0.06em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: tokens.$subtle;
  }

  dd {
    margin: 0;
    font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
    font-size: var(--wbp-fs-figure);
    font-weight: 600;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    text-align: right;
    white-space: nowrap;
    color: tokens.$navy-deep;
  }
}

// The gauge fills the row's foot from the surface down ("how much below ground") with its pictogram on the
// end; the water column fills the stretch it occupies.
.wbp__gauge {
  --wbp-tone: color-mix(in srgb, var(--wp-wb-outline) 60%, transparent);

  position: relative;
  grid-column: 1 / -1;
  height: var(--wbp-gauge-h, 16px);

  &::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    inset: 50% 0 auto;
    height: 3px;
    margin-top: -1.5px;
    border-radius: 2px;
    background: var(--wp-line);
  }
}

.wbp__gauge--water {
  --wbp-tone: var(--wp-wb-water);
}

.wbp__gauge--pump {
  --wbp-tone: var(--wp-accent);
}

.wbp__gauge-fill {
  position: absolute;
  top: 50%;
  display: block;
  height: 3px;
  margin-top: -1.5px;
  border-radius: 2px;
  background: var(--wbp-tone);
}

// Centred on the line at its point; the tone doubles as the glyph's colour.
.wbp__gauge-mark {
  position: absolute;
  top: 50%;
  color: var(--wbp-tone);
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 1.5px var(--wp-surface));
  transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
}

// The qualifier beside the label ("9″ Liner"): prose, not another upper-case label.
.wbp__stat-hint {
  font-family: tokens.$font-family;
  font-size: var(--wbp-fs-small);
  font-weight: 500;
  letter-spacing: 0;
  text-transform: none;
  color: tokens.$muted;
}

.wbp .wbp__stat--warn {
  background-image: var(--wbp-sheen), linear-gradient(var(--wp-warning-soft), var(--wp-warning-soft));

  dd {
    color: var(--wp-warning);
  }

  .wbp__stat-hint {
    font-weight: 600;
    color: var(--wp-warning);
  }
}

// Section label shared across the panel's cards.
.wbp__eyebrow {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.4rem;
  // As tall as the count badge some carry, so a label and its card start on the same line in every column.
  min-height: 1.15rem;
  margin: 0;
  font-size: var(--wbp-fs-micro);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: tokens.$subtle;
}

.wbp__count {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  min-width: 1.25rem;
  height: 1.15rem;
  padding: 0 0.35rem;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-pill;
  background: tokens.$surface-sunken;
  font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
  font-size: var(--wbp-fs-micro);
  letter-spacing: 0;
  color: tokens.$muted;
}

// ── Stage: the depth-gridded drawing surface ───────────────────────
.wbp__stage {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: center;
  container-type: inline-size;
  padding: 0.6rem 0.5rem 0.5rem 0;
  background: tokens.$surface-alt;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-md;
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px tokens.$border-soft;
}

// Width is the smallest of: the stage, the max width, and the width at which the max height is
// reached — so the aspect ratio always holds and the SVGs (`preserveAspectRatio="none"`) never
// stretch.
.wbp__figure {
  display: flex;
  align-items: stretch;
  flex: none;
  width: min(100cqw, var(--wbp-max-width, 100cqw), calc(var(--wbp-max-height, 9999px) * var(--wbp-ratio)));
  aspect-ratio: var(--wbp-ratio);
}

.wbp__plot {
  position: relative;
  flex: 1 1 0;
  min-width: 0;
}

.wbp__grid {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  pointer-events: none;

  line {
    stroke: var(--wbp-grid);
    stroke-width: 1;
    stroke-dasharray: 2 5;
    vector-effect: non-scaling-stroke;
  }
}

.wbp__diagram {
  position: relative;
  display: block;
  height: 100%;
}

// Hides the D3-rendered "TOTAL DEPTH: 0 FT" label until a target depth actually exists — and, since
// the scale then ends at a placeholder depth, stops calling its last tick out as the target.
.wbp__figure--no-depth .td-label {
  display: none;
}

.wbp .wbp__figure--no-depth {
  .tick-line--target {
    stroke: tokens.$muted;
  }

  .tick-label--target {
    fill: tokens.$navy-deep;
    font-weight: 600;
  }
}

// Beats the diagram's own `[_nghost] .geo-axis`-style rules (0,2,0): this compiles to (0,3,0).
.wbp .wbp__stage {
  @include wb.wellbore-theme($ng-deep: false);
}

// The figures behind the drawing: stacked in the sidebar, and where there is room (the view page, Sections view) the
// three cards — key depths, casing program, formation tops — stand side by side, each the width the sidebar gives it.
.wbp__data {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
  gap: var(--wbp-block-gap) 1rem;
}

.wbp:not(.wbp--compact):not(.wbp--expanded) {
  .wbp__data {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(17rem, 1fr));
    align-items: start;
  }

  .wbp__facts {
    display: contents;
  }
}

// The drawing's own type is set in viewBox units, so at the sidebar's size it printed at 8–10px. Lifted to about 12px
// (WWELL_IR_SIDEBAR_DIAGRAM_LAYOUT gives the depth axis the room its numerals then need; the geologic column and the
// total-depth label had it). The wider forms already print the same type at 12px and more.
.wbp--compact .wbp__stage {
  .tick-label,
  .geo-depth,
  .geo-code {
    font-size: 28px;
  }

  // The two-line titles keep the renderer's fixed line spacing (19–20 units), which caps their type.
  .axis-title {
    font-size: 18px;
  }

  .column-label {
    font-size: 19px;
  }

  .ground-label {
    font-size: 25px;
  }

  // Centred on the bore, so its width is what keeps it inside the drawing: 20 characters at 28 units just fit.
  .total-depth-main {
    font-size: 28px;
  }

  .casing-size-inner {
    font-size: 27px;
  }
}

// Sidebar form: the card fills the sidebar exactly — the well name, the drawing in whatever height is left, and the
// four key depths under it — so nothing is cut off at the foot and nothing has to be scrolled to. The legend and the
// casing / formation-top tables are left out here: the editor prints both, in its own Horizons and Casing sections a
// few inches to the left, and the drawing's symbols are named in the maximised view where they can be read.
// The host is as tall as the column the sidebar gives it, and the card fills it.
.wbp-host--compact {
  height: 100%;
}

.wbp--compact {
  height: 100%;

  .wbp__stage {
    flex: 1;
    // Below this the drawing's own numerals fall under 11px; a window too short for it scrolls the card instead.
    min-height: 22rem;
    container-type: size;
  }

  .wbp__figure {
    width: min(100cqw, calc(100cqh * var(--wbp-ratio)));
  }

  .wbp__block--legend,
  .wbp__facts {
    display: none;
  }
}

// Sidebar form: the leader labels render at ~6px and collide there, so they are left to Expand (showing them would
// cost ~145px of drawing each — a ~500px sidebar). Hover tooltips on the casing and the depth/geologic labels still
// work. The water arrow goes with the water label it points at, so nothing in the drawing points at a label that is
// not there.
.wbp--compact .wbp__stage {
  .casing-label__primary,
  .casing-label__secondary,
  .casing-label__line,
  .casing-label__arrow,
  .oh-label-line,
  .oh-label-arrow,
  .open-hole-text,
  .screen-label-line,
  .screen-label-text,
  .pump-leader-group,
  .water-label,
  .water-arrow {
    display: none;
  }
}

// ── Legend ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// A grid, so the swatches stand in one column and the names start on one line; the columns are as many as fit.
.wbp__legend {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(7.5rem, 1fr));
  gap: 0.55rem 0.75rem;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0.7rem 0.8rem;
  list-style: none;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  border: 1px solid var(--wp-line-soft);
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-md;
}

.wbp__key {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1.4rem minmax(0, 1fr);
  align-items: center;
  column-gap: 0.5rem;
  overflow: hidden;
  font-size: var(--wbp-fs-small);
  font-weight: 500;
  line-height: 1.2;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  white-space: nowrap;
  color: tokens.$muted;
}

.wbp__swatch {
  justify-self: center;
  display: inline-block;
  width: 14px;
  height: 12px;
}

// Swatches mirror how each element is drawn, so the legend reads as a key, not decoration.
.wbp__swatch--ground,
.wbp__swatch--water,
.wbp__swatch--tops {
  height: 0;
  align-self: center;
}

.wbp__swatch--ground {
  border-top: 2px solid var(--wp-wb-ground);
}

.wbp__swatch--water {
  border-top: 2px dashed var(--wp-wb-water);
}

.wbp__swatch--tops {
  width: 10px;
  border-top: 2px solid var(--wp-wb-outline);
}

.wbp__swatch--casing,
.wbp__swatch--liner {
  border-radius: 1px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #7c8798 0%, #dfe5ee 48%, #7c8798 100%);
}

.wbp__swatch--casing {
  width: 10px;
}

.wbp__swatch--liner {
  width: 7px;
}

.wbp__swatch--open-hole {
  width: 10px;
  border: 1.5px dashed var(--wp-wb-outline);
  border-top: 0;
}

.wbp__swatch--gravel {
  background: radial-gradient(circle, var(--wp-subtle) 1px, transparent 1.6px) 0 0 / 4px 4px;
}

.wbp__swatch--screen {
  background:
    repeating-linear-gradient(0deg, transparent 0 2px, var(--wp-subtle) 2px 3px),
    repeating-linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0 2px, var(--wp-subtle) 2px 3px);
  opacity: 0.8;
}

.wbp__swatch--perforated {
  width: 10px;
  border-right: 2px dotted var(--wp-wb-outline);
  border-left: 2px dotted var(--wp-wb-outline);
}

// ── Casing program + formation tops ────────────────────────────────
// These also stand in for the leader labels the compact sidebar hides.
.wbp__facts {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(15rem, 1fr));
  gap: var(--wbp-block-gap) 1rem;
}

.wbp__table {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  min-width: 0;
}

// A 1px grid gap over a tinted backing draws the hairline row dividers with no per-row borders. The list owns the
// columns and each row takes them from it (subgrid), so the sizes, types and depths line up down the card whatever a
// row holds; where subgrid is missing a row sizes its own columns, as it always did.
.wbp__rows {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: max-content minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
  gap: 1px;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  list-style: none;
  background: var(--wp-line-soft);
  border: 1px solid var(--wp-line-soft);
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-md;
}

.wbp__rows--tops {
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
}

.wbp__row {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: subgrid;
  grid-column: 1 / -1;
  align-items: baseline;
  column-gap: 0.75rem;
  padding: var(--wbp-row-pad, 0.45rem 0.8rem);
  background-color: tokens.$surface;
  font-size: var(--wbp-fs-body);
}

.wbp__row-size,
.wbp__row-depth {
  font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
  font-weight: 600;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  color: tokens.$navy-deep;
}

.wbp__row-type {
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  white-space: nowrap;
  color: tokens.$muted;
}

.wbp__row-type--code {
  font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: tokens.$navy;
}

.wbp__row-depth {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: baseline;
  justify-content: flex-end;
  gap: 0.4rem;
}

.wbp .wbp__row--warn {
  background-image: linear-gradient(var(--wp-warning-soft), var(--wp-warning-soft));

  .wbp__row-depth {
    color: var(--wp-warning);
  }
}

.wbp__row-flag {
  font-family: tokens.$font-family;
  font-size: var(--wbp-fs-micro);
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.05em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--wp-warning);
}

// ── Empty ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
.wbp__empty {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.3rem;
  padding: 1.5rem 1rem 1.3rem;
  text-align: center;
  background: tokens.$surface-alt;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-md;
}

.wbp__empty-icon {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 40px;
  height: 40px;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-md;
  background: var(--wp-accent-soft);
  color: tokens.$accent;

  mat-icon {
    width: 22px;
    height: 22px;
    font-size: 22px;
  }
}

.wbp__empty-title {
  margin: 0.35rem 0 0;
  font-size: 0.86rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: tokens.$navy;
}

.wbp__empty-hint {
  margin: 0;
  max-width: 32ch;
  font-size: 0.74rem;
  line-height: 1.5;
  color: tokens.$muted;
}

// ── Motion ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Entrance only, once, on compositor properties: the bar draws in from the surface, its markers drop
// on, the tiles rise in turn.
@keyframes wbp-rise {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(6px);
  }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .wbp,
  .wbp *,
  .wbp *::before,
  .wbp *::after {
    animation: none !important;
    transition: none !important;
  }
}

// ── Expanded (dialog) form ─────────────────────────────────────────
// The maximised view: nothing in it scrolls. The dialog takes most of the window (see `expand()`); inside it the
// drawing takes the left with its key in one line right under it, and the figures stand in a rail beside them. The
// figure is sized off the stage's own box (container query units) so it fills its area at the diagram's true aspect
// ratio instead of being cropped or stretched. The rail is set tight enough to fit a 900px-high window whole; from
// 980px up (`wbp-dialog`) it gets the room back.
.wbp--expanded {
  --wbp-block-gap: 0.6rem;
  --wbp-stat-pad: 0.26rem 0.8rem 0.2rem;
  --wbp-gauge-h: 12px;
  --wbp-row-pad: 0.22rem 0.8rem;

  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) clamp(19.5rem, 22vw, 23rem);
  grid-template-rows: auto minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
  grid-template-areas:
    'head   head'
    'stage  data'
    'legend data';
  gap: 0.65rem 1.25rem;
  height: 100%;
  padding: 0.85rem 1.25rem 0.75rem;
  background: transparent;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 0;
  box-shadow: none;

  .wbp__head {
    grid-area: head;
  }

  // The rail: key depths, then the two tables in what is left.
  .wbp__data {
    grid-area: data;
    grid-template-rows: auto minmax(0, 1fr);
    min-height: 0;
  }

  .wbp__stage {
    grid-area: stage;
    align-items: center;
    min-height: 0;
    container-type: size;
    padding: 0.75rem 1rem 0.75rem 0;
  }

  // The key: one line under the drawing, in the drawing's own column. No label of its own (the section is named for
  // assistive tech); the entries sit close together and never wrap.
  .wbp__block--legend {
    grid-area: legend;
  }

  .wbp__block--legend .wbp__eyebrow {
    display: none;
  }

  .wbp__legend {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: nowrap;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 0 1.1rem;
    padding: 0.5rem 0.9rem;
  }

  .wbp__key {
    display: flex;
    flex: 0 0 auto;
    gap: 0.45rem;
    font-size: var(--wbp-fs-body);
  }

  // Only if the window is too short for the rail after all does it scroll, on its own.
  .wbp__facts {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
    align-content: start;
    min-height: 0;
    overflow-y: auto;
  }

  .wbp__title {
    font-size: 1.5rem;
  }

  .wbp__sub {
    font-size: 0.875rem;
  }

  .wbp__stat dd {
    font-size: 1.0625rem;
  }

  .wbp__figure {
    width: min(100cqw, calc(100cqh * var(--wbp-ratio)));
  }
}

// A taller window gives the rail its room back.
@container wbp-dialog (min-height: 980px) {
  .wbp--expanded {
    .wbp__block--stats {
      --wbp-stat-pad: 0.5rem 0.8rem 0.4rem;
      --wbp-gauge-h: 16px;
    }

    .wbp__facts {
      --wbp-row-pad: 0.45rem 0.8rem;
      --wbp-block-gap: 0.95rem;
    }

    .wbp__stat dd {
      font-size: 1.125rem;
    }
  }
}

// The drawing's own type, in viewBox units, so what it prints at follows the stage's size. Two steps, each taken when
// the stage is small enough in *either* direction (a tall, narrow stage draws the well just as small as a short one):
// the first lifts everything about a third, the second again for a stage at about half scale. Two caps hold through
// both: the two-line titles ("Geologic Horizons", "DEPTH (FT BGL)") stay ≤19 units because the renderer's line
// spacing is fixed at 19–20, and the casing remarks stay at 21 or they run past the drawing's right edge.
// `@container` here asks the stage itself — the nearest container above each label.
@mixin stage-type-step-1 {
  .tick-label {
    font-size: 24px;
  }

  .geo-depth,
  .geo-code {
    font-size: 26px;
  }

  .axis-title {
    font-size: 18px;
  }

  .column-label {
    font-size: 19px;
  }

  .ground-label {
    font-size: 23px;
  }

  .total-depth-main {
    font-size: 27px;
  }

  .casing-label__primary,
  .water-label,
  .pump-label {
    font-size: 26px;
  }

  .casing-label__secondary {
    font-size: 21px;
  }

  .open-hole-text,
  .screen-label-text {
    font-size: 25px;
  }

  .casing-size-inner {
    font-size: 28px;
  }
}

@mixin stage-type-step-2 {
  .tick-label {
    font-size: 28px;
  }

  .geo-depth,
  .geo-code {
    font-size: 29px;
  }

  .ground-label {
    font-size: 27px;
  }

  .total-depth-main {
    font-size: 31px;
  }

  .casing-label__primary,
  .water-label,
  .pump-label {
    font-size: 30px;
  }

  .open-hole-text,
  .screen-label-text {
    font-size: 29px;
  }

  .casing-size-inner {
    font-size: 30px;
  }
}

@container (max-height: 680px) {
  .wbp--expanded .wbp__stage {
    @include stage-type-step-1;
  }
}

@container (max-width: 950px) {
  .wbp--expanded .wbp__stage {
    @include stage-type-step-1;
  }
}

@container (max-height: 600px) {
  .wbp--expanded .wbp__stage {
    @include stage-type-step-2;
  }
}

@container (max-width: 780px) {
  .wbp--expanded .wbp__stage {
    @include stage-type-step-2;
  }
}

// Under ~900px of stage the browser sets small text a little wider than its box scales to, so the leader labels take
// one unit off here — last, so it holds whichever step above applied — and the widest of them ("18" Intermediate
// @ 1,500") still ends inside the drawing.
@container (max-width: 900px) {
  .wbp--expanded .wbp__stage {
    .casing-label__primary,
    .water-label,
    .pump-label {
      font-size: 29px;
    }

    .casing-label__secondary {
      font-size: 20px;
    }
  }
}

// Not enough width for a rail: stack everything and let the dialog body scroll. The stage needs a
// definite height of its own then (it is a size container), so it gets one.
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .wbp--expanded {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    overflow-y: auto;

    .wbp__stage {
      flex: none;
      height: min(70vh, 560px);
    }

    .wbp__data {
      flex: none;
      grid-template-rows: none;
    }

    .wbp__facts {
      overflow-y: visible;
    }

    .wbp__legend {
      flex-wrap: wrap;
      justify-content: flex-start;
    }
  }
}

// The panel's host is the container the maximised view asks about its size (`@container wbp-dialog`): it is as tall
// and wide as the dialog, and `.wbp--expanded` re-grids itself by it.
.wp-wellbore-dialog app-wwell-ir-wellbore-panel,
.wbp-host--fit {
  container: wbp-dialog / size;
}

// ── Fit form (the view page's Well Design step) ────────────────────
// The maximised layout, inline: the host is as tall as the page it sits in (its parent gives it a height), and the
// panel is a card again. The drawing and its key stand left, the figures right; the page already names the well and
// the step, so the title is kept for assistive tech only and Expand floats over the drawing's own row. The rail is
// set as tight as a laptop-height page needs, and is given its room back as it grows.
.wbp-host--fit {
  height: 100%;
}

.wbp--expanded.wbp--fit {
  --wbp-block-gap: 0.5rem;
  --wbp-stat-pad: 0.24rem 0.7rem 0.18rem;
  --wbp-gauge-h: 8px;
  --wbp-row-pad: 0.2rem 0.7rem;

  position: relative;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) 14.5rem;
  grid-template-rows: minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
  grid-template-areas:
    'stage  data'
    'legend legend';
  gap: 0.4rem 0.7rem;
  padding: 0.45rem 0.6rem 0.5rem;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  border: 1px solid tokens.$border;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-lg;
  box-shadow: tokens.$shadow-1;

  .wbp__head {
    position: absolute;
    top: 0.62rem;
    right: 0.85rem;
    z-index: 2;
    grid-area: auto;
  }

  .wbp__heading {
    position: absolute;
    width: 1px;
    height: 1px;
    overflow: hidden;
    clip-path: inset(50%);
    white-space: nowrap;
  }

  .wbp__action {
    width: 26px;
    height: 26px;
  }

  .wbp__stage {
    padding: 0.35rem 0.4rem 0.35rem 0;
  }

  .wbp__legend {
    flex-wrap: nowrap;
    justify-content: space-evenly;
    gap: 0 0.6rem;
    padding: 0.4rem 0.6rem;
  }

  .wbp__title {
    font-size: 1.25rem;
  }
}

// The rail's density follows the page height: below ~780px the gauges shrink to a hairline, below ~700 they go.
@container wbp-dialog (max-height: 780px) {
  .wbp--expanded.wbp--fit {
    --wbp-stat-pad: 0.2rem 0.7rem 0.16rem;
    --wbp-gauge-h: 3px;
    --wbp-row-pad: 0.14rem 0.7rem;
    --wbp-block-gap: 0.4rem;
  }
}

@container wbp-dialog (max-height: 700px) {
  .wbp--expanded.wbp--fit {
    --wbp-stat-pad: 0.14rem 0.7rem 0.12rem;
    --wbp-row-pad: 0.1rem 0.7rem;
    --wbp-block-gap: 0.3rem;

    .wbp__gauge,
    .wbp__stat-hint {
      display: none;
    }

    .wbp__row,
    .wbp__stat {
      line-height: 1.15;
    }

    // Still on the scale (12px), one step down from the rows' usual size.
    .wbp__row,
    .wbp__stat dt {
      font-size: var(--wbp-fs-small);
    }

    .wbp__stat dd {
      font-size: 0.9375rem;
    }

    .wbp__eyebrow {
      min-height: 0.9rem;
    }

    .wbp__legend {
      padding: 0.28rem 0.6rem;
    }
  }
}

.wp-wellbore-dialog {
  .mat-mdc-dialog-surface {
    background: tokens.$surface-raised !important;
    border: 1px solid tokens.$border;
    border-radius: tokens.$radius-lg !important;
    box-shadow: tokens.$shadow-3 !important;
    color-scheme: var(--wp-scheme);
    overflow: hidden;
  }

  app-wwell-ir-wellbore-panel {
    height: 100%;
  }
}

// ── Both sides of the well (a wide maximised view) ─────────────────
// Wide enough to read the drawing from both sides: the well stands in the middle, the depth figures it is measured by
// (key depths) on the left — the side its ruler is drawn on — and the two programmes the leader labels on its right
// name (casing, then formation tops) on the right. The key stays under the drawing, as its footnote. Each side is
// shorter than one stacked rail, so a laptop-height window fits without scrolling too. Last in the file on purpose:
// it also re-grids the inline `fit` form, whose own rule has the same weight.
@container wbp-dialog (min-width: 1140px) {
  .wbp--expanded,
  .wbp--expanded.wbp--fit {
    // The sides grow with the dialog (`cqw` is its width), so the well keeps the middle at any size.
    grid-template-columns: clamp(14rem, 16cqw, 20rem) minmax(0, 1fr) clamp(14rem, 16cqw, 20rem);
    grid-template-rows: auto minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
    grid-template-areas:
      'head  head  head'
      'stats stage facts'
      '.     legend .';

    .wbp__data {
      display: contents;
    }

    .wbp__block--stats {
      grid-area: stats;
      align-content: start;
      min-height: 0;
      overflow-y: auto;
    }

    .wbp__facts {
      grid-area: facts;
      grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
      align-content: start;
      min-height: 0;
      overflow-y: auto;
    }

    // In a column this narrow a label and its figure cannot share a line ("STATIC WATER LEVEL" alone is most of it),
    // so each key depth becomes a tile: label over figure, the gauge under both. It reads as a column of readings
    // beside the well, and fills the height the two tables take on the other side.
    .wbp__stat {
      grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
      row-gap: 0.05rem;
      padding: 0.42rem 0.8rem 0.38rem;

      dd {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        text-align: left;
      }
    }

    .wbp__stat-hint {
      width: 100%;
    }
  }

  // The inline form has no header row of its own (its one action floats over the drawing).
  .wbp--expanded.wbp--fit {
    grid-template-rows: minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
    grid-template-areas:
      'stats stage facts'
      '.     legend .';
  }
}

// The key sizes itself by the room its own row has, not by the dialog's: under the drawing it has the middle column,
// in the stacked forms the whole width. Measured, its eight entries need about 857px at `body` with roomy gaps,
// 749 at `small`, 696 at `micro` — so each step down is taken just before they would wrap.
.wbp--expanded .wbp__block--legend {
  container: wbp-key / inline-size;
}

@container wbp-key (max-width: 875px) {
  .wbp--expanded .wbp__legend {
    gap: 0 0.625rem;
  }

  .wbp--expanded .wbp__key {
    gap: 0.4rem;
    font-size: var(--wbp-fs-small);
  }
}

@container wbp-key (max-width: 765px) {
  .wbp--expanded .wbp__legend {
    gap: 0 0.5rem;
  }

  .wbp--expanded .wbp__key {
    font-size: var(--wbp-fs-micro);
  }
}

@container wbp-key (max-width: 710px) {
  .wbp--expanded .wbp__legend {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    justify-content: flex-start;
  }
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-wellbore-panel/wwell-ir-wellbore-panel.component.ts

```ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, TemplateRef, ViewEncapsulation, computed, inject, input, output, signal, viewChild } from '@angular/core';
import { MatButtonModule } from '@angular/material/button';
import { MatDialog, MatDialogRef } from '@angular/material/dialog';
import { MatIconModule } from '@angular/material/icon';
import { MatTooltipModule } from '@angular/material/tooltip';
import { DiagramLayout, WellboreDiagramData } from '@models/well-design/wellbore-diagram.model';
import { createDepthScale } from '@shared/utils/wellbore-math.util';
import { WellBoreViewComponent } from '../../../presentation/well-bore-view/well-bore-view.component';
import { WwellIrDepthScaleComponent } from '../wwell-ir-depth-scale/wwell-ir-depth-scale.component';
import { buildScaleTicks, resolveScaleDepthFt } from '../wwell-ir-depth-scale/wwell-ir-depth-ticks.util';
import { WWELL_IR_EXPANDED_DIAGRAM_LAYOUT, WWELL_IR_PREVIEW_DIAGRAM_LAYOUT } from '../wwell-ir-editor/wwell-ir-preview-diagram-layout';
import { buildWellboreFacts } from './wwell-ir-wellbore-facts.util';
import { WwellIrWellboreGlyphComponent } from './wwell-ir-wellbore-glyph.component';

const CASING_TYPE_LINER = 'Liner';
const CASING_TYPE_GRAVEL_PACK = 'Gravel Pack';
const DEFAULT_MAX_WIDTH_PX = 600;
const DEFAULT_MAX_HEIGHT_PX = 700;
/** The maximised view takes most of the window: this share of it, but not less than the floors (any smaller and the
 *  drawing and every figure cannot all be shown without scrolling) nor more than the caps (past them a bigger dialog
 *  only adds margin round the drawing). */
const EXPANDED_VIEWPORT_SHARE_PCT = 92;
const EXPANDED_MIN_WIDTH_PX = 1240;
const EXPANDED_MIN_HEIGHT_PX = 700;
const EXPANDED_MAX_WIDTH_PX = 1760;
const EXPANDED_MAX_HEIGHT_PX = 1100;

interface WellborePanelLegendKey {
  readonly key: 'ground' | 'water' | 'casing' | 'liner' | 'open-hole' | 'gravel' | 'screen' | 'perforated' | 'tops';
  readonly label: string;
}

interface WellboreGridLine {
  readonly depth: number;
  readonly y: number;
}

const isFlagYes = (flag: string | null | undefined): boolean => (flag ?? '').trim().toUpperCase() === 'Y';

/** The wellbore diagram as a finished panel: header, readouts, the depth-gridded diagram, a legend of only the
 *  elements actually drawn, the casing program and formation tops, and an Expand action that opens the same
 *  diagram in Presentation's fullscreen layout (see WWELL_IR_EXPANDED_DIAGRAM_LAYOUT) — the inline preview is
 *  deliberately compact, so its labels are small by design and Expand is where they are comfortable to read.
 *  Shared by the editor's preview (Sections and the Timeline sidebar) and the view page's Well Design step.
 *  The Letter has its own diagram markup (it is exported as a PNG for the PDF, which is captured from a
 *  paper-scoped copy).
 *
 *  `ViewEncapsulation.None`: the Expand dialog is portalled to <body>, so its surface can only be
 *  styled by global rules (`.wp-wellbore-dialog`). Everything else is namespaced `.wbp`. */
@Component({
  selector: 'app-wwell-ir-wellbore-panel',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MatButtonModule, MatIconModule, MatTooltipModule, WellBoreViewComponent, WwellIrDepthScaleComponent, WwellIrWellboreGlyphComponent],
  templateUrl: './wwell-ir-wellbore-panel.component.html',
  styleUrl: './wwell-ir-wellbore-panel.component.scss',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  encapsulation: ViewEncapsulation.None,
  host: { '[class.wbp-host--fit]': 'fit()', '[class.wbp-host--compact]': 'compact()' },
})
export class WwellIrWellborePanelComponent {
  readonly diagramData = input.required<WellboreDiagramData>();
  readonly hasData = input.required<boolean>();
  /** Hides the D3 "TOTAL DEPTH: 0 FT" label (and the readout) until a target depth exists. */
  readonly hasTargetDepth = input(true);
  readonly layout = input<DiagramLayout>(WWELL_IR_PREVIEW_DIAGRAM_LAYOUT);
  readonly title = input('Wellbore Preview');
  readonly emptyHint = input('Add a casing or formation top row — with type, size, and depth filled in — to preview the wellbore diagram.');
  /** Caps for the inline figure; whichever binds first sets its width (height follows the ratio). */
  readonly maxWidthPx = input(DEFAULT_MAX_WIDTH_PX);
  readonly maxHeightPx = input(DEFAULT_MAX_HEIGHT_PX);
  /** The narrow sidebar form: the diagram's leader labels are hidden (too small to read, and they
   *  collide) and left to the Expand view. */
  readonly compact = input(false);
  /** Set by the Expand dialog's own instance: fills the dialog, shows Close instead of Expand. */
  readonly expanded = input(false);
  /** Fills the box its parent gives it (which must have a height), laid out like the maximised view — the drawing
   *  and its key left, the figures right — so the whole panel is seen without scrolling. Keeps the Expand action.
   *  The parent passes the layout the drawing is to use (WWELL_IR_EXPANDED_DIAGRAM_LAYOUT). */
  readonly fit = input(false);
  readonly closed = output<void>();

  /** The maximised layout: the dialog's own instance, or an inline one that fills its box. */
  protected readonly isFilled = computed(() => this.expanded() || this.fit());

  protected readonly expandedLayout = WWELL_IR_EXPANDED_DIAGRAM_LAYOUT;

  private readonly dialog = inject(MatDialog);
  private readonly expandedTemplate = viewChild.required<TemplateRef<unknown>>('expandedTpl');
  private expandedDialog: MatDialogRef<unknown> | null = null;

  /** While the Expand dialog is open the inline diagram is unmounted (its box is kept). The shared
   *  renderer gives its clip-paths and gradients fixed SVG ids, and `url(#id)` resolves to the
   *  *first* match in the document — so two diagrams mounted at once make the second one draw
   *  against the first one's coordinate space and its casing vanishes. */
  protected readonly isExpandedOpen = signal(false);

  /** Width : height of the whole figure (depth scale + diagram). Matching the SVG viewBoxes'
   *  combined aspect is what keeps `preserveAspectRatio="none"` from distorting the drawing. */
  protected readonly ratio = computed(() => {
    const { depthScaleWidth, wellboreViewWidth, svgHeight } = this.layout();
    return (depthScaleWidth + wellboreViewWidth) / svgHeight;
  });

  protected readonly scalePercent = computed(() => {
    const { depthScaleWidth, wellboreViewWidth } = this.layout();
    return (depthScaleWidth / (depthScaleWidth + wellboreViewWidth)) * 100;
  });

  protected readonly gridViewBox = computed(() => {
    const { wellboreViewWidth, svgHeight } = this.layout();
    return `0 0 ${wellboreViewWidth} ${svgHeight}`;
  });

  /** The depth the scale runs to: the target depth, redrawn to the deepest formation top once one
   *  goes past it (see resolveScaleDepthFt). */
  protected readonly scaleDepth = computed(() => resolveScaleDepthFt(this.diagramData()));

  /** Depth → y in the diagram SVG's own coordinates, before the top margin: the axis's scale. */
  private readonly depthScale = computed(() => createDepthScale(this.scaleDepth(), this.layout().drawingHeight));

  /** One hairline per depth tick, in the diagram SVG's own coordinates — the same scale and ticks
   *  the depth axis uses, so each line continues its tick across the diagram. */
  protected readonly gridLines = computed<readonly WellboreGridLine[]>(() => {
    const scaleDepth = this.scaleDepth();
    if (scaleDepth <= 0) return [];
    const { marginTop, drawingHeight } = this.layout();
    const scale = this.depthScale();
    return buildScaleTicks(scaleDepth, drawingHeight, this.diagramData().targetDepth)
      .filter(depth => depth > 0)
      .map(depth => ({ depth, y: marginTop + scale(depth) }));
  });

  private readonly staticWaterLevelFt = computed(() => {
    const data = this.diagramData();
    return data.wellDesign?.staWaterLvl ?? data.hydrogeology?.estStaticWaterLevel ?? 0;
  });

  /** 0 until a target depth exists: every depth-dependent figure is then left out. */
  protected readonly totalDepthFt = computed(() => (this.hasTargetDepth() ? this.diagramData().totalDepth : 0));

  /** The readouts and casing/tops tables printed around the diagram. */
  protected readonly facts = computed(() => {
    const data = this.diagramData();
    return buildWellboreFacts({
      totalDepth: this.totalDepthFt(),
      staticWaterLevel: this.staticWaterLevelFt(),
      pumpSetting: data.wellDesign?.pumpLvl ?? 0,
      casings: data.casings,
      tops: data.geologicTops,
    });
  });

  /** Only what the diagram actually draws, from the same data/flags the renderers read. */
  protected readonly legend = computed<readonly WellborePanelLegendKey[]>(() => {
    const data = this.diagramData();
    const design = data.wellDesign;
    const keys: WellborePanelLegendKey[] = [{ key: 'ground', label: 'Ground level' }];
    if (this.staticWaterLevelFt() > 0) keys.push({ key: 'water', label: 'Water level' });
    if (data.casings.some(c => c.csgType !== CASING_TYPE_LINER && c.csgType !== CASING_TYPE_GRAVEL_PACK)) {
      keys.push({ key: 'casing', label: 'Casing' });
    }
    if (data.casings.some(c => c.csgType === CASING_TYPE_LINER)) keys.push({ key: 'liner', label: 'Liner' });
    if (isFlagYes(design?.ohFlg)) keys.push({ key: 'open-hole', label: 'Open hole' });
    if (isFlagYes(design?.gpFlg)) keys.push({ key: 'gravel', label: 'Gravel pack' });
    if (isFlagYes(design?.lsFlg)) keys.push({ key: 'screen', label: 'Liner screen' });
    if (isFlagYes(design?.perforatedFlg)) keys.push({ key: 'perforated', label: 'Perforated' });
    if (data.geologicTops.length > 0) keys.push({ key: 'tops', label: 'Formation top' });
    return keys;
  });

  protected expand(): void {
    this.isExpandedOpen.set(true);
    this.expandedDialog = this.dialog.open(this.expandedTemplate(), {
      panelClass: 'wp-wellbore-dialog',
      width: `min(max(${EXPANDED_VIEWPORT_SHARE_PCT}vw, ${EXPANDED_MIN_WIDTH_PX}px), 98vw, ${EXPANDED_MAX_WIDTH_PX}px)`,
      maxWidth: '98vw',
      height: `min(max(${EXPANDED_VIEWPORT_SHARE_PCT}vh, ${EXPANDED_MIN_HEIGHT_PX}px), 98vh, ${EXPANDED_MAX_HEIGHT_PX}px)`,
      ariaLabel: this.title(),
      autoFocus: 'dialog',
    });
    this.expandedDialog.afterClosed().subscribe(() => {
      this.expandedDialog = null;
      this.isExpandedOpen.set(false);
    });
  }

  protected collapse(): void {
    this.expandedDialog?.close();
  }
}
```
