## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir.component.html

```html
<section class="wwell-ir">
  <div class="wwell-ir__master">
    <header class="wwell-ir__header">
      <div class="wwell-ir__heading">
        <h1 class="wwell-ir__title">Integrated Requirements</h1>
        <span class="wwell-ir__count">{{ rows().length }}</span>
      </div>

      <div class="wwell-ir__actions">
        <button
          type="button"
          mat-flat-button
          color="primary"
          class="wwell-ir__new-btn"
          [disabled]="store.creatingIr()"
          [attr.aria-busy]="store.creatingIr()"
          (click)="createNewIr()"
        >
          <span class="wwell-ir__new-inner">
            <span class="wwell-ir__new-icon" aria-hidden="true">
              @if (store.creatingIr()) {
                <span class="wwell-ir__new-spinner"></span>
              } @else {
                <mat-icon>add</mat-icon>
              }
            </span>
            {{ store.creatingIr() ? 'Creating&hellip;' : 'New IR' }}
          </span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </header>

    <div class="wwell-ir__filter-bar">
      <button
        type="button"
        class="wwell-ir__lifecycle-toggle"
        [class.wwell-ir__lifecycle-toggle--inactive]="lifecycleFilter() === 'inactive'"
        [attr.aria-label]="lifecycleFilter() === 'active' ? 'Showing active records — click for inactive' : 'Showing inactive records — click for active'"
        [attr.title]="lifecycleFilter() === 'active' ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'"
        (click)="toggleLifecycleFilter()"
      >
        <mat-icon>{{ lifecycleFilter() === 'active' ? 'toggle_on' : 'toggle_off' }}</mat-icon>
      </button>

      @if (lifecycleFilter() === 'active') {
        <div class="wwell-ir__status-chips" role="group" aria-label="Status filter" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
          @for (status of statusChips; track status) {
            <button
              type="button"
              class="wwell-ir__status-chip"
              [class.wwell-ir__status-chip--active]="statusFilter() === status"
              (click)="setStatusFilter(status)"
            >
              {{ statusChipLabel(status) }} &middot; {{ statusChipCount(status) }}
            </button>
          }
        </div>
      }
    </div>

    <label class="wwell-ir__search">
      <mat-icon class="wwell-ir__search-icon">search</mat-icon>
      <input
        type="search"
        placeholder="Search code, well&hellip;"
        aria-label="Search by code, well, request or client"
        title="Search by code, well, request or client"
        [ngModel]="searchTerm()"
        (ngModelChange)="onSearchChange($event)"
      />
    </label>

    @if (isLoading()) {
      <ul class="wwell-ir__list wwell-ir__list--skeleton" aria-hidden="true">
        @for (n of skeletonRows; track n) {
          <li class="wwell-ir__row wwell-ir__skeleton-row">
            <div class="wwell-ir__row-top">
              <span class="wwell-ir__skeleton-bar wwell-ir__skeleton-bar--code"></span>
              <span class="wwell-ir__skeleton-bar wwell-ir__skeleton-bar--badge"></span>
            </div>
            <span class="wwell-ir__skeleton-bar wwell-ir__skeleton-bar--name"></span>
            <span class="wwell-ir__skeleton-bar wwell-ir__skeleton-bar--meta"></span>
          </li>
        }
      </ul>
    } @else if (loadError()) {
      <div class="wwell-ir__status wwell-ir__status--error">{{ loadError() }}</div>
    } @else if (rows().length === 0) {
      <div class="wwell-ir__status">No integrated requirements found.</div>
    } @else {
      <cdk-virtual-scroll-viewport
        [itemSize]="rowItemSize"
        class="wwell-ir__viewport"
        #listHost
        role="list"
        aria-label="Integrated requirements"
      >
        <div
          *cdkVirtualFor="let ir of rows(); trackBy: trackByIrRow"
          class="wwell-ir__row-item"
          [style.height.px]="rowItemSize"
          role="listitem"
        >
          <button
            type="button"
            class="wwell-ir__row"
            [class.wwell-ir__row--active]="ir.irSeq === selectedIrSeq()"
            [class.wwell-ir__row--pending]="ir.irSeq === pendingId()"
            [style.borderLeftColor]="chainBorderColor(ir.chain)"
            [disabled]="rowIrSeq(ir) === null"
            (click)="selectIrRow(ir)"
          >
            <div class="wwell-ir__row-top">
              <span class="wwell-ir__row-code">{{ ir.code }}</span>
              @if (hasKnownStatus(ir)) {
                <span
                  class="wwell-ir__badge"
                  [style.background]="statusMeta[ir.status].bg"
                  [style.color]="statusMeta[ir.status].color"
                >
                  <span class="wwell-ir__badge-dot" [style.background]="statusMeta[ir.status].dot"></span>
                  {{ ir.sapWfStatusText ?? statusMeta[ir.status].label }}
                </span>
              }
            </div>
            @if (ir.requestReference.wellName) {
              <p class="wwell-ir__row-name">{{ ir.requestReference.wellName }}</p>
            }
            <p class="wwell-ir__row-meta">{{ ir.requestReference.client }} &middot; Updated {{ ir.updatedDate }}</p>
          </button>

          @if (rowIrSeq(ir); as irSeq) {
            <button
              type="button"
              mat-icon-button
              class="wwell-ir__view-btn"
              aria-label="View integrated requirement"
              title="View"
              (click)="$event.stopPropagation(); viewIr(irSeq)"
            >
              <mat-icon>visibility</mat-icon>
            </button>
          }
        </div>
      </cdk-virtual-scroll-viewport>
    }
  </div>

  <div class="wwell-ir__detail">
    @if (isSelecting()) {
      <!-- 'edit' is the default/sticky detail mode (see WwellIrDetailMode's own doc comment), so
           most selections land on the editor's rail+section+table layout, not the read-only
           stepper — the shimmer below matches whichever one is about to render. -->
      @if (detailMode() === 'view') {
        <app-wwell-detail-loading />
      } @else {
        <app-wwell-ir-editor-loading [viewMode]="editorViewMode()" />
      }
    } @else if (selectedIr(); as ir) {
      <div class="wwell-ir__detail-panel" [style.--chain-color]="chainBorderColor(ir.chain)" #detailHost>
        @if (detailMode() === 'view') {
          <div class="wwell-ir__view-layout">
            <app-wwell-ir-view
              class="wwell-ir__view-main"
              [ir]="ir"
              [linkedRequestWellOptions]="store.linkedRequestWellOptions()"
              [(activeTab)]="viewTab"
              (editRequested)="setDetailMode('edit')"
            />
            <aside class="wwell-ir__view-sidebar">
              <div class="wwell-ir__approval-card">
                <header class="wwell-ir__approval-head">
                  <span class="wwell-ir__approval-icon">
                    <mat-icon>verified</mat-icon>
                  </span>
                  <div class="wwell-ir__approval-head-text">
                    <h2 class="wwell-ir__approval-title">Approval Workflow</h2>
                    @if (store.irApprovalTimelineLoading()) {
                      <p class="wwell-ir__approval-progress">Loading status…</p>
                    } @else if (ir.sapWfId === null) {
                      <p class="wwell-ir__approval-progress">Not yet submitted for review.</p>
                    } @else {
                      <p class="wwell-ir__approval-progress">{{ approvedStepCount(ir.chain) }} of {{ ir.chain.length }} approved</p>
                    }
                  </div>
                  @if (ir.sapWfId !== null) {
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      mat-icon-button
                      class="wwell-ir__approval-refresh-btn"
                      aria-label="Refresh approval status"
                      title="Refresh approval status"
                      [disabled]="store.irApprovalTimelineLoading()"
                      (click)="refreshApprovalStatus(ir)"
                    >
                      <mat-icon>refresh</mat-icon>
                    </button>
                  }
                </header>
                @if (ir.sapWfId !== null) {
                  <app-wwell-approval-timeline [steps]="ir.chain" [orientation]="'vertical'" />
                }
              </div>
            </aside>
          </div>
        } @else {
          <app-wwell-ir-editor [ir]="ir" [(viewMode)]="editorViewMode" />
        }
      </div>
    } @else {
      <div class="wwell-ir__empty">
        <mat-icon>description</mat-icon>
        <p>Select an integrated requirement to view its details.</p>
      </div>
    }
  </div>
</section>
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir.component.scss

```scss
@use '../shared/wwell-design-tokens' as tokens;
@use '../shared/wwell-material' as wmat;

$navy: tokens.$navy-deep;
$muted: tokens.$muted;
$border: tokens.$border;
$accent: tokens.$accent;

// Matches the WLDA/Land-Affairs siblings' skinny master column. Rows still truncate cleanly
// via `text-overflow: ellipsis` below.
$master-width: 240px;

:host {
  display: block;
  height: 100%;
  background: tokens.$page-bg;
  color: tokens.$navy;
  color-scheme: var(--wp-scheme);
  font-family: tokens.$font-family;
  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
  @include wmat.buttons;
}

/* The project has no global `box-sizing: border-box` reset, so `max-height: 100%` /
 * `width: $master-width` on a padded/bordered box (e.g. `.wwell-ir__master`) only caps its
 * *content* box — padding+border then get added on top, overflowing the intended size. See
 * the identical fix + full rationale in wwell-requests.component.scss. Scoped to this
 * component only (Angular's emulated encapsulation confines the `*` selector to this
 * template), not a global change. */
:host * {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

.wwell-ir {
  display: flex;
  /*
   * `stretch` (the flex default) — both columns always match `.wwell-ir`'s full height. This used
   * to be `flex-start` (each column sized to its own content) specifically to avoid stretching the
   * master column's white card down past its last row when there were only a few of them. But that
   * traded one visual problem for a worse one: with few/zero rows the master card shrank to a tiny
   * stub sitting next to the full-height detail column, an obviously broken-looking mismatch
   * instead of the harmless whitespace it was meant to avoid. `.wwell-ir__master`'s own inner
   * `.wwell-ir__viewport`/`.wwell-ir__status` (flex: 1) absorb the extra height themselves — see
   * their own comments — so the empty space now lives *inside* a properly-sized card instead of
   * showing up as a short card beside a tall one.
   */
  align-items: stretch;
  // One gutter for the whole page: round the edge, between the list and the detail, and (inherited) between the
  // cards inside the detail — a hairline of page showing through, not a margin.
  --wwell-gutter: 0.375rem;

  gap: var(--wwell-gutter);
  height: 100%;
  padding: var(--wwell-gutter);
}

/* ── Master column ────────────────────────────────────────────── */
/* Shadow matches `wwell-ir-new-page`'s card treatment (accent-tinted, two-layer) so the
 * list/detail view and the create-flow page read as one feature, not two depth systems. */
.wwell-ir__master {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.75rem;
  width: $master-width;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  max-height: 100%;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-lg;
  padding: 0.9rem;
  @include tokens.glass-panel;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.wwell-ir__header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 0.5rem;
}

.wwell-ir__heading {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.5rem;
}

.wwell-ir__title {
  font-family: tokens.$font-display;
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 1.08rem;
  font-weight: 650;
  color: $navy;
  letter-spacing: -0.018em;
}

.wwell-ir__count {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  min-width: 22px;
  height: 20px;
  padding: 0 6px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: tokens.alpha($accent, 12%);
  color: $accent;
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  font-weight: 650;
}

/* The action row: New IR takes the width the toggle group leaves, so the pair reads as one control
 * row rather than a stub button beside a stranded pair of icons. Both are 32px tall. */
.wwell-ir__actions {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  width: 100%;
}

.wwell-ir__new-btn {
  flex: 1 1 auto;
  min-width: 0;
  height: 34px;
  padding: 0 14px 0 6px;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-btn;
  font-size: tokens.$text-sm;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.005em;
  // The CTA's own depth token: a top highlight + soft shadow in light, a hairline ring + aurora glow
  // in dark. The gradient fill itself comes from `wmat.buttons`.
  box-shadow: var(--wp-cta-shadow);
  transition:
    transform tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out,
    box-shadow tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out,
    background-color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out;

  &:hover:not(:disabled) {
    transform: translateY(-1px);
  }

  &:active:not(:disabled) {
    transform: translateY(0) scale(0.985);
  }

  &:focus-visible {
    @include tokens.focus-ring;
  }

  &:disabled {
    box-shadow: none;
  }
}

// Icon + label as one centred group. Own wrapper (not Material's projected label span, which scoped
// styles can't reach) so the gap and alignment are ours.
.wwell-ir__new-inner {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 8px;
}

// The plus sits in a small chip — tinted from the label colour so it works on both the ink (light) and
// inverted (dark) fills — and turns a quarter on hover, a cue that this creates something.
.wwell-ir__new-icon {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  width: 20px;
  height: 20px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: tokens.alpha(tokens.$on-cta, 16%);
  transition: transform tokens.$duration-base tokens.$ease-out;

  mat-icon {
    width: 14px;
    height: 14px;
    font-size: 14px;
    line-height: 14px;
    margin: 0;
  }
}

.wwell-ir__new-btn:hover:not(:disabled) .wwell-ir__new-icon {
  transform: rotate(90deg);
}

// Busy state while the server mints the new IR: the ring replaces the plus and the label says why the
// button is inert, instead of just going grey.
.wwell-ir__new-spinner {
  width: 12px;
  height: 12px;
  border: 2px solid currentColor;
  border-right-color: transparent;
  border-radius: 50%;
  animation: wwell-ir-spin 0.7s linear infinite;
}

@keyframes wwell-ir-spin {
  to {
    transform: rotate(360deg);
  }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .wwell-ir__new-btn,
  .wwell-ir__new-icon {
    transition: none;
  }

  .wwell-ir__new-btn:hover:not(:disabled),
  .wwell-ir__new-btn:hover:not(:disabled) .wwell-ir__new-icon {
    transform: none;
  }

  .wwell-ir__new-spinner {
    animation-duration: 1.8s;
  }
}

/* ── Search ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
.wwell-ir__search {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  padding: 0.42rem 0.75rem;
  background: tokens.$surface-alt;
  border: 1px solid $border;
  border-radius: 8px;
  transition: border-color 150ms ease;

  &:focus-within {
    border-color: $accent;
  }
}

.wwell-ir__search-icon {
  width: 18px;
  height: 18px;
  font-size: 18px;
  color: tokens.$subtle;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.wwell-ir__search input {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  border: none;
  outline: none;
  background: transparent;
  font-size: 0.93rem;
  color: $navy;

  &::placeholder {
    color: tokens.$subtle;
  }

  &::-webkit-search-cancel-button {
    cursor: pointer;
  }
}

/* ── Lifecycle toggle + status chips: one skinny, horizontally-scrolling line ────── */
.wwell-ir__filter-bar {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.35rem;
  min-width: 0;
}

.wwell-ir__lifecycle-toggle {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  border: none;
  background: transparent;
  padding: 0;
  line-height: 0;
  color: $accent;
  cursor: pointer;

  mat-icon {
    width: 22px;
    height: 22px;
    font-size: 22px;
  }

  &--inactive {
    color: tokens.$subtle;
  }
}

.wwell-ir__status-chips {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: nowrap;
  gap: 0.3rem;
  min-width: 0;
  overflow-x: auto;
  scrollbar-width: none;

  &::-webkit-scrollbar {
    display: none;
  }
}

.wwell-ir__status-chip {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  border: 1px solid $border;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 0.14rem 0.4rem;
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: $muted;
  cursor: pointer;
  white-space: nowrap;
  transition:
    background 150ms ease,
    border-color 150ms ease,
    color 150ms ease;

  &:hover {
    border-color: tokens.alpha($accent, 40%);
    color: $navy;
  }

  &--active {
    background: tokens.alpha($accent, 12%);
    border-color: tokens.alpha($accent, 35%);
    color: $accent;
  }
}

/* `flex: 1` + centering (not just padded text) so the empty/error state actually fills whatever
 * height `.wwell-ir__master` now stretches to (see that rule's own comment) instead of sitting as
 * a short block pinned to the top with dead space below it. */
.wwell-ir__status {
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 2rem 1rem;
  text-align: center;
  color: $muted;
  font-size: 0.93rem;

  &--error {
    color: var(--wp-danger);
  }
}

/* ── Loading skeleton ─────────────────────────────────────────────
 * Mirrors `.wwell-ir__row`'s shape so the swap into real rows reads as a
 * content refresh, not a layout jump. Shimmer is a CSS keyframe loop
 * (indefinite duration), matching the approval-timeline pulse convention —
 * GSAP is reserved for the finite, one-shot reveal transitions.
 */
.wwell-ir__list--skeleton {
  overflow: hidden;
}

.wwell-ir__skeleton-row {
  cursor: default;
  pointer-events: none;
}

.wwell-ir__skeleton-bar {
  display: block;
  height: 0.6rem;
  border-radius: 4px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--wp-shimmer-a) 25%, var(--wp-shimmer-b) 37%, var(--wp-shimmer-a) 63%);
  background-size: 400% 100%;
  animation: wwell-ir-shimmer 1.4s ease-in-out infinite;

  &--code {
    width: 34%;
  }

  &--badge {
    width: 22%;
    height: 0.9rem;
    border-radius: 999px;
  }

  &--name {
    width: 62%;
    height: 0.7rem;
    margin-top: 0.2rem;
  }

  &--meta {
    width: 48%;
    margin-top: 0.15rem;
  }
}

@keyframes wwell-ir-shimmer {
  0% {
    background-position: 100% 50%;
  }
  100% {
    background-position: -100% 50%;
  }
}

/* ── Row list ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
.wwell-ir__list {
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  list-style: none;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.3rem;
  overflow-y: auto;
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;

  /* Without this, scrolling past the last row chains into `.dashboard__content`
   * (the app shell's content region) — it's `overflow: hidden` for this route, but still
   * has a few px of its own layout slack, just enough for the chained scroll to shove the
   * whole shell up and flash a sliver of bare background above the fixed topbar. `contain`
   * stops the scroll gesture dead at this list's own boundary instead of handing the
   * leftover delta to an ancestor. */
  overscroll-behavior-y: contain;

  scrollbar-width: none;
  -ms-overflow-style: none;

  &::-webkit-scrollbar {
    display: none;
  }
}

/* ── Virtualized row list ─────────────────────────────────────────
 * cdk-virtual-scroll-viewport (see wwell-ir.component.html) only ever keeps the rows currently
 * in/near view mounted in the DOM — everything else exists purely as data, not markup — so a list
 * of hundreds of IRs costs the same to render as a list of ten. It replaces `.wwell-ir__list`
 * (still used as-is above by the plain, never-virtualized loading skeleton) for the real rows.
 * `flex: 1`/`min-height: 0` give it the same "fill the master card" sizing `.wwell-ir__list` had;
 * the rest (hidden scrollbar, scroll-chaining guard) is duplicated rather than shared because the
 * viewport also needs `display: block` (its own required layout mode) undisturbed — see the
 * skeleton `<ul>`'s `display: flex` above, which would otherwise have to be split out anyway.
 */
.wwell-ir__viewport {
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;
  overscroll-behavior-y: contain;
  scrollbar-width: none;
  -ms-overflow-style: none;

  &::-webkit-scrollbar {
    display: none;
  }
}

/* One virtualized row's slot — pinned to exactly `rowItemSize` (wwell-ir.component.ts) so the
 * viewport's index * itemSize positioning math stays exact; `.wwell-ir__row` below fills whatever
 * that leaves after this bottom gap, replacing the plain list's `gap` (meaningless here — the
 * viewport's content wrapper positions rows by absolute offset, not flex layout). */
.wwell-ir__row-item {
  position: relative;
  padding-bottom: 0.3rem;
}

.wwell-ir__row-item .wwell-ir__row {
  height: 100%;
  /* Defensive only: the fixed row-item height above is sized with a small margin over the row's
   * real content height (three text lines + padding + border), so this should never actually
   * clip anything — it just guarantees a rendering-metric surprise stays invisible instead of
   * spilling into the next row. */
  overflow: hidden;
  /* Room for `.wwell-ir__view-btn` below, pinned in the same bottom-right corner — otherwise a
   * long client/well name in `.wwell-ir__row-meta` could run underneath it. */
  padding-right: 2.1rem;
}

/* Pinned bottom-right rather than top-right — `.wwell-ir__row-top` already has the status badge
 * in that corner, and this sits right on top of `.wwell-ir__row`'s own click target. Always
 * visible (not hover-only) so it's reachable on touch, where there's no hover to reveal it. */
.wwell-ir__view-btn {
  position: absolute;
  right: 0.35rem;
  bottom: 0.35rem;
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  padding: 0;
  line-height: 28px;
  color: $muted;

  ::ng-deep .mat-icon {
    font-size: 18px;
    width: 18px;
    height: 18px;
    line-height: 18px;
  }

  &:hover {
    color: $accent;
  }
}

.wwell-ir__row {
  width: 100%;
  text-align: left;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.2rem;
  padding: 0.6rem 0.7rem;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  border-left: 2px solid transparent;
  border-radius: 7px;
  cursor: pointer;
  font: inherit;
  transition:
    background-color 150ms ease,
    border-color 150ms ease,
    box-shadow 150ms ease;

  &:hover {
    background: tokens.$surface-alt;
    box-shadow: var(--wp-shadow-1);
  }

  &--active {
    background: tokens.alpha($accent, 6%);
    border-color: tokens.alpha($accent, 16%);
    border-left-color: $accent;
  }

  &--active:hover {
    box-shadow: none;
  }

  /* Brief pulse while the detail panel's loading shimmer plays (see `selectIr()` /
   * `createPendingSelection`) — gives the clicked row itself a visible "working on it" cue,
   * not just the detail pane. */
  &--pending {
    border-left-color: $accent;
    animation: wwell-ir-row-pending-pulse 0.9s ease-in-out infinite;
  }
}

@keyframes wwell-ir-row-pending-pulse {
  0%,
  100% {
    background: tokens.$surface;
  }
  50% {
    background: tokens.alpha($accent, 7%);
  }
}

.wwell-ir__row-top {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 0.5rem;
}

.wwell-ir__row-code {
  font-size: 0.82rem;
  font-weight: 650;
  color: $navy;
  letter-spacing: 0.01em;
  font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

.wwell-ir__row-name {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 0.88rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: $navy;
  white-space: nowrap;
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
}

.wwell-ir__row-meta {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  color: $muted;
  white-space: nowrap;
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
}

.wwell-ir__badge {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 4px;
  padding: 2px 8px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  font-weight: 600;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.wwell-ir__badge-dot {
  width: 4px;
  height: 4px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

/* ── Detail column ────────────────────────────────────────────── *
 * No longer the scroll container itself — `app-wwell-ir-editor` now bounds its own height
 * and owns its own internal scrolling (only `.ire__body` scrolls; its header/footer stay
 * fixed), so this column just needs to hand it a definite, non-growing height to bound
 * against instead of scrolling on its behalf.
 */
.wwell-ir__detail {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  align-self: stretch;
  max-height: 100%;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.wwell-ir__detail > * {
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;
}

/*
 * Deliberately no background/border/shadow here — that used to wrap the whole detail
 * view in its own white card, which then contained the bento cards from
 * wwell-ir-detail.component.scss, i.e. cards nested inside a card. This is just a
 * padding wrapper now; the neutral page background (`:host`) shows through behind
 * the bento cards themselves, which is the only "card" layer left.
 */
.wwell-ir__detail-panel {
  padding: 0;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  min-height: 0;

  > app-wwell-ir-editor,
  > .wwell-ir__view-layout {
    flex: 1;
    min-height: 0;
  }
}

/* ── View mode — read-only stepper + approval-workflow sidebar, side by side ────── */
.wwell-ir__view-layout {
  display: flex;
  gap: var(--wwell-gutter, 0.9rem);
  min-height: 0;
}

.wwell-ir__view-main {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  min-height: 0;
}

/* Skinny — matches the width `app-wwell-approval-timeline`'s own vertical/compact variant was
 * actually designed around (see that component's "~168px column" comment); the previous 280px
 * here fought that sizing instead of using it. `align-items: stretch` (flex default, from
 * `.wwell-ir__view-layout`) is what makes this match `.wwell-ir__view-main`'s full height. */
.wwell-ir__view-sidebar {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  width: 200px;
  overflow-y: auto;
  scrollbar-width: none;
  -ms-overflow-style: none;

  &::-webkit-scrollbar {
    display: none;
  }
}

/* Same card language as `.irv__header`/`.kv-card` (10px radius, #e8edf4 border, identical
 * shadow) so the sidebar reads as one family with the rest of the view instead of a bolted-on
 * panel — only the top accent strip (chain-status color, replacing the old flat left border) and
 * the richer header below are new. */
.wwell-ir__approval-card {
  position: relative;
  height: 100%;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  min-height: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-lg;
  padding: 1.2rem 0.9rem 1rem;
  @include tokens.glass-panel;
  // The timeline's type, a step up from its compact default (see `--wtl-fs` in the timeline's stylesheet).
  --wtl-fs: #{tokens.$text-xs};
  --wtl-name-fs: #{tokens.$text-sm};

  &::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0 0 auto 0;
    height: 3px;
    background: var(--chain-color, #{$accent});
  }
}

/* The timeline itself grows to fill whatever height the card has (rather than the card
 * shrink-wrapping to a short chain and leaving the border short of `.wwell-ir__view-main`'s
 * height) and scrolls internally if a long chain ever exceeds the available space. */
.wwell-ir__approval-card app-wwell-approval-timeline {
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;
  overflow-y: auto;
  scrollbar-width: none;
  -ms-overflow-style: none;

  &::-webkit-scrollbar {
    display: none;
  }
}

.wwell-ir__approval-head {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  gap: 0.55rem;
  // The rail is narrow and the title wraps: nothing here may push past its edge.
  min-width: 0;
  margin-bottom: 0.9rem;
  padding-bottom: 0.85rem;
  border-bottom: 1px solid tokens.$border-soft;
}

.wwell-ir__approval-icon {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: tokens.$accent-soft;
  color: $accent;

  mat-icon {
    font-size: 16px;
    width: 16px;
    height: 16px;
  }
}

.wwell-ir__approval-head-text {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
}

.wwell-ir__approval-title {
  font-family: tokens.$font-display;
  margin: 0;
  font-size: tokens.$text-md;
  font-weight: 650;
  color: $navy;
  letter-spacing: -0.018em;
}

.wwell-ir__approval-progress {
  margin: 3px 0 0;
  font-size: tokens.$text-xs;
  color: $muted;
}

.wwell-ir__approval-refresh-btn {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  align-self: center;
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  padding: 0;
  line-height: 28px;
  color: $muted;

  ::ng-deep .mat-icon {
    font-size: 18px;
    width: 18px;
    height: 18px;
    line-height: 18px;
  }

  &:hover:not(:disabled) {
    color: $accent;
  }
}

.wwell-ir__empty {
  height: 100%;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  color: $muted;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  border: 1px dashed $border;
  border-radius: 12px;
  min-height: 320px;

  mat-icon {
    width: 32px;
    height: 32px;
    font-size: 32px;
    color: var(--wp-line-strong);
  }

  p {
    margin: 0;
    font-size: 0.96rem;
  }
}

/* ── Responsive ───────────────────────────────────────────────── */
/*
 * `.wwell-ir` becomes the scroll container here (was `height: auto`, no `overflow-y`) —
 * that left NOTHING to scroll: the parent app shell applies `overflow: hidden` to itself for
 * every route under `integrated-requirements` (`.dashboard__content--scroll-locked` in
 * layout.component.scss), on the assumption that the routed page owns its own scrolling.
 * On desktop that contract was satisfied by `.wwell-ir__master`/`.wwell-ir__detail` each
 * scrolling independently side-by-side; stacking them to `height: auto` dropped that contract
 * without anything replacing it, so ~2/3 of a typical detail view (everything below the KPI
 * stat strip) was permanently unreachable — confirmed live: `.dashboard__content`'s
 * `scrollHeight` (2968px) vastly exceeded its `clientHeight` (962px) with `overflow-y: hidden`
 * and no scrollable descendant anywhere in the tree.
 *
 * Fix: keep `.wwell-ir` at the full `height: 100%` it already has on desktop and let it scroll
 * as ONE unit (master list + detail stacked, not two nested independently-scrolling boxes —
 * nested scroll areas are their own bad-mobile-UX problem). `app-wwell-ir-detail`'s own bounded
 * internal-scroll layout (see wwell-ir-detail.component.scss) is a desktop-only concern — undone
 * here via `overflow: visible` so its content just flows at natural height into this shared
 * mobile scroll instead of trying to open its own nested scrollbox.
 */
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .wwell-ir {
    flex-direction: column;
    overflow-y: auto;
    /* Same scroll-chaining guard as `.wwell-ir__list`/`.wwell-ir__detail` — this element
     * takes over as the single scroll container at this breakpoint, so it needs the same
     * protection against handing overscroll off to `.dashboard__content`. */
    overscroll-behavior-y: contain;
  }

  .wwell-ir__master {
    width: 100%;
    max-height: none;
  }

  .wwell-ir__detail {
    max-height: none;
    overflow: visible;
  }

  .wwell-ir__view-layout {
    flex-direction: column;
  }

  .wwell-ir__view-sidebar {
    width: 100%;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 640px) {
  .wwell-ir {
    padding: 1rem;
  }
}

/* Printing needs the editor's full, unclipped height — `.wwell-ir__detail`'s `overflow: hidden`
 * above exists only to bound the on-screen internal-scroll layout, and would otherwise cut
 * content off at whatever fit the last viewport height before print was triggered. */
@media print {
  .wwell-ir__detail,
  .wwell-ir__detail-panel,
  .wwell-ir__detail-panel > app-wwell-ir-editor {
    display: block;
    flex: none;
    max-height: none;
    overflow: visible;
  }
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir.component.ts

```ts
import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  ElementRef,
  HostListener,
  OnDestroy,
  OnInit,
  ViewChild,
  computed,
  effect,
  inject,
  signal,
} from '@angular/core';
import { FormBuilder, FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { ScrollingModule } from '@angular/cdk/scrolling';
import { MatButtonModule } from '@angular/material/button';
import { MatIconModule } from '@angular/material/icon';
import { Observable, from, of, switchMap, take } from 'rxjs';
import { ActionConfirmDialogService } from '@shared/components/action-confirm-dialog/action-confirm-dialog.service';
import { NotificationService } from '@shared/components/notification/notification.service';
import { ComponentCanDeactivate } from '@guards/unsaved-changes.guard';
import { ApprovalChainStep } from '@models/wwell-planning/wwell-planning.model';
import { IntegratedRequirement, IntegratedRequirementStatus } from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';
import { buildDetailRevealTimeline } from '../shared/dynamic-form/detail-reveal.animation';
import { playIfMotionAllowed } from '../shared/dynamic-form/gsap-motion.util';
import { buildListRowsRevealTimeline } from '../shared/dynamic-form/list-reveal.animation';
import { WwellApprovalTimelineComponent } from '../shared/wwell-approvals/wwell-approval-timeline/wwell-approval-timeline.component';
import { chainCompletionColor } from '../shared/wwell-approvals/wwell-approvals.config';
import { WwellCollapseAnimateDirective } from '../shared/wwell-collapse-animate/wwell-collapse-animate.directive';
import { WWELL_LOAD_DELAY_MS } from '@models/wwell-planning/wwell-planning-loading.constants';
import { WwellDetailLoadingComponent } from '../shared/wwell-detail-loading/wwell-detail-loading.component';
import { createPendingSelection } from '../shared/wwell-pending-selection.util';
import { WwellIrStore } from './store/wwell-ir.store';
import { WwellIrEditorComponent, WwellIrEditorViewMode } from './wwell-ir-editor/wwell-ir-editor.component';
import { WwellIrEditorLoadingComponent } from './wwell-ir-editor-loading/wwell-ir-editor-loading.component';
import { WwellIrViewComponent, WwellIrViewTab } from './wwell-ir-view/wwell-ir-view.component';
import { buildWwellDynamicForm } from '../shared/dynamic-form/wwell-dynamic-form.builder';
import { buildIntegratedRequirementFromPayload } from './wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-draft.util';
import { mapIrFormValueToSubmission } from './wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-form.mapper';
import { WWELL_IR_FORM_SCHEMA } from './wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-form.schema';
import { WwellIrFormRawValue } from './wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-form.types';
import { WWELL_IR_STATUS_META, hasKnownIrStatus } from './wwell-ir.config';

/** Which surface WwellIrEditorComponent's slot in the detail panel currently shows for the
 *  selected record — 'edit' is the existing view-with-inline-edit surface, 'view' is the
 *  read-only stepper (WwellIrViewComponent). Deliberately independent of *which* record is
 *  selected (see `viewIr`/`setDetailMode` below) so browsing other rows while viewing one keeps
 *  showing each newly-selected record in view mode too, instead of bouncing back to edit on
 *  every click. */
type WwellIrDetailMode = 'edit' | 'view';

const SKELETON_ROW_COUNT = 6;

// Fixed row height the virtual-scroll viewport needs up front to compute which rows are in view
// (see cdk-virtual-scroll-viewport's `itemSize`) — every `.wwell-ir__row-item` is pinned to
// exactly this height in wwell-ir.component.scss so the two never drift out of sync (a mismatch
// shows up as overlapping or gapped rows, since the viewport positions each one by index * itemSize).
const WWELL_IR_ROW_ITEM_SIZE_PX = 84;

/** Coarse lifecycle split for the master list's top toggle — "Active" is every status before the
 *  record leaves the author's hands, "Inactive" is the one terminal status. Kept separate from the
 *  finer-grained `statusFilter` chips below (which only make sense to show *within* Active, since
 *  Inactive only ever has one possible status). */
type WwellIrLifecycleFilter = 'active' | 'inactive';

const WWELL_IR_ACTIVE_STATUSES: readonly IntegratedRequirementStatus[] = [
  'draft',
  'in-progress',
  'submit-for-review',
  'in-review',
];

@Component({
  selector: 'app-wwell-ir',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [
    FormsModule,
    ScrollingModule,
    MatButtonModule,
    MatIconModule,
    WwellIrEditorComponent,
    WwellIrViewComponent,
    WwellApprovalTimelineComponent,
    WwellDetailLoadingComponent,
    WwellIrEditorLoadingComponent,
    WwellCollapseAnimateDirective,
  ],
  templateUrl: './wwell-ir.component.html',
  styleUrl: './wwell-ir.component.scss',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class WwellIrComponent implements OnInit, OnDestroy, ComponentCanDeactivate {
  protected readonly store = inject(WwellIrStore);
  private readonly fb = inject(FormBuilder);
  private readonly notify = inject(NotificationService);
  private readonly confirmDialog = inject(ActionConfirmDialogService);

  protected readonly isLoading = this.store.irsLoading;
  protected readonly loadError = this.store.irsError;
  protected readonly isLoadingDetail = this.store.irDetailLoading;

  protected readonly searchTerm = signal('');
  // ir_seq is the real primary key once an IR is saved (see integrated-requirement.model.ts) —
  // selection is keyed on it directly, not the display-only `code`/`id` string, so it can be
  // handed straight to loadIrDetail() below without a lookup.
  protected readonly selectedIrSeq = signal<number | null>(null);

  /** Sticky across selection changes — see WwellIrDetailMode's own doc comment. */
  protected readonly detailMode = signal<WwellIrDetailMode>('edit');

  /** Sticky for the same reason as `detailMode`: the editor is destroyed and rebuilt on a row
   *  switch (loading skeleton) and on the view→edit toggle, so a mode held on the editor itself
   *  snapped back to its default each time. Two-way bound to the editor's `viewMode` model. */
  protected readonly editorViewMode = signal<WwellIrEditorViewMode>('timeline');

  /** Sticky for the same reason as `editorViewMode`, for the read-only side: pick Well Design on
   *  one record and it stays selected when you open the next one, instead of snapping back to
   *  Details. Two-way bound to the view's `activeTab` model. */
  protected readonly viewTab = signal<WwellIrViewTab>('details');

  protected readonly lifecycleFilter = signal<WwellIrLifecycleFilter>('active');
  protected readonly statusFilter = signal<IntegratedRequirementStatus | 'all'>('all');
  // Sub-filter chips shown only in the Active bucket — Inactive is exactly one status
  // (approved), so there's nothing left to narrow further once that's selected.
  protected readonly statusChips: readonly (IntegratedRequirementStatus | 'all')[] = ['all', ...WWELL_IR_ACTIVE_STATUSES];

  // Scoped by the lifecycle toggle only (not the status chip or search term) so chip counts stay
  // stable while the user narrows further — each chip shows how many rows *it* would reveal, not
  // how many remain after some other filter already trimmed the set.
  private readonly lifecycleScopedIrs = computed(() => {
    const all = this.store.irs();
    return this.lifecycleFilter() === 'inactive'
      ? all.filter(ir => ir.status === 'approved')
      : all.filter(ir => WWELL_IR_ACTIVE_STATUSES.includes(ir.status));
  });

  protected readonly rows = computed(() => {
    const term = this.searchTerm().trim().toLowerCase();
    const status = this.statusFilter();
    const scoped = this.lifecycleScopedIrs().filter(ir => status === 'all' || ir.status === status);
    if (!term) return scoped;

    return scoped.filter(
      ir =>
        ir.code.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
        ir.requestReference.wellName.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
        (ir.requestReference.linkedRequestId ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
        ir.requestReference.client.toLowerCase().includes(term),
    );
  });

  // A brand-new IR created via createNewIr() below — never in `store.irs()` (no backend call has
  // happened yet, so there's nothing to add it to) and takes priority over `selectedIrSeq` here.
  // Cleared once its first save actually persists it (see the creatingIr effect below) or the
  // user selects an existing row instead (see selectIrRow), discarding whatever was unsaved.
  protected readonly pendingDraftIr = signal<IntegratedRequirement | null>(null);

  // Deliberately looks up the *unfiltered* store list (not `rows()`) so narrowing the search
  // never drops the current selection just because it fell outside the filtered results.
  // Water Well Name/Coordinates/Surface Elevation arrive on the record itself once its detail has
  // been fetched (see mergeIrDetailBlocksIntoIntegratedRequirement's exad_rcd_prewap block) — no
  // separate hydration pass needed here.
  protected readonly selectedIr = computed<IntegratedRequirement | null>(
    () => this.pendingDraftIr() ?? this.store.irs().find(ir => ir.irSeq === this.selectedIrSeq()) ?? null,
  );

  // Identity/order of the *visible set* of rows, not the row objects themselves — selecting a row
  // patches that one IR's `chain` in place (loadIrDetail / loadIrApprovalTimeline both replace
  // `irs` with a new array reference), which would otherwise look identical to "the result set changed" to a plain
  // `rows()` reference check below and replay the list-reveal animation across every row on every
  // click. This string only changes on an actual add/remove/reorder/filter.
  protected readonly rowsSignature = computed(() => this.rows().map(ir => ir.irSeq ?? ir.id).join('|'));

  protected readonly statusMeta = WWELL_IR_STATUS_META;
  protected readonly hasKnownStatus = hasKnownIrStatus;

  protected readonly skeletonRows = Array.from({ length: SKELETON_ROW_COUNT }, (_, index) => index);

  // Bound to the viewport's [itemSize] and each row-item's fixed height in the template — see
  // WWELL_IR_ROW_ITEM_SIZE_PX's own comment for why these must stay pinned together.
  protected readonly rowItemSize = WWELL_IR_ROW_ITEM_SIZE_PX;

  private readonly pendingSelection = createPendingSelection<number>(WWELL_LOAD_DELAY_MS);
  protected readonly pendingId = this.pendingSelection.pendingId;
  // Shows the loading UI for the cosmetic selection transition *and* the real detail fetch it
  // now kicks off — otherwise the shimmer could resolve before the fetch does, briefly exposing
  // stale summary-only fields (blank casing/tops) before the enriched detail lands. Deliberately
  // excludes the approval-timeline flag: that fetch never blocks this panel, including the one
  // real fetch a brand-new record needs right after its first save (see the creatingIr effect
  // below) — re-showing the whole panel as a loading skeleton the instant after a save completes
  // would undo the "seamless save" this effect exists to provide.
  protected readonly isSelecting = computed(() => this.pendingSelection.isSelecting() || this.isLoadingDetail());

  @ViewChild('detailHost') private readonly detailHost?: ElementRef<HTMLElement>;
  // `{ read: ElementRef }` is required now that #listHost sits on <cdk-virtual-scroll-viewport>
  // (a component) — an unqualified @ViewChild query on a component host resolves to that
  // component instance by default, not its native element, which used to be the (unstated)
  // default back when this ref lived on a plain <ul>.
  @ViewChild('listHost', { read: ElementRef }) private readonly listHost?: ElementRef<HTMLElement>;
  // Only rendered while detailMode() === 'edit' (see the template's `@else` branch) — undefined
  // whenever the detail panel is showing the read-only view stepper or the empty state, which is
  // exactly when there's nothing open to lose (see canDeactivate()'s guard below).
  @ViewChild(WwellIrEditorComponent) private readonly editorRef?: WwellIrEditorComponent;
  private previousSelectedIrSeq: number | null = null;
  private wasCreatingIr = false;
  private wasDeletingIr = false;
  private wasSubmittingForReview = false;

  constructor() {
    effect(() => {
      const message = this.store.createIrError();
      if (message) this.notify.error(message);
    });

    // Reports the real outcome of a save once it settles — the editor deliberately shows nothing
    // at click time (see saveMergedRaw), since the request is still in flight there (its own Save
    // button shows a spinner instead — see wwell-ir-editor.component.html). A failure is already
    // toasted by the createIrError effect above, so only success is announced here.
    //
    // On success, a save that completes while pendingDraftIr is set means *this* draft just got
    // its first real backend row — swap from the local-only draft over to the now-real,
    // ir_seq-keyed record. By the time `creatingIr` flips false, store.createIr has already
    // waited out a real follow-up GET and merged it into `irs` (no optimistic echo — see that
    // method's own doc comment), so this is already the confirmed, persisted record. Future saves
    // then UPDATE instead of re-INSERTing.
    effect(() => {
      const creating = this.store.creatingIr();
      const settled = this.wasCreatingIr && !creating;
      this.wasCreatingIr = creating;
      if (!settled || this.store.createIrError()) return;

      const wasNewIr = this.pendingDraftIr() !== null;
      const savedIrSeq = wasNewIr ? this.store.draftIrSeq() : this.selectedIrSeq();

      if (wasNewIr && savedIrSeq !== null) {
        this.pendingDraftIr.set(null);
        // Deliberately sets `previousSelectedIrSeq` before selecting, so the "selection changed"
        // effect below no-ops for this transition instead of calling loadIrDetail (redundant —
        // store.createIr's own follow-up GET already covers it — and it would flip
        // isLoadingDetail(), swapping the whole detail panel to the loading skeleton right after
        // the user just watched this exact record save).
        this.previousSelectedIrSeq = savedIrSeq;
        this.selectedIrSeq.set(savedIrSeq);
        // The real approval chain (if any) is fetched from inside store.createIr itself, once its
        // own follow-up GET resolves a sapWfId — no separate call needed here.
        // A brand-new record has an ir_seq for the first time here, so this is the first moment
        // its attachment slots can be fetched (and the first moment uploads become possible).
        this.store.loadIrAttachments(savedIrSeq);
        // Anything the user attached while this was still an unsaved draft had nowhere to go until
        // now — send it against the id the save just assigned.
        this.store.uploadPendingAttachments(savedIrSeq);
      }

      const savedCode = savedIrSeq !== null ? this.store.irs().find(ir => ir.irSeq === savedIrSeq)?.code : undefined;
      const subject = savedCode ?? 'Integrated requirement';
      this.notify.info(wasNewIr ? `${subject} created successfully.` : `${subject} updated successfully.`);
    });

    effect(() => {
      const message = this.store.sapTriggerError();
      if (message) this.notify.error(message);
    });

    // Reports the outcome of a Submit for Review once the whole three-step flow settles (mint
    // workflow id → SAP /trigger → stamp sap_wf_id/sap_wf_status on the header). Same settled-edge
    // shape as the save effect above, and for the same reason: nothing is announced at click time
    // because the request is still in flight — the editor's own button shows a spinner until then.
    //
    // Without this the success path was silent: the flow completes in ~200ms locally and its only
    // visible trace was the Submit for Review button disappearing (the status leaves
    // WWELL_IR_SUBMITTABLE_STATUSES, so canSubmitForReview drops it), which reads as the click
    // having done nothing. A failure is already toasted by the sapTriggerError effect above, so
    // only success is announced here.
    effect(() => {
      const submitting = this.store.updatingIrStatus();
      const settled = this.wasSubmittingForReview && !submitting;
      this.wasSubmittingForReview = submitting;
      if (!settled || this.store.sapTriggerError()) return;

      const submittedCode = this.store.irs().find(ir => ir.irSeq === this.selectedIrSeq())?.code;
      this.notify.info(`${submittedCode ?? 'Integrated requirement'} submitted for review.`);
    });

    effect(() => {
      const message = this.store.irDetailError();
      if (message) this.notify.error(message);
    });

    effect(() => {
      const message = this.store.irApprovalTimelineError();
      if (message) this.notify.error(message);
    });

    effect(() => {
      const message = this.store.deleteIrError();
      if (message) this.notify.error(message);
    });

    // Reports success once a delete settles — the editor's own Delete IR button shows a spinner
    // while in flight (see wwell-ir-editor.component.html's isDeleting()), so nothing needs
    // announcing at click time. A failure is already toasted by the effect above.
    effect(() => {
      const deleting = this.store.deletingIr();
      const settled = this.wasDeletingIr && !deleting;
      this.wasDeletingIr = deleting;
      if (!settled || this.store.deleteIrError()) return;

      this.notify.info('Integrated requirement deleted.');

      // The deleted record is already gone from store.irs() by this point (WwellIrStore.deleteIr
      // removes it on success) — if it was the one open, fall back to null so the "default to
      // first visible record" effect below picks whatever the list now shows first (or leaves the
      // panel empty if nothing remains), the same path a fresh session start already uses.
      if (this.selectedIrSeq() !== null && !this.store.irs().some(ir => ir.irSeq === this.selectedIrSeq())) {
        this.selectedIrSeq.set(null);
        this.previousSelectedIrSeq = null;
      }
    });

    // Default to the first *visible* record once the list loads — mirrors the reference app,
    // which always has something selected in the detail pane rather than starting on an empty
    // state. Reads `rows()` (lifecycle/status/search-scoped), not the raw store list, so the
    // default selection is never a row the Active/Inactive toggle is currently hiding. Skipped
    // while a pendingDraftIr is open — selectedIrSeq is intentionally left null throughout
    // drafting (the draft isn't in `rows()`), and this effect would otherwise immediately fetch
    // and select an unrelated real record's detail out from under the still-open draft.
    effect(() => {
      if (this.pendingDraftIr()) return;
      const firstIrSeq = this.rows()[0]?.irSeq;
      if (firstIrSeq != null && this.selectedIrSeq() === null) {
        this.selectedIrSeq.set(firstIrSeq);
      }
    });

    // Selection changed → play the reveal transition and fetch that record's real detail
    // (casing/tops/hydrogeology) by ir_seq, its primary key.
    effect(() => {
      const irSeq = this.selectedIrSeq();
      if (irSeq === null || irSeq === this.previousSelectedIrSeq) return;

      const host = this.detailHost?.nativeElement;
      if (host) playIfMotionAllowed(buildDetailRevealTimeline(host));
      this.previousSelectedIrSeq = irSeq;
      this.store.loadIrDetail(irSeq);
      // The approval chain is fetched from inside store.loadIrDetail itself, once that GET's own
      // exad_gwd_ir_header block resolves the record's sapWfId (only the detail fetch carries it —
      // see wwell-ir-summary.mapper.ts) — no separate call needed here.
      // Also its own fetch: attachments are files on the backend's filesystem keyed by ir_seq, not
      // exad_gwd_ir_* columns, so they can't ride along in the detail blocks above.
      this.store.loadIrAttachments(irSeq);
      // Files held for an abandoned New IR belong to that draft alone — drop them rather than let
      // them surface on whichever record was just selected. (This effect deliberately no-ops on the
      // save transition, see the comment above, so it can't discard files mid-upload.)
      this.store.clearPendingAttachments();
      // Re-fetched on every selection, not just once at ngOnInit — this backs the editor's own
      // "Link to Request" dropdown and its live auto-populate-on-pick (see
      // wwell-ir-editor.component.ts's applyLinkedRequestWell / wwell-ir-linked-well.util.ts),
      // used when the user picks a NEW link on the opened record. Eligibility can change
      // server-side between selections in a long-lived session (another user initiates an IR for
      // a well, a well newly becomes eligible...) — no cached/one-time snapshot, always the
      // current server state for whichever record is being opened.
      this.store.loadLinkedRequestWells();
    });

    // Re-plays whenever the visible rows actually change (initial load finishing, or the
    // search term narrowing/widening the result set) — keyed on `rowsSignature()` (identity/order
    // only), not `rows()` itself, so a selected row's detail/timeline arriving in place never
    // replays this across the whole list — see `rowsSignature`'s comment above.
    effect(() => {
      const signature = this.rowsSignature();
      if (this.isLoading() || signature === '') return;

      const host = this.listHost?.nativeElement;
      if (!host) return;

      const items = Array.from(host.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.wwell-ir__row'));
      if (items.length) playIfMotionAllowed(buildListRowsRevealTimeline(items));
    });
  }

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.store.loadIrs();
    // Started here (not lazily in the editor once a record is selected) so the "Link to Request"
    // dropdown/picker (see loadLinkedRequestWells's other call site, on selection change below)
    // has options ready by the time the first record's editor renders, instead of racing it.
    this.store.loadLinkedRequestWells();
    // Geologist Contact picker's option list — stable reference data, unlike linkedRequestWells
    // above, so it's only loaded once here rather than re-fetched on every IR selection.
    this.store.loadGwdOpsMembers();
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.pendingSelection.clear();
  }

  protected onSearchChange(term: string): void {
    this.searchTerm.set(term);
  }

  /** Switching lifecycle bucket resets the status chip back to "All" — a chip selected in the old
   *  bucket (e.g. "In Review") would otherwise carry over into Inactive, where it can never match
   *  anything, silently showing an empty list. */
  protected setLifecycleFilter(filter: WwellIrLifecycleFilter): void {
    this.lifecycleFilter.set(filter);
    this.statusFilter.set('all');
  }

  protected toggleLifecycleFilter(): void {
    this.setLifecycleFilter(this.lifecycleFilter() === 'active' ? 'inactive' : 'active');
  }

  protected setStatusFilter(status: IntegratedRequirementStatus | 'all'): void {
    this.statusFilter.set(status);
  }

  // Chips only ever render within the Active lifecycle bucket (see the template's
  // `@if (lifecycleFilter() === 'active')` gate) — "All" here already only ever means "every
  // active-status record", never the inactive/approved ones the toggle hides. Labeling it
  // "Active" instead of the bare, easy-to-misread "All" makes that scope explicit.
  protected statusChipLabel(status: IntegratedRequirementStatus | 'all'): string {
    return status === 'all' ? 'Active' : this.statusMeta[status].label;
  }

  protected statusChipCount(status: IntegratedRequirementStatus | 'all'): number {
    const scoped = this.lifecycleScopedIrs();
    return status === 'all' ? scoped.length : scoped.filter(ir => ir.status === status).length;
  }

  protected selectIr(irSeq: number): void {
    this.guardNavigation(() => this.performSelectIr(irSeq));
  }

  /** Selecting a real row discards whatever unsaved local draft was open — nothing was ever
   *  persisted for it, so there's nothing to preserve (see guardNavigation()'s own comment for why
   *  a section *with* real edits is asked about before this ever runs). */
  private performSelectIr(irSeq: number): void {
    this.pendingDraftIr.set(null);
    this.pendingSelection.select(irSeq, this.selectedIrSeq(), resolved => this.selectedIrSeq.set(resolved));
  }

  /** Normalizes the model's `irSeq?: number | null` to a plain `number | null` for the template
   *  (which can't express an `== null` check under the eqeqeq lint rule). */
  protected rowIrSeq(ir: IntegratedRequirement): number | null {
    return ir.irSeq ?? null;
  }

  protected selectIrRow(ir: IntegratedRequirement): void {
    const irSeq = this.rowIrSeq(ir);
    if (irSeq !== null) this.selectIr(irSeq);
  }

  /** The row's "view" (eye) icon — selects the record exactly like selectIrRow above, plus
   *  switches the detail panel to the read-only stepper. Staying in view mode afterwards (rather
   *  than resetting on every selection change) is what lets the master list double as a way to
   *  browse several records' read-only views in a row, without an extra click back into view
   *  mode each time. */
  protected viewIr(irSeq: number): void {
    // Switching to the read-only stepper destroys this editor instance (see the template's
    // `@if (detailMode() === 'view')` branch) even when `irSeq` is the record already open here —
    // so this needs the same guard as an actual selection change, not just performSelectIr's own
    // "discard a blank draft" case.
    this.guardNavigation(() => {
      this.detailMode.set('view');
      this.performSelectIr(irSeq);
    });
  }

  /** WwellIrViewComponent's own "Edit" button — switches the currently-open record's detail
   *  panel back to the inline-edit surface. */
  protected setDetailMode(mode: WwellIrDetailMode): void {
    this.detailMode.set(mode);
  }

  /** `cdkVirtualFor`'s identity function — same identity rule as the old `@for`'s `track`
   *  expression, just spelled as a function since `*cdkVirtualFor` (a plain structural directive,
   *  not the `@for` built-in control flow) takes `trackBy` instead of an inline `track` expression. */
  protected trackByIrRow(_index: number, ir: IntegratedRequirement): number | string {
    return ir.irSeq ?? ir.id;
  }

  /** Opens a blank draft in the detail pane and selects it — purely client-side, no backend call,
   *  since there's nothing to save yet (see this class's `pendingDraftIr`). WwellIrEditorComponent
   *  binds to it exactly like any persisted record; its first real Save (a section's own, or the
   *  global "Save" button) is what actually creates the backend row. */
  protected createNewIr(): void {
    this.guardNavigation(() => this.performCreateNewIr());
  }

  private performCreateNewIr(): void {
    const raw = buildWwellDynamicForm(WWELL_IR_FORM_SCHEMA, this.fb).getRawValue() as WwellIrFormRawValue;
    const { payload } = mapIrFormValueToSubmission(raw, 'draft');
    const draft = buildIntegratedRequirementFromPayload(payload, { id: 'draft', code: 'New IR', irSeq: null });
    // Start the new draft with empty slots — a previous draft's held files must not carry over.
    this.store.clearPendingAttachments();

    this.pendingDraftIr.set(draft);
    this.selectedIrSeq.set(null);
    this.previousSelectedIrSeq = null;
    // A brand-new draft has nothing committed to view read-only yet — always open it for editing,
    // regardless of whatever mode the previously-open record was left in.
    this.detailMode.set('edit');

    const host = this.detailHost?.nativeElement;
    if (host) playIfMotionAllowed(buildDetailRevealTimeline(host));
  }

  protected chainBorderColor(chain: readonly ApprovalChainStep[]): string {
    return chainCompletionColor(chain);
  }

  /** Powers the approval-workflow sidebar's "N of M approved" progress line (view mode only). */
  protected approvedStepCount(chain: readonly ApprovalChainStep[]): number {
    return chain.filter(step => step.status === 'approved').length;
  }

  /** Manual "Refresh" action on the Approval Workflow sidebar — pulls SAP's real current
   *  status/chain for the open record on demand, without needing to reselect it. A no-op for a
   *  record with no `sapWfId` (never submitted, so there is nothing in SAP to ask about) or no
   *  `irSeq` (a local-only draft) — the store's own `refreshIrApprovalStatus` guards the same
   *  case, but checking here too keeps the button itself disabled rather than silently doing
   *  nothing on click. */
  protected refreshApprovalStatus(ir: IntegratedRequirement): void {
    if (ir.irSeq == null || ir.sapWfId == null) return;
    this.store.refreshIrApprovalStatus(ir.irSeq, ir.sapWfId);
  }

  // ── Unsaved-changes guard — backs three separate "leaving the currently-open record" paths with
  // one prompt: switching the selected row / opening "New IR" / clicking the view (eye) icon (all
  // via guardNavigation below), and a real Angular route change away from this page entirely (via
  // canDeactivate(), wired up as the 'integrated-requirements' route's CanDeactivate guard — see
  // app.routes.ts and unsaved-changes.guard.ts). Browser tab close/refresh is covered separately by
  // the beforeunload listener below, which can't run this same async dialog flow.

  /** Router entry point (ComponentCanDeactivate) — resolves `true` once it's safe to actually leave
   *  the route: immediately if nothing is unsaved, after a confirmed Discard, or after a confirmed
   *  Save has settled. `false` means stay (Cancel, or a save that failed). */
  canDeactivate(): Observable<boolean> {
    const editor = this.editorRef;
    if (!editor?.hasUnsavedChanges()) return of(true);

    return this.confirmDialog
      .openWithResult({
        title: 'Unsaved changes',
        subtitle: 'This Integrated Requirement has section edits that haven’t been saved yet. Save them before leaving?',
        confirmLabel: 'Save & Leave',
        discardLabel: 'Discard Changes',
        cancelLabel: 'Stay Here',
        severity: 'warning',
      })
      .pipe(
        switchMap(result => {
          if (result === 'cancel') return of(false);
          if (result === 'discard') {
            editor.discardUnsavedChanges();
            return of(true);
          }
          return from(editor.saveUnsavedChangesAndWait());
        }),
      );
  }

  /** In-page equivalent of canDeactivate() — same prompt/save/discard flow, but for switching what
   *  this page itself shows (selecting another row, opening a blank "New IR", or the view-icon's
   *  mode switch) rather than leaving the route. `action` only runs once it's actually safe to
   *  proceed. */
  private guardNavigation(action: () => void): void {
    this.canDeactivate()
      .pipe(take(1))
      .subscribe(canLeave => {
        if (canLeave) action();
      });
  }

  /** Native browser navigation (tab close, refresh, typed URL) bypasses the Router entirely, so
   *  canDeactivate() never runs for it — this is the one place that has to use the browser's own
   *  confirmation prompt instead (no custom dialog is possible here; per spec, `returnValue` must be
   *  set and the browser supplies its own generic copy). */
  @HostListener('window:beforeunload', ['$event'])
  protected onBeforeUnload(event: BeforeUnloadEvent): void {
    if (!this.editorRef?.hasUnsavedChanges()) return;
    event.preventDefault();
    event.returnValue = '';
  }
}
```

```scss
@use '../shared/wwell-design-tokens' as tokens;
@use '../shared/wwell-material' as wmat;

$navy: tokens.$navy-deep;
$muted: tokens.$muted;
$border: tokens.$border;
$accent: tokens.$accent;

// Matches the WLDA sibling's skinny master column. Rows still truncate cleanly via
// `text-overflow: ellipsis` below.
$master-width: 240px;

:host {
  display: block;
  height: 100%;
  background: tokens.$page-bg;
  color: tokens.$navy;
  color-scheme: var(--wp-scheme);
  font-family: tokens.$font-family;
  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
  @include wmat.buttons;
}

/* The project has no global `box-sizing: border-box` reset, so `max-height: 100%` /
 * `width: $master-width` on a padded/bordered box (e.g. `.wla__master`) only caps its
 * *content* box — padding+border then get added on top, overflowing the intended size. See
 * the identical fix + full rationale in wwell-requests.component.scss. Scoped to this
 * component only (Angular's emulated encapsulation confines the `*` selector to this
 * template), not a global change. */
:host * {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

.wla {
  display: flex;
  /* Not `stretch` (the flex default): with only a handful of rows, stretching the master
   * column to match `.wla`'s full 100%-height would force its white card down to fill the
   * whole page, leaving dead empty space below the last row. Each column sizes to its own
   * content instead, capped by `max-height: 100%` — `.wla__detail` opts back into filling the
   * available height via `align-self: stretch` since its content is meant to. */
  align-items: flex-start;
  gap: 1.25rem;
  height: 100%;
  padding: 1.5rem;
}

/* ── Master column ────────────────────────────────────────────── */
.wla__master {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.75rem;
  width: $master-width;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  max-height: 100%;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-lg;
  padding: 0.9rem;
  @include tokens.glass-panel;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.wla__header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 0.5rem;
}

.wla__heading {
  font-family: tokens.$font-display;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.5rem;
}

.wla__title {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 1.08rem;
  font-weight: 650;
  color: $navy;
  letter-spacing: -0.018em;
}

.wla__count {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  min-width: 20px;
  height: 18px;
  padding: 0 5px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: tokens.alpha($accent, 12%);
  color: $accent;
  font-size: 0.76rem;
  font-weight: 650;
}

.wla__new-btn {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 4px;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  border-radius: 7px;
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 0.84rem;
  padding: 0 10px;
  box-shadow: none;

  mat-icon {
    width: 15px;
    height: 15px;
    font-size: 15px;
  }
}

/* ── Search ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
.wla__search {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  padding: 0.42rem 0.75rem;
  background: tokens.$surface-alt;
  border: 1px solid $border;
  border-radius: 8px;
  transition: border-color 150ms ease;

  &:focus-within {
    border-color: $accent;
  }
}

.wla__search-icon {
  width: 18px;
  height: 18px;
  font-size: 18px;
  color: tokens.$subtle;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.wla__search input {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  border: none;
  outline: none;
  background: transparent;
  font-size: 0.93rem;
  color: $navy;

  &::placeholder {
    color: tokens.$subtle;
  }

  &::-webkit-search-cancel-button {
    cursor: pointer;
  }
}

.wla__status {
  padding: 2rem 1rem;
  text-align: center;
  color: $muted;
  font-size: 0.93rem;

  &--error {
    color: var(--wp-danger);
  }
}

/* ── Loading skeleton ─────────────────────────────────────────── */
.wla__list--skeleton {
  overflow: hidden;
}

.wla__skeleton-row {
  cursor: default;
  pointer-events: none;
}

.wla__skeleton-bar {
  display: block;
  height: 0.6rem;
  border-radius: 4px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--wp-shimmer-a) 25%, var(--wp-shimmer-b) 37%, var(--wp-shimmer-a) 63%);
  background-size: 400% 100%;
  animation: wla-shimmer 1.4s ease-in-out infinite;

  &--code {
    width: 34%;
  }

  &--badge {
    width: 22%;
    height: 0.9rem;
    border-radius: 999px;
  }

  &--name {
    width: 62%;
    height: 0.7rem;
    margin-top: 0.2rem;
  }

  &--meta {
    width: 48%;
    margin-top: 0.15rem;
  }
}

@keyframes wla-shimmer {
  0% {
    background-position: 100% 50%;
  }
  100% {
    background-position: -100% 50%;
  }
}

/* ── Row list ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
.wla__list {
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  list-style: none;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.3rem;
  overflow-y: auto;
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;

  /* Skinny hidden scrollbar — the rows are the only chrome here; a visible track/thumb
   * would just be more noise on a narrow 240px column. */
  scrollbar-width: none;
  -ms-overflow-style: none;

  &::-webkit-scrollbar {
    display: none;
  }
}

.wla__row {
  width: 100%;
  text-align: left;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.2rem;
  padding: 0.55rem 0.65rem;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  border-left: 2px solid transparent;
  border-radius: 7px;
  cursor: pointer;
  font: inherit;
  transition:
    background-color 150ms ease,
    border-color 150ms ease,
    box-shadow 150ms ease;

  &:hover {
    background: tokens.$surface-alt;
    box-shadow: var(--wp-shadow-1);
  }

  &--active {
    background: tokens.alpha($accent, 6%);
    border-color: tokens.alpha($accent, 16%);
    border-left-color: $accent;
  }

  &--active:hover {
    box-shadow: none;
  }

  /* Brief pulse while the detail-loading shimmer plays, so the row the user just clicked
   * stays visually "live" instead of looking unresponsive during the short select-loading beat. */
  &--pending {
    animation: wla-row-pending-pulse 0.9s ease-in-out infinite;
  }
}

@keyframes wla-row-pending-pulse {
  0%,
  100% {
    background: tokens.$surface;
  }
  50% {
    background: tokens.alpha($accent, 7%);
  }
}

.wla__row-top {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 0.5rem;
}

.wla__row-code {
  font-size: 0.82rem;
  font-weight: 650;
  color: $navy;
  letter-spacing: 0.01em;
  font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

.wla__row-name {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 0.88rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: $navy;
  white-space: nowrap;
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
}

.wla__row-meta {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 0.78rem;
  color: $muted;
  white-space: nowrap;
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
}

.wla__row-chain {
  margin-top: 3px;
}

.wla__badge {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 4px;
  padding: 2px 7px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  font-size: 0.7rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  white-space: nowrap;

  &--lg {
    padding: 4px 11px;
    font-size: 0.8rem;
  }
}

.wla__badge-dot {
  width: 4px;
  height: 4px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

/* ── Detail column ────────────────────────────────────────────── */
.wla__detail {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  align-self: stretch;
  max-height: 100%;
  overflow-y: auto;
  overscroll-behavior-y: contain;

  /* Scrolls, but stays visually quiet — the cards inside are the only chrome on screen; a
   * visible track/thumb here would just be more noise. */
  scrollbar-width: none;
  -ms-overflow-style: none;

  &::-webkit-scrollbar {
    display: none;
  }
}

/* Deliberately no background/border/shadow here — just a padding wrapper. The neutral page
 * background (`:host`) shows through behind the cards themselves, which is the only "card"
 * layer left; wrapping the whole column in its own card too would nest cards inside a card
 * and leave a visible blank box below short content (e.g. a request with no attachments). */
.wla__detail-panel {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.9rem;
  padding: 0.25rem;
}

.wla__detail-header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 1rem;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-lg;
  padding: 0.9rem 1.1rem;
  @include tokens.glass-panel;
}

.wla__eyebrow {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 0.76rem;
  font-weight: 650;
  color: $accent;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
  font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

.wla__detail-title {
  margin: 0.15rem 0 0;
  font-size: 1.32rem;
  font-weight: 650;
  color: $navy;
  letter-spacing: -0.018em;
}

.wla__detail-subtitle {
  margin: 0.2rem 0 0;
  font-size: 0.84rem;
  color: $muted;
}

/* ── Body: card column + approval sidebar ────────────────────────
 * `align-items: start` — not `stretch` — so the sidebar's height follows its own content
 * (a 3-step vertical chain) instead of stretching to match a taller Details card, which
 * would otherwise leave visible blank space inside the sidebar card.
 */
.wla__body {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) 220px;
  align-items: start;
  gap: 1.25rem;
}

.wla__main {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.9rem;
  min-width: 0;
}

.wla__sidebar {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.85rem;
}

/* `--chain-color` is set once on `.wla__detail-panel` (see the template) and inherits down
 * through every card here — see the identical pattern + full rationale in
 * wwell-ir-detail.component.scss's `.ird__card`. */
.wla__card {
  border: 1.5px solid var(--chain-color, $border);
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-lg;
  padding: 0.95rem 1.1rem;
  @include tokens.glass-panel;
  transition:
    box-shadow tokens.$duration-fast ease,
    transform tokens.$duration-fast ease;

  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference) {
    &:hover {
      transform: translateY(-2px);
      box-shadow: var(--wp-panel-shadow);
    }
  }
}

/* Approval card in the sidebar reads as a compact rail, not a bento card. */
.wla__card--sidebar {
  padding: 0.85rem 0.9rem;
}

.wla__card-title {
  margin: 0 0 0.8rem;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  font-size: 0.76rem;
  font-weight: 650;
  color: $navy;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
}

.wla__card-icon {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  width: 21px;
  height: 21px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  background: tokens.alpha($accent, 10%);
  color: $accent;
  flex-shrink: 0;

  &.mat-icon {
    width: 21px;
    height: 21px;
    font-size: 13px;
  }
}

/* ── Key/value grid ───────────────────────────────────────────── */
.wla__kv-grid {
  margin: 0;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
  gap: 0.5rem 2rem;
}

.wla__kv {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: baseline;
  gap: 0.75rem;
  font-size: 0.89rem;
  padding: 0.3rem 0;
  border-bottom: 1px dashed $border;

  dt {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.4rem;
    color: $muted;
  }

  dd {
    margin: 0;
    color: $navy;
    font-weight: 600;
    text-align: right;
  }
}

.wla__kv-icon {
  color: tokens.$subtle;
  flex-shrink: 0;

  &.mat-icon {
    width: 14px;
    height: 14px;
    font-size: 14px;
  }
}

.wla__pdf-btn {
  width: 100%;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 6px;
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 0.9rem;

  mat-icon {
    width: 16px;
    height: 16px;
    font-size: 16px;
  }
}

.wla__empty {
  height: 100%;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  color: $muted;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  border: 1px dashed $border;
  border-radius: 12px;
  min-height: 320px;

  mat-icon {
    width: 32px;
    height: 32px;
    font-size: 32px;
    color: var(--wp-line-strong);
  }

  p {
    margin: 0;
    font-size: 0.96rem;
  }
}

/* ── Responsive ───────────────────────────────────────────────── */
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .wla {
    flex-direction: column;
    overflow-y: auto;
  }

  .wla__master {
    width: 100%;
    max-height: none;
  }

  .wla__detail {
    max-height: none;
    overflow-y: visible;
  }

  .wla__body {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .wla__sidebar {
    order: -1;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 640px) {
  .wla {
    padding: 1rem;
  }

  .wla__kv-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
}
```
